This is a task-selected reference, not an always-loaded rail. Root and local
AGENTS.md govern agent workflows. Domain contracts below remain binding for their
scope; historical status is not permission to implement or enable flags.

# Webmail — AGENTS.md (DOX child)

SvelteKit SPA serving the mail client (inbox, compose, calendar, contacts, tasks, quarantine) and the platform admin console. JMAP over HTTP + WebSocket. Deployed as a static build served by the Go gateway.

Parent: `mailserver/AGENTS.md`. Root rail: `/AGENTS.md`.

---

## 1. Purpose

This area is the entire browser-side product: user mail client, PIM (calendar/contacts/tasks), user settings, and admin console — all routes under `mailserver/webmail/src/routes/`. It is a thin presentation layer; all data mutations call JMAP reducers or REST endpoints in the Go gateway. No business logic lives here.

---

## 2. Ownership

**This doc owns:** all SvelteKit routes, lib components, stores, JMAP client, i18n, design tokens, and the UI primitive library (`src/lib/components/ui/`).

**Does not own:** Go gateway handlers, JMAP reducers, PL/pgSQL, auth server endpoints, or the Go middleware that enforces RBAC — those are in `mailserver/AGENTS.md`, `mailserver/sql/AGENTS.md`, and `authserver/AGENTS.md`. Design token sources live in `docs/design/setjoin-design-system/` (working reference only; vendored copies are canonical).

---

## 3. Local Contracts

### Svelte 5 runes (all files)

- Every `.svelte` file opens with `<svelte:options runes={true} />`.
- Reactive state: `$state`, `$derived`, `$derived.by`, `$effect`.
- Event handlers: `onclick=` (not `on:click`). `bind:value` still valid.
- Legacy syntax (`on:click`, `$:`, `<slot />`) must not appear in any file.
- Stores from `.ts` files still use `$storeName` syntax.
- `onMount` / `onDestroy` from `svelte` are still valid.

### TypeScript

- `tsconfig.json` has `"strict": true`. All lib files are `.ts`; all pages use `<script lang="ts">`.
- JMAP domain types: `src/lib/types/jmap.ts`. Add types there before implementing client/store/page.
- Svelte/TypeScript changes require `npm run check` with **0 errors, 0 warnings**; pure documentation moves use content/link/budget checks.

### Accessibility

- Prefer `<button>` over clickable `<div>`. If a `<div>` must be clickable, add `role="button" tabindex="0"` and a keypress handler.
- Never put `onclick` directly on `<li>` — wrap content in a `<div role="button">` inside the `<li>`.
- Every `<label>` needs a `for=` matching an `id` on its control.
- Modal overlays handle `Escape`. Focus trap + return on close. The shared
  `$lib/actions/focusTrap` owns nesting with an active-trap stack: only the
  topmost trap may contain focus or handle Tab/Escape, and destroying it
  returns focus to the trap below (or the original trigger).

### Design system — setjoin flat/indigo

The active design language is the **setjoin design system**: flat, border-first, light-first, single indigo accent `#4A50D0` (`--indigo-600`), IBM Plex Sans/Mono type, Lucide icons at `strokeWidth={1.75}`.

- **Replaced** Liquid Glass (now design history): no backdrop-filter, no aurora wallpaper, no glass surfaces/classes, no Prism-era tokens, no emoji-as-icons. These are enforced as permanent regression guards by `src/lib/theme/tokens.test.ts` plus the M8 audit greps (see §6 Verification).
- Tokens in `src/lib/theme/`: `colors.css`, `typography.css`, `spacing.css`, `base.css`, `fonts.css`, `global.css`. **The design contract that ratifies these decisions is `mailserver/webmail/DESIGN.md`** (flat-over-glass, indigo-only accent, the 4px grid, IBM Plex + Noto Sans SC, the dot+colour+icon status convention, the documented inline-style exceptions, and the token-only contract — now **extended to spacing**). When DESIGN.md and the code disagree, the code is the bug.
- **Contrast / AA:** small must-read text (input hint/error, form labels, captions, timestamps, counts, stat labels) uses **`--text-caption`** (`#696D7E` light / `#888D9E` dark — ≥4.5:1 on page+card in both themes), **not** `--text-tertiary` (`--gray-500`, 3.52:1 on page — below AA, reserved for decorative/large glyphs: carets, separators, watermarks). Small-text floor: error/hint copy + recovery labels render at `--text-sm` (13px)+, not `--text-xs`/`--text-2xs`. Links keep a legible visited state via `--link`/`--link-visited` (wired globally in `base.css`). Touch targets: dense 30px rows lift to ≥44px under `@media (pointer: coarse)` in `global.css` (desktop density untouched). All 8 tag hues define `bg`/`fg`/`dot` in **both** themes (a missing dark dot breaks `Tag.svelte`).
- Compat bridge: **deleted in M8**. All surfaces use canonical setjoin tokens directly; the legacy `compat.css` Liquid-Glass-name mapping no longer exists (`tokens.test.ts` guards its absence).
- Dark mode: `data-theme="dark"` on `<html>` (set by pre-paint inline script in `app.html`). No `prefers-color-scheme` CSS fallback. Drive via `dataset.theme`, not class toggling.
- **CSP (M9):** `svelte.config.js` `csp.mode:'hash'` emits a `<meta>` CSP whose `script-src` has **no `'unsafe-inline'`** — it auto-hashes SvelteKit's inline hydration bootstrap. A `<meta>` CSP only governs content **after** it, and SvelteKit injects it after `app.html`'s pre-paint theme `<script>`, so the meta hash-locks the bootstrap + anything the app injects at runtime (the XSS surface); the pre-paint theme script is **before** the meta and is governed only by the gateway header CSP (`mailserver/internal/middleware/security.go`, which keeps `script-src 'unsafe-inline'` — it can't track the per-build bootstrap hash). Do **not** add custom inline `<script>` after `%sveltekit.head%`: it would be CSP-blocked (no hash). `style-src` keeps `'unsafe-inline'` (inline `style=""` attributes are pervasive; hash mode covers `<style>` elements, not attributes). Guards: `src/lib/theme/csp.test.ts` (script-src never regains `'unsafe-inline'`) + `e2e/csp.spec.ts` (live Chromium: meta hash-locks script-src and the app still hydrates; run `npm run test:e2e:csp` after `npm run build`). **Meet B1 Task 16b delta (locked, triple-homed):** `script-src` additionally carries `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` in all three CSP homes (this meta CSP, the gateway header, Electron's `SESSION_CSP`) — background blur's WASM segmentation model (`@livekit/track-processors`/`@mediapipe/tasks-vision`) fails `WebAssembly.instantiate` under Chromium's CSP without it; the narrower directive permits only wasm compilation, never textual `eval`/`new Function`, so `'unsafe-inline'` stays banned. WASM + the `.tflite` segmentation model are self-hosted (never a CDN URL): `scripts/vendor-blur-assets.mjs` (wired into `predev`/`prebuild`) copies the fileset out of `@mediapipe/tasks-vision`'s own `node_modules` install and downloads the model with a hard-coded SHA-256 (mirrors `lib-livekit.sh`'s checksum pattern) into `static/meet/mediapipe/` — gitignored, regenerated at build time, never committed. **Meet B3 Task 9 (whiteboard): zero CSP delta, verified not assumed.** Excalidraw's canvas rendering needs no WASM/eval and its fonts are self-hosted under the existing `font-src 'self'` — build-artifact static analysis (grepped the built chunks) found no `eval`/`new Function`/`WebAssembly` usage anywhere in the Excalidraw payload. No triple-lock process needed; all three CSP homes are unchanged by this task.
- Density: `data-density="compact"` on `<html>` tightens `--row-height` 36→30px.
- Fonts are self-hosted. IBM Plex Sans/Mono own Latin UI and metadata. CJK uses
  an explicit operating-system stack first, followed by the distinct
  `Setjoin Noto SC Fallback` family. That fallback is generated as physical
  `unicode-range` shards from the pinned sources under `assets/fonts/source/`;
  the deployed `static/fonts/` tree must never contain or reference a monolithic
  Noto SC face. Supported system-font hosts therefore transfer zero bundled CJK
  bytes, while minimal hosts fetch only the ranges they render. No font CDN.
- Icons: `Icon.svelte` wraps `lucide-svelte`; default `strokeWidth={1.75}`. Status conveyed by 6–8px solid dots, not color alone.
- No emoji in product chrome. Sentence case for all UI strings. No exclamation marks.

### UI primitive library (`src/lib/components/ui/`)

28-component library (M1; Slider added in offline #1b; ConfirmDestructive added Chat A4a Task 12). Import from `$lib/components/ui`. Barrel: `src/lib/components/ui/index.ts`.

Token-only styling; scoped `<style>` blocks. Conventions: 32px default control height (26 sm / 40 lg), radii 3/4/6/10px, `--focus-ring` on `:focus-visible`, disabled = opacity 0.5.

Child doc: `src/lib/components/ui/AGENTS.md`.

### JMAP client pattern

When adding a new JMAP domain: type in `$lib/types/jmap.ts` → client helper in `$lib/jmap/client.ts` → store in `$lib/stores/{domain}.ts` → page in `src/routes/{domain}/+page.svelte`.

**Capabilities (c4):** `jmapCall` derives the `using` array from the method names via the ONE capability table in `client.ts` (`CAP_BY_PREFIX` / `usingFor`) — do NOT hand-build `using` arrays per call site (the pre-c4 per-family `*_CAPS` constants are gone; pass an explicit `capabilities` arg only for deliberate overrides). The table mirrors the gateway's method registry and is pinned by a vitest in `client.test.ts` that reads the server's golden artifact (`mailserver/internal/jmap/testdata/methods.golden`), so a server-side capability move fails the webmail suite until `usingFor` is updated in the same commit. Notable: `Identity/*` requires the submission capability; `Continuity/*` requires mail; `QuickStep/*`/`SavedSearch/*`/`EmailTemplate/*` require the vendor URN (`urn:setjoin:params:jmap:mail`); `EmailSubmission/*` also declares mail for `onSuccessUpdateEmail`.

**Per-call accountId override (#13 shared-mailbox use-surface):** `client.ts`'s `accountId` is a module-singleton (the caller's own primary mailbox, set by `loadSession()` from `session.primaryAccounts.mail` — NOT `Object.keys(session.accounts)[0]`, which iterates in ascending-numeric key order and silently picked a delegated mailbox once #13 added one with a smaller id). MAIL read helpers (`mailboxGet`/`emailQuery`/`emailGet`/`threadGet`/`searchSnippetGet`) accept trailing `MailReadOptions { accountId?, errorPolicy? }`; account selection is independent from method-envelope handling (`return-empty` preserves the historical read fallback, `throw` surfaces `accountNotFound`/`forbidden`). The legacy string/null override remains as a compatibility form for existing shared-mailbox callers and preserves their exact behavior during incremental migration. MAIL mutations keep their existing explicit account override and throwing contracts; PIM families (Calendar/Contacts/Tasks) remain personal. Auth teardown invokes `resetJmapAuthContext()` synchronously: it increments a module-local generation before clearing session/account discovery caches, and `loadSession()` may write them only when its captured generation still matches, so delayed prior-user discovery cannot restore stale identity state. `getAccountId()` preserves stale-cache fallback only for refresh failure within the same generation; it rethrows `JmapAuthGenerationError` after teardown so a prior-user continuation cannot borrow the next user's account or issue a follow-on mutation. `getDelegatedAccounts()` is the ONE identity enumeration — every session account other than `primaryAccounts.mail`, reading `accountCapabilities['urn:setjoin:params:jmap:mail'].delegateGrants` (the vendor block the gateway's `Session()` populates, `mailserver/internal/jmap/handler.go`) — consumed by `MailSidebar`'s SHARED MAILBOXES section, `inbox/+page.svelte`'s `accountOverride`/`?mbx=` workspace switch, and `ComposeScreen`'s From Select shared rows.

### e2e suite

Spec-writing contract lives in **`e2e/AGENTS.md`** (tag routing, network-ACK persistence pattern, dedicated fixtures, strict API teardown, toast/hygiene subtleties); `admin-crud.spec.ts` is its reference implementation.

### API paths — everything under `/api`

All app JSON is served under **`/api/…`** (the gateway mounts its API mux there via StripPrefix; registrations in `main.go` keep root-relative literals). The SPA and the API can no longer collide: an old-path GET falls through to the SPA page, an old-path non-GET gets a one-release 308 to `/api/…`. Client rules: direct `fetch`/`httpRequest`/`typedFetch` literals start with `/api` (or a pinned prefix); `adminFetch` prepends `/api` itself (its `API_BASE` seam) so its callers keep `/admin/…` paths. The only root-served surfaces are the pinned prefixes in `scripts/api-pinned-prefixes.txt` (protocol, IdP-registered, links in sent mail, pre-auth bootstrap, ops probes) — `scripts/check-api-namespace.sh` (run-guards manifest) enforces the pinned-file↔`main.go` sync, pinned-vs-SPA-page collisions, and that webmail source never fetches app JSON at a root path. The service worker is deny-by-default: only exact generated public-asset paths are cache-first; `/api/` and every pinned/user-derived request are network-only (`sw-strategy.ts`).

`/guest-invite/accept` is the pre-authenticated browser landing for the pinned
`/auth/guest-invite/accept` API. The root layout must keep `/guest-invite/*`
outside its logged-out redirect. The unauthenticated root container is the
viewport-height vertical scroll owner, so long localized disclosures and wrapped
addresses cannot strand the acceptance controls below an unscrollable app shell.
The page captures the token once, strips it
from browser history/address-bar state before rendering, displays only the
server-authored disclosure (the API localizes its built-in EN/ZH/DE copy from
the browser's `Accept-Language` and names `Content-Language`), and posts the in-memory token with explicit
disclosure acceptance and the chosen password. It never logs, displays,
persists, or synthesizes the raw credential. Successful activation returns the
user to the ordinary login flow, where mandatory TOTP enrollment remains
server-authoritative. Before enabling activation it also reads the public
`/auth/password-policy` contract and mirrors the configured length and character
rules in HTML constraints plus localized inline validation. A terminal token
rejection stays enumeration-safe and offers only sign-in; transient disclosure
or policy failures show a distinct retryable state without exposing the token.
The policy also publishes the shared 72-byte cross-profile password ceiling;
the page counts UTF-8 bytes before submit, and the activation handler rejects an
oversized password with 400 before any invite/account/audit write. Accepted
credentials are hashed through the shared crypto-profile-aware entry point.

OIDC Provider login continuation is the one post-auth protocol hand-off the
root login page accepts. An anonymous `/auth/oidc/op/complete?id=…` request is
redirected to `/?continue=…`; `$lib/auth/oidcContinue.ts` allows only that exact
same-origin route with one non-empty, at-most-256-byte `id`, and the login page
performs a full browser navigation after password, passkey, CAPTCHA, TOTP, or
mandatory-enrollment completion. External URLs, scheme-relative URLs, hashes,
duplicate IDs, extra parameters, unrelated routes, and oversized values are
discarded, so this is not a general return-URL or open-redirect mechanism. The
root layout honors the same parser to avoid racing its default first-app
redirect against the login page.

### i18n

Custom framework at `src/lib/i18n/` (ICU plural/select, `Intl` wrappers). EN +
简体中文 are required; German is best-effort. Catalogs are dynamically imported:
the root `+layout.ts` resolves the persisted/browser locale and awaits exactly
one full active catalog before localized content mounts. `t()` remains
synchronous after that boundary. A live switch loads and validates the target,
then atomically commits catalog plus locale; failure keeps the old catalog,
route, form state, scroll, and focus. Do not restore the root-wide `{#key
$locale}` remount or eager imports of all three catalogs. `index.test.ts`
enforces en↔zh↔de key parity and import ownership. The service worker consumes
only `push.generated.ts`, a tiny generated push dictionary with parity tests;
it must not import browser catalogs or the i18n runtime. Hardcoded English
strings must be converted to keys as each surface is migrated. The
`formatDate`/`formatDateTime`/`formatNumber`/`formatRelativeTime` wrappers resolve
the active locale via `localeCode()` (`en`→`en-US`, `de`→`de-DE`,
`zh`→`zh-CN`); bespoke `Intl` call sites must also use `localeCode()`, never a
hardcoded locale. Reverse-key guards for Meet and Chat remain required.

### App/feature gating

All app visibility and feature-dependent UI derive from the per-user effective-capabilities object in `/jmap/session` (global `feature.*` toggle ∧ per-user entitlement, computed server-side). The SPA reads this once on login. Rail icons, app drawer tiles, and command-palette entries all render from this single source. SPA route guards redirect blocked apps; the real enforcement is the 403 on the data API.

The unified admin feature registry may render `dengbao.sod_mode`, but mutations for
that key always use the dedicated `/api/admin/dengbao/sod-mode` endpoint. Its role
assignment preflight is authoritative; the generic feature endpoint intentionally
rejects the key so the separation-of-duty invariant cannot be bypassed.

### Native bridge (C0)

`src/lib/native/` is the **only** place native-platform APIs are reached. One web build runs identically in a plain browser, the Tauri desktop shell, and a future mobile shell.

- **Rule:** call the facade (`getNative()`, `isNative()`, `hasCapability()` from `$lib/native`) — **never** touch `window.setjoinNative` directly. The facade feature-detects the shell-injected `window.setjoinNative` bridge and delegates to it when present, falling back to web-platform equivalents (`localStorage`-backed `secureStore`, Web Share / clipboard, `Notification`, `<input type=file>`, `window.open`) when absent.
- **Capability-gate** every native-only feature with `hasCapability('push' | 'biometric' | 'vault' | …)` before use — the web fallbacks advertise only the subset the browser can actually do (`secureStore`, `share`, `notify`, `pickFile`, `openExternal`); `push`/`biometric`/`vault` are native-only.
- **Contract stability:** the bridge interface (`SetjoinNativeBridge`) and `BRIDGE_VERSION` live in `src/lib/native/types.ts` — the boundary a shell implements. Any breaking change to the contract **must** increment `BRIDGE_VERSION`.
- **Files:** `types.ts` (contract + `NATIVE_CAPABILITIES` + `BRIDGE_VERSION`; exports `OfflineDbBridge` — the generic per-appKey SQLCipher bridge with typed `query<T>(): Promise<T[]>`; `DbBridge` is a deprecated alias), `fallbacks.ts` (web implementations; `db` is absent — `isOfflineAvailable()` checks `window.setjoinNative?.db`), `index.ts` (facade), `sw-strategy.ts` (pure `chooseStrategy` cache-routing helper). All have colocated `*.test.ts`.
- **Service worker** (`src/service-worker.ts`, SvelteKit-auto-registered in
  production builds): an app-shell cache only with a deny-by-default request
  classifier. Only same-origin members of the generated critical-shell
  manifest may be installed; unknown routes and every JMAP/auth/API/blob/
  download/update/user-derived response are network-only and never written to
  CacheStorage. Candidate install validates the whole bounded manifest before
  promotion. `SETJOIN_ACTIVATE_CANDIDATE` and `SETJOIN_SHELL_MOUNTED` carry an
  exact build ID; the page coordinator activates only at a safe task boundary.
  A candidate may wait indefinitely at that boundary; its durable 15-second
  mount-acknowledgement clock starts only when the worker activates, survives
  worker termination, and fails closed on absent/rolled-back time evidence.
  Candidate plus current/previous acknowledged generations are explicit, and
  one bounded recovery attempt prevents reload loops. Offline user data remains
  owned by the encrypted/native or IndexedDB JMAP offline paths, never the shell
  cache.
- **Connectivity-aware attachment transfer (E3 foundation):**
  `src/lib/offline/attachmentTransfer.ts` is a pure, strict checkpoint/Range
  state machine. It persists no bearer or URL, appends only an exact contiguous
  identity-coded 206 with the strong SHA-256 ETag, discards ambiguous partials,
  preserves verified bytes across 401/403/409 reauthorization, purges an
  authorized 404/410, caps at 8 GiB, and promotes only after exact final size and
  SHA-256. The service worker must never shell-fallback or cache
  `/mail-attachment/*`. E4 platform adapters own network and sink effects.
- **Connectivity E4-I production coordinator (E4-I):** `src/lib/offline/coordinator.ts`
  (`createCoordinator`) owns deduplicated trigger ingestion (foreground /
  reconnect / authenticated push / native opportunity / action), coalesced
  generations, and bounded execution. It calls ONLY the branded bounded steps in
  `src/lib/offline/steps.ts` (`createSyncDeltaStep`/`createSyncWindowStep`,
  `isBoundedSyncStep` refuses unbounded `syncDelta`/`syncWindow` wrappers);
  admission reuses `eligibility.ts`/`scheduler.ts` (E2) unchanged; the default
  policy source is `createOffPolicySource()` — everything stays OFF until the
  plan's rollout step. Capability freshness (E4 R4): observations bind to the
  native lifecycle epoch + monotonic receipt, stale >5s/failed/prior-epoch
  cannot authorize work. `src/lib/offline/migrations.ts` replaces the single
  `MAIL_SCHEMA` block with versioned TS-owned migrations (v1 base, v2
  `optional_jobs`/`attachment_transfers` — content-minimal, no bearer/URL/
  subject/location columns). Authorized paths only: native `migrateStore`
  (bundled manifest) or the one-shot `execSchemaInit` seam — DDL never rides
  `transactionBatch` (frozen N66 contract), an already-current store re-opens
  without consuming the one-shot seam, v2 is detected only from BOTH
  optional tables so an interrupted v2 replay recovers, and a store is never
  reported current while `recovery_artifacts.eligibility` is still missing —
  the interrupted legacy replay is detected and applied on open. `nativeBatch.ts`
  pre-admits the N66 native atomic batch (`transactionBatch`/`migrateStore`
  declared optional on `OfflineDbBridge` in `src/lib/native/types.ts`;
  ordinary entries reject transaction control by parsed keyword, never
  prefix). `jobStore.ts` provides CAS lease/reclaim (backoff jobs come due at
  their retry deadline; terminal jobs never re-queue), lease-fenced
  checkpoint/result commits — `claimLease` returns the winner's stamped
  (owner, expiry) fence and that verbatim fence is the ONLY commit credential
  (an absent fence never means unfenced) — coalescing that never clobbers a
  concurrent lease or drops columns (targeted UPDATE + `ON CONFLICT DO
  NOTHING` — never `INSERT OR REPLACE`; a RUNNING job stays running, a
  non-terminal paused result survives, a TERMINAL result is cleared), newer
  triggers RE-OPEN terminal jobs as fresh runnable work at the new generation,
  and duplicate-effect rejection (durable variant fail-closed without the
  native batch entry).
  `stagedPage.ts` buffers a step's page writes and flushes all-or-nothing
  INSIDE the composed checkpoint commit — page and checkpoint commit as one
  unit, failure exposes neither; the rollback is MERGE-based (it restores
  only the staged fields, preserving any concurrent local edit that landed
  between the page's apply and its rollback) and runs through the store
  seam's ATOMIC per-field conditional restore (`restoreFieldsIfUnchanged` on
  `OfflineStore`: per-field CASE-WHEN-current-equals-expected predicates in
  one UPDATE/batch, dirty-claimed fields never restored) — the old
  snapshot-then-whole-row `upsertMessage` left a read/write TOCTOU that
  clobbered an edit committing between the snapshot and the write. The coordinator executes every
  dispatch under the ONE budget issued at its admission (a narrowed policy
  stops the job; it never renews the budget), charges each committed step's
  actual service back to weighted DRR (`chargeClassService`), stops the
  dispatch at the next loop head once a partial step makes no progress and
  can make no more (byte allowance exhausted or non-fitting — the steps
  issue no network call at all on an exhausted allowance), yields on actual
  request cancellation (DOM `AbortError`) without recording a failure,
  releases undispatched scheduler reservations when Critical interrupts the
  dispatch loop, and `drainForRollback` awaits in-flight admission cycles so
  a pending admission can never claim or write after the drain.
  `transferExecutor.ts` wires E3
  decisions to real effects: memory-only bearer + restart remint under current
  authorization, cancellation armed BEFORE mint and held for the WHOLE cycle
  with re-checks at every await boundary (postflight authorization, sink
  writes, digest verification, promotion), the retained deadline bounding
  every terminal path (fresh expired starts and deadlines crossed during
  fetch/verification/promotion terminate as expired), Critical-transition
  subscription aborting mid-flight I/O, account identity gating every terminal
  path
  (including completed replay and zero-length completion), unproven 404/410
  preserving the resumable checkpoint UNCHANGED (no failed row — recovery can
  remint) while only an authenticated-result-proven 404/410 purges (mint
  facts never authorize), lifecycle-aware restart (complete/failed/expired/
  verifying replay and expired-deadline expiry without network), E3 transport
  classification for fetch exceptions retaining the deadline/attempt
  envelope, destination boundary (`transferDestination.ts` — exact
  `/mail-attachment/{sha256}` from the approved origin; caller URLs validated
  with raw-text ambiguity checks BEFORE URL normalization, including control
  characters that URL parsing strips; redirects rejected, never followed),
  account-change/revocation cleanup. Bounded-step cursors make forward
  progress under byte/time stops: the delta step rides a `delta:page:` cursor
  carrying not-yet-applied ids, the window step advances its position past
  the applied prefix — a fitting prefix is never fetched or charged twice.
  Steps report their applied byte charge (`progress.appliedBytes`); the
  coordinator deducts it per step and hands the next step only the remainder,
  so one dispatch is bounded by its byte allowance in TOTAL, not per partial
  step. The promote→persist window is closed: a revoke observed between the
  awaited sink promote and the completion persist purges instead of
  persisting, and a deadline crossed in the same window expires instead of
  persisting. Durable account cancellation writes the canonical cancelled
  shape (result_state `cancelled`, payload cleared — the reader synthesizes
  the cancelled result from the row's committed checkpoint), so a cancelled
  row with a stale paused payload can never read as no result.
  `criticalScope.ts` gives balanced begin/end Critical handles
  (teardown-fenced, mode-independent, multi-subscriber transitions).
  `statusProjection.ts:schedulerStateFromJobResult` is the single E4→E5 result
  mapping (unknown = idle, never an invented completion). Retained case
  receipts are written ONCE per run in `afterAll` from the auditor verdict
  AND gated on every scenario's actual verdict (`gateVerdictOnScenarios` over
  `getCurrentTest()` results) — a failing scenario can never hide behind a
  PASS receipt. Receipts land where the case validator resolves them
  (`<repo-root>/test-results/connectivity-aware-sync/<run-id>/e4i/`, carrying
  the manifest's repo-root-relative fixture identity), so an honestly
  generated receipt validates unchanged. Test-build-only
  harness + case auditors: `src/lib/native/testing/backgroundWorkHarness.ts`
  (imports from production code are forbidden and guarded). Binding suites:
  `src/lib/offline/e4i.*.test.ts` for `CAS-E4I-TRIGGER-STEP-BOUNDS`,
  `CAS-E4I-PERSISTENCE-RECOVERY`, `CAS-E4I-TRANSFER-LIFECYCLE`,
  `CAS-E4I-CONSUMER-PARITY` (manifest:
  `docs/verification/connectivity-aware-sync-cases.json`; checkpoint:
  `retired-docs:verification/2026-09-17-connectivity-aware-sync-e4i.md`).
- **Connectivity E4-I production boot composition (E4-I, 2026-09-21):**
  `src/lib/offline/boot.ts` is the ONLY production `createCoordinator` caller,
  wired from `routes/+layout.svelte` by one session-scoped `$effect` (cleanup
  disposes on logout/session change). Gating chain, fail-closed: (1) the
  session document's `effectiveCapabilities.offline.syncQoS` block — present
  ⇔ the gateway's `offline_effective_policy` projected BOTH
  `feature.offline_mail` AND `feature.connectivity_aware_sync` ON (the SQL
  strips the block otherwise); absent block, or `mode:'off'` ⇒ INERT (no
  coordinator, no listeners, no window global). (1b) harness-only bridge seam
  `window.setjoinNative.connectivity.rolloutMode` (the E6 pair's per-member
  flag delta, attached by the Electron preload ONLY when
  `SETJOIN_E6_CONNECTIVITY_SYNC_MODE` is set; the production bridge gains no
  field) stands in where the session cannot project — the fixture's
  password-login session carries no JWT device claim, so the device-gated
  block is absent even with the toggles ON; an explicit server-projected
  `'off'` beats the seam. (2) platform capability: `isNative()` AND the
  bridge `db.transactionBatch` — refusal is the honest UNAVAILABLE state
  (exposed as a static gate diagnostic, never a fake runner; this desktop
  build's native crate exports no `transactionBatch` — the E4 native owners'
  gate). When composed, the ports are real: bounded steps over the app's JMAP
  client (`emailChanges` in `jmap/client.ts`), the native store (`init()`
  applies the versioned migrations), durable persistence, online/visibility/
  focus triggers (push/native-opportunity/action expose seams), policy
  re-read fresh per admission, capability observations only via
  `deliverConnectivityCapabilityObservation` (same-epoch, ≤5 s; none delivered
  yet ⇒ admission pauses `capabilityUnavailable` — no manufactured snapshot).
  SATURATION TRUTH: the composition installs
  `window.__setjoinConnectivityQueueState` (ONLY while live) answering the
  content-free queue/running/trigger-counter snapshot; the Electron E6
  saturation endpoint (`clients/desktop/electron/src/connectivity/
  saturation.ts`) reads it through the window bridge and upgrades its body to
  `"basis":"engine-queue-observed"` with REAL queue depth, names the gate
  diagnostic when composition refused, and falls back to
  `policy-mode-configured` otherwise. Tests: `src/lib/offline/boot.test.ts`.
- **Connectivity E5 UI/accessibility (E5):** the consumer-parity seam's UI
  half, entirely behind `feature.connectivity_aware_sync` (default OFF —
  every wired surface renders nothing new when the gate is off, proven by
  `e5.defaultInert.test.ts`). Pure projections (no DOM, no network, no
  invented completions): `e5.uiProjection.ts` — `e5SchedulerFromJobResult`
  (the ONLY scheduler path into the UI, delegating to
  `statusProjection.ts:schedulerStateFromJobResult`), honest freshness keys
  (`neverSynced` = "Not synced on this device", `unknown` = "Sync status
  unavailable", never an empty mailbox/currentness), `e5StatusText` (stable
  i18n keys; every `PauseReason` keeps its own verbatim key;
  `role=alert` reserved for action-required), per-transfer row display with
  the R4 terminals (`download_expired` + newly-authorized restart,
  `content_unavailable` without retry, `verification_failed` without
  Open/Save), save-all group roll-up/retry/cancel semantics, and the
  announcer policy (phase changes + 10% bands, one group announcement per
  settled batch). Controllers: `e5SaveAll.ts` (grouped Save-all with
  independent child identities; cancel preserves completed downloads; retry
  selects only eligible failed children), `e5Settings.ts`
  (requested-vs-effective; pending save refuses duplicate submission; only
  the authoritative response updates effective state; failure preserves
  drafts + remedy; account/device change invalidates in-flight responses;
  `explainCrossedAdmission` scopes refresh vs attachment admission without an
  "all network disabled" promise), `e5AdminPreview.ts` (target+generation
  bound preview; stale responses refused; failed refresh labels retained
  facts stale; disabled winning policies stay visible). Runtime singleton
  `e5ui.svelte.ts` mirrors the coordinator policy source
  (`configureE5FromPolicySource` over `createOffPolicySource()` — OFF by
  construction) and owns transfer rows/save-all groups/the single polite
  announcer channel. Wired surfaces (all gated on `e5ui.active`):
  `OfflineStatus.svelte` (E5 freshness/secondary/action row; single-announcer
  contract per design Pass 5 — the visual container is a NON-live
  `role=group` and ONE offscreen polite `role=status` region owns async
  outcomes for both legacy and E5 events, most-recent-wins; the one action
  button dispatches to the real owning flow: retry/syncNow → requestSync,
  goOnline, unlock, viewSettings → `/settings`, signIn → `/` (the sign-in
  screen), freeStorage → `/settings/storage`, resolve → the sync-issues
  inspector; the not-current primary interpolates `{ago}` from
  `e5ui.lastCurrentAt`, honestly "unknown time" when never recorded),
  `MessageCard.svelte` (durable transfer rows + grouped
  Save-all once the shell proves `durableTransferAvailable`; browser anchors
  unchanged), `/settings` (read-only effective-window summary + storage link
  — `/settings/storage` is the sole window writer in this posture — plus the
  requested-vs-effective block with race-safe save bound to the REAL
  lifecycle identity: `authIdentity()` from `$lib/stores/auth`
  (`user:{id}` from the JWT claims, else the email) + the canonical
  secure-store `device_id` via `$lib/native/deviceId.ts:readOwnDeviceId()`
  (shared with `deviceProof.ts`/`wipe.ts`; `null` in a plain browser — never
  a placeholder); the crossed-settings explanation composes the EFFECTIVE
  `backgroundAllowed` (server authority) and the LIVE `e5ui.networkCost`
  metering snapshot, never the draft switch; must-read policy/pause text
  uses the AA-safe `--text-caption` channel), `OfflinePolicyEditor.svelte`
  → `OfflinePolicyPreview.svelte` (gated server-resolved preview over E1's
  auth-first `/admin/offline/policy-preview`; wire enums render translated
  via `offline.e5.admin.sync_mode.*` / the shared `offline.e5.settings.net.*`
  + `att.*` keys). i18n keys live under
  `offline.e5.*` (en/de/zh, parity-guarded). Binding suites:
  `src/lib/offline/e5.statusSemantics.test.ts` (`CAS-E5-NEVER-UNKNOWN`),
  `e5.transferTerminals.test.ts` (`CAS-E5-TRANSFER-TERMINALS`),
  `e5.saveAllGroup.test.ts` (`CAS-E5-SAVE-ALL-PARTIAL`,
  `CAS-E5-SAVE-ALL-CANCEL-RETRY`, `CAS-E5-GROUP-ANNOUNCER`),
  `e5.settingsPreferences.test.ts` (`CAS-E5-PREFERENCE-SAVE-FAILURE`,
  `CAS-E5-PREFERENCE-DOUBLE-SUBMIT`, `CAS-E5-PREFERENCE-ACCOUNT-RACE`,
  `CAS-E5-CROSSED-NETWORK-ATTACHMENT`), `e5.adminPreview.test.ts`
  (`CAS-E5-PREVIEW-TARGET-RACE`, `CAS-E5-PREVIEW-UNAVAILABLE`) — same
  receipt discipline as E4-I (auditors + pinned FAIL texts in the harness,
  `afterAll` receipts under `<run-id>/e5/`, manifest
  `introducedBy: "E5"`). One-level-up rendered-UI coverage (no separate
  receipts — the controller suites own them): `e5.statusSemantics.test.ts`
  renders `OfflineStatus` to prove the honest unknown/neverSynced labels and
  the `{ago}`-from-`lastCurrentAt` interpolation survive the DOM path;
  `e5.settingsIdentity.test.ts` renders `/settings` to prove the real
  identity binding (incl. the null-device browser case), the mid-flight
  account-change race through the page, and effective-policy/live-metering
  crossed copy.
- **Web Push (QoL #9).** The SW dispatches mail and chat payloads by `type`; mail supports Archive/Reply, while body-free chat notifications deep-link to the exact channel/sequence. `src/lib/push/notificationCommand.ts` is the worker-safe command owner: it takes injected fetch/client ports, validates same-origin session data plus exact JMAP method/call IDs and `notUpdated`, coalesces duplicate in-process commands, and returns `archived` with **no navigation** only after semantic archive success (already outside Inbox is idempotent success). Failure uses one bounded safe fallback: focus an exact target, else navigate only a client carrying the explicit non-sensitive `idleInbox:true` port attestation, else attempt `openWindow` once. Idle is never guessed from `/inbox`: compose state is not encoded in the URL, so the production `WindowClient` adapter currently supplies no attestation and fails closed to a new window rather than overwrite compose/tasks/chat/settings work. `handleNotificationClick` closes immediately and registers exactly one `waitUntil`. Chat copy comes only from generated `push.generated.ts`; locale bootstrap/live switches post the non-sensitive `SETJOIN_PUSH_LOCALE` signal and a fresh worker defaults safely to English. Subscription remains an explicit Settings action, never a page-load permission prompt; native-shell OS push remains the separate `window.__setjoinPushAction` bridge path. Producer/reducer authority lives in `mailserver/AGENTS.md` and `mailserver/internal/chat/AGENTS.md`.
- **PWA manifest:** `static/manifest.webmanifest` (installable: `standalone`, `start_url:/`, indigo `theme_color #4A50D0`); linked from `app.html` via a non-script `<link rel="manifest">` + `<meta name="theme-color">`. Icons `static/icon-192.png` / `static/icon-512.png` are referenced but **not yet committed** — placeholder follow-up (manifest test does not assert PNG bytes).

### Dependency policy

- For dependency changes, inspect `npm outdated` and `npm audit`; do not repeat them for unrelated phases or prose edits.
- Never `npm audit fix --force` or delete the lockfile to re-resolve everything. Update selected dependencies and their lockfile entries; preserve reproducibility and review the delta.
- Include the corresponding `package-lock.json` delta when dependency changes are committed; commits still require user authorization.

### Commit discipline

- Preserve experimental work with a task-owned snapshot or isolated worktree. Do not stage/commit/push without the user's request.
- Never `git checkout -- .` unless working tree is known disposable.

---

## 4. Features / Capabilities

### App shell (`src/routes/+layout.svelte`)

- **56px app rail**: logomark; app buttons (Mail/Calendar/Contacts/Tasks, 38×38, 18px Lucide icons, `--surface-selected` wash on active); admin icons (RBAC-gated, four consoles); theme toggle; settings gear; avatar/sign-out button.
- **App visibility gating**: rail, mobile drawer, launcher tiles, and command palette all render from the per-user effective-access list in `/jmap/session`. A blocked app does not appear anywhere. Guest identity adds an independent, fail-closed shell projection: even while capabilities are absent, loading, or errored, a guest sees only the current guest-safe app set (Chat and, since G3, entitlement-backed Meet) plus theme/sign-out — never Settings, admin rows, mail/PIM navigation, compose surfaces, reminders, the welcome tour, or member palette actions. The backend remains authoritative; this client projection prevents fallback-capability flashes.
- **52px per-app topbar**: title, search (where relevant), contextual actions.
- **Responsive breakpoints**: `<768` — hamburger drawer (no rail, 44px touch rows, Escape/backdrop close, `role="dialog"`); `768–1023` — rail + two panes, folder via drawer; `≥1024` — full three-pane.
- **Draggable pane resizing** (SplitPane/Resizer): sidebar 200–320px, message list 280–560px, reading pane min 360px; persisted per user in localStorage; double-click resets; keyboard-accessible (`role="separator"`, arrow-key stepping). Used within the inbox three-pane layout (M3) and Chat's `ChatApp` — NOT in the top-level shell (see the removed side-dock note below).
- **Split-view side dock — REMOVED (whole-pipeline review UX #19).** M2 built `SideDock.svelte` as a generic right-hand split-view slot (an outer `SplitPane` wrapping the primary app in pane A and the dock in pane B), intended to later host a second app or the Assist panel. No later task ever wired a producer into its `children` snippet, and no UI anywhere (topbar, rail, command palette) ever called the layout's `openDock()` — it was dead from the day it shipped. Because `SplitPane`'s min widths (`minA=360`/`minB=320`) are satisfied on any normal desktop viewport, this was not merely inert: it rendered as a permanent ~40%-width empty panel (with a live, going-nowhere app-switcher `<select>` in its header) next to the primary app on every desktop load. Deleted entirely — `SideDock.svelte`, `SideDock.test.ts`, the outer `SplitPane` wrap, and the `dockOpen`/`dockApp`/`openDock`/`handleDockCollapse` state in `+layout.svelte`; the primary app now renders directly in `.sj-shell-body`. If a real split-view/multi-app-pane feature is wanted later, design its content producer FIRST (what mounts in pane B, and what UI opens it) — do not resurrect the empty-slot shape.
- **Primary-pane sizing is load-bearing.** `.sj-primary-pane` is the sole flex child of `.sj-shell-body` and must retain `flex: 1; min-width: 0`; without those declarations, percentage-width PIM shells collapse the pane to 0px while leaving their accessibility trees present, so Playwright resolves controls whose clicks actually land on the outer shell. The authenticated-route E2E loop asserts `#main-content` has non-zero width.
- **Container-driven responsive layouts**: each app still responds to its container width (not viewport) via `container-type: inline-size` on its own root, independent of the removed side-dock slot.
- **AppRail** (`src/lib/components/AppRail.svelte`): 56px vertical nav rail (M2). Props: `enabledApps`, `activeApp`, `adminConsoles`, `theme`, `userName`, `userEmail`, `guest?`, `onNavigate`, `onToggleTheme?`, `onSignOut`. Hidden below 768px via CSS. App buttons, admin section, theme toggle, settings, avatar/sign-out. Uses `Avatar` from `$lib/components/ui` and `Icon.svelte`. `AdminConsoleId` type and `RAIL_APPS`/`RAIL_ADMIN` constants imported from `$lib/nav/navConfig`. Guest G3 intersects `enabledApps` with a client-side Chat+Meet allowlist and removes admin/Settings actions; Meet appears only from server-derived effective capability and creation remains hidden unless `guest.allowMeetCreate`. The Meet empty state uses join-only copy when creation is denied, so it never tells a constrained guest to start a meeting it cannot create. Drive stays absent until G4, and an older gateway with missing capabilities cannot expose the member rail through fallback defaults; theme/sign-out remain and `+layout.svelte` retains the matching fail-closed route allowlist.
- **GlobalDrawer** (`src/lib/components/GlobalDrawer.svelte`): mobile hamburger slide-out navigation drawer (M2). Props mirror `AppRail` plus `open: boolean` and `onClose: () => void`. Renders only when `open=true` (conditional mount). Has `role="dialog"` `aria-modal="true"` `tabindex="-1"` panel + backdrop. Full focus trap: `$effect` auto-focuses drawer on open, traps Tab/Shift+Tab within panel, handles Escape → `onClose()`, returns focus to trigger on close. `AdminConsoleId` type and nav constants imported from `$lib/nav/navConfig`. Rows: app list filtered by `enabledApps`; divider; admin rows when `adminConsoles` non-empty; optional theme row; settings; sign-out; user email footer. Guest projection reduces those rows to Chat plus optional theme and sign-out. Each row click calls `onNavigate(path)` then `onClose()`. Width `min(304px, 85vw)`; 44px touch rows; z-index 901 (backdrop 900).
- **AppTopbar** (`src/lib/components/AppTopbar.svelte`): 52px per-app topbar (M2). Props: `title`, `showBurger?` (boolean; renders hamburger `IconButton` when true — used below 1024px), `onBurger?`, `onSearch?` (renders search input with 300ms debounce when provided), `searchPlaceholder?`, `actions?` (Snippet for right-side contextual actions). Height token `--topbar-height` (52px). Search field: max-width 560px, `--surface-sunken` background, focused state via `$state isFocused`. Border-bottom `1px solid var(--border-default)`.
- **CommandPalette** (`src/lib/components/CommandPalette.svelte`): Cmd+K search/command palette. Guest identity ignores caller-supplied member commands as well as capability-derived defaults and exposes only Chat navigation, closing the custom-action bypass during absent/error capability states.
- **WelcomeTour** (`src/lib/components/WelcomeTour.svelte`): four-step first-run non-modal coachmark tour, mounted once in the authenticated member shell (`+layout.svelte`; never for guests). It renders a labelled `role="region"`, connector, and pointer-transparent target ring while the real application remains visible, focusable, and actionable—there is no scrim, cut-out, blocker, shell `inert`, modal role, or focus trap. `src/lib/onboarding/tour.svelte.ts` owns one per-shell `createTourRun()` controller plus the per-email seen flag (`localStorage` `sj-welcome-tour:{email}`); `host.ts` carries inbox readiness; `steps.ts` freezes the visible/enabled `apps`, `compose`, `search`, and optional `organize` anchors; `geometry.ts` owns visual-viewport placement. Auto-start waits for loaded inbox state or a bounded three-second degradation and does not move focus; replay from Settings → Appearance or the command palette may focus controls and restores its trigger. Product surfaces suspend/resume the run explicitly. **Anchoring convention:** keep `[data-tour="apps|compose|search|organize"]` on the visible rail/burger, the single responsive Compose action (desktop sidebar; tablet label; mobile icon), the inbox search wrapper, and the first `MailRow`. Browser evidence is `e2e/welcome-tour.spec.ts` plus the 4-width × 2-locale × 2-theme matrix and 16 canonical pixel baselines in `e2e/welcome-tour.visual.spec.ts`.
- **TermsAcceptance** (`src/lib/components/TermsAcceptance.svelte`, CA-3b): scroll-gated end-user Terms-of-Use acceptance modal. Props `{pending: Tou[], onAllAccepted}`; steps the queue one doc at a time, enables Accept only after scroll-to-bottom (when `require_scroll`), POSTs `/terms/accept` per doc and logs out (`POST /auth/logout`) on decline; a polite live region announces queue advance + final acceptance. The pending list comes from authserver `GET /terms/pending`. **Built + tested (`TermsAcceptance.test.ts`) but not yet mounted in the authenticated shell** — client-side ToU enforcement is not wired; the server-side `require_terms` CA verb is the actual gate.
- **DeviceNotApprovedBanner** (`src/lib/components/DeviceNotApprovedBanner.svelte`, CA-2): thin `role="alert"` warning banner mounted once in the authenticated shell (`+layout.svelte`). Renders only when `lockedDown && !approved` (derives from `$effectiveCaps.deviceLockdown`). Warning-fg/bg palette; Lucide `ShieldAlert`; i18n key `device_lockdown.report_only_banner`. Purely informational — the hard block is the `/blocked` route.
- **RiskRemediation** (`src/lib/components/RiskRemediation.svelte`, CA-3b): full-screen fixed overlay (`z-index 1001`) mounted in the authenticated shell (`+layout.svelte`), activated when the runtime CA middleware returns a `require_risk_remediation` 403 response. Props: `signals[]`, `correlationId`, `onVerified`, `initialState` (test seam). States: `idle → loading → success → error → locked` (423 = too many attempts). POSTs `POST /auth/mfa/reauth`; on success calls `onVerified()` after 1.8 s delay. Trust copy contract: never says "high risk" or "suspicious" — maps `impossible_travel`/`failed_auth_velocity` signals to reassuring copy. "What clears this?" `aria-expanded` disclosure. Correlation-ID chip with clipboard copy. Focus-trapped via `focusTrap` action; `aria-live="polite"` announcer; `prefers-reduced-motion` guards all animations. Built + tested (`RiskRemediation.test.ts`).

---

### Login (`src/routes/+page.svelte`)

- **Two-step card (setjoin design)**: email/password step → TOTP step (centered mono 6-digit input). Built with M1 primitives (`Input`, `Checkbox`, `Button`), setjoin tokens only; no Liquid Glass.
- **Layout**: `sj-login-page` (centered, `surface-page` bg) → `sj-login-col` (360px max) → wordmark SVG (`/brand/logo-wordmark.svg`, 146×34) → `sj-login-card` (card surface, border, radius-lg, shadow-sm) → footer (`sj-login-footer` with hostname + self-hosted copy).
- **Auto-discovery**: on `@` input, hits `/api/discover?domain=` to resolve server URL, login field hint, domain hint — debounced 500ms.
- **Branding**: reads `branding.name` and `branding.logoUrl` from `/jmap/session`; falls back to "SetJoin" + wordmark SVG.
- **WebAuthn/passkey login**: "Use a passkey instead" triggers the real WebAuthn flow via `startWebAuthnLogin()` from `$lib/stores/auth`.
- **TOTP MFA**: second step exchanges MFA-pending token + 6-digit code via `verifyTOTP`.
- **Mandatory first-login TOTP enrollment**: `MFAEnrollmentRequiredError` retains the server's five-minute MFA-pending token. The login card calls `/auth/totp/enroll/setup`, parses the standard `provisioning_uri` into a manual secret without changing the backend wire shape, accepts the first 6-digit code, and calls `/auth/totp/enroll/confirm`; success runs the same `checkAuth` path and enters `/apps`. The pending token never becomes an app session by itself. Possession scheme v7 §1.5.1: for a C1 passkey-holder the bare enroll/setup answers the typed 403 `reauth_required` (no password bootstrap grant exists) — the login card then offers the passkey step (`mfa.enroll_uv_*`), runs `provePossessionWebAuthn('bootstrap.totp.enroll', { mfaToken })` (Mode B admission, finish `{"granted": true}` mints the pending grant), and retries setup with the UNCHANGED token; `confirmTOTPEnrollment` presents the setup-issued `enrollment_id`. An `enrollGeneration` fence retires in-flight bootstrap responses when a new login attempt starts or the back link is used (§3.1 UX fence). `login.possession.test.ts` and `stores/auth.test.ts` pin the UI and client mapping.
- **Force-password-change flow**: detected on `ForcePasswordChangeError`; inline new/confirm password form within same card.
- **Remember device**: M1 `Checkbox` with `rememberDevice` state (default checked); passed to `login(email, password, rememberDevice)` which sends `remember_device` in POST body.
- **Token binding (CA-3a, `feature.token_binding`, default OFF)**: `login()`/`loginWithCaptcha()` (`$lib/stores/auth.ts`) send the SPA's DPoP public JWK as `dpop_jwk` (`$lib/auth/dpop.ts`'s `getOrCreateDpopKey()` — non-extractable ECDSA P-256 via WebCrypto, persisted in IndexedDB so a page reload keeps signing with the SAME key the server bound `cnf.jkt` to). Every MFA-shaped login completion sends the same JWK on ITS OWN request (a separate HTTP call from the password step, so the JWK can't ride server-side context across the handoff): `verifyTOTP`, `verifyPush`'s status poll, and `startWebAuthnLogin`'s finish call all fetch the key (`dpopJwkForLogin()`) and include `dpop_jwk` in their POST body — WebAuthn login is passwordless, so its finish call is the ONLY place it can supply one. `$lib/http.ts`'s `httpRequest` — the one seam every app JSON call routes through — attaches the per-request `DPoP` proof header (`htm`/`htu`/`iat`/`jti`, RFC 9449 minus `ath`) whenever a key is bound; the hot-path check is synchronous (`peekDpopKeySync()`) so the toggle-off / no-key case costs zero extra awaits (mirrors the `hasCapability('deviceProof')` guard in `jmap/client.ts` — an unconditional await here raced deferred-promise fetch mocks in several svelte-component tests). Cross-impl verified: `shared/threat/dpop_golden_vector_test.go` checks a real WebCrypto-produced proof against the Go `VerifyDPoP` path (vector regenerated via `scripts/gen-dpop-golden-vector.mjs`). Not yet mounted: `sessionRevoked`/`idleTimer` (`$lib/ws/sessionRevoked.ts`, `$lib/auth/idleTimer.ts`) — those gate `feature.cae_runtime`, a separate toggle (TODOS.md).
- **Threat-aware errors**: CAPTCHA/tarpit/lockout states surface with fact + next-step copy (`ThreatBlockedError` handling, `loginWithCaptcha`, countdown, tested in `login.test.ts`).
- **Session redirect**: if already logged in (session check at mount), redirects to inbox.
- **i18n**: `t()` for all strings (EN/DE/ZH). New keys: `login.subtitle`, `login.continue`, `login.remember_device`, `login.checking`, `login.passkey_instead`, `login.forgot_password`, `login.footer_self_hosted`, `login.footer_data_local`, `mfa.totp_title`, `mfa.totp_subtitle`, `mfa.totp_placeholder`, `mfa.verifying`, `mfa.sign_in`, `login.threat.*`, `login.captcha_submit` (CAPTCHA "Submit answer" button — distinct from `login.submit`).
- **Password recovery**: `src/routes/recover/+page.svelte` — forgot-password flow.
- **First-boot setup**: `src/routes/setup/+page.svelte` — read-only bootstrap status and local-operator instructions. It never accepts an administrator password, restore credential, setup token, or any other mutation; the server redirects `/` → `/setup` while no users exist, and local `mailctl bootstrap-admin` completes initialization.

---

### Inbox (`src/routes/inbox/+page.svelte`)

Thin coordinator over three extracted presentational components in `src/lib/components/mail/`:

- **`MailSidebar`** — 8 fixed kit views (Inbox/Snoozed/Scheduled/Drafts/Sent/**Archive** (A2)/All Mail/Trash), TAGS header with "+ new tag" inline Input + an 8-swatch hue picker (`TAG_COLORS`), user tag rows with hue dots + depth-indented tree via `buildTagTree`, a **Saved searches** section, and a primary Compose `Button`. **Label management:** each USER tag row has a hover/focus-revealed edit `IconButton` (Pencil) that opens an inline rename/recolor/delete form (name `Input` + the shared `colorSwatches` snippet + Save `Button` + Delete `IconButton`). `onNewTag(name, color)` → Mailbox/set create; `onUpdateTag(id, {name?, color?})` → Mailbox/set update (rename/recolor); `onDeleteTag(id)` → Mailbox/set destroy — all wired in `inbox/+page.svelte` through one shared `mailboxSet(args)` raw-JMAP caller. System tags render no manage affordance (the tree is `buildTagTree(userTags(...))`, role-null only) and the reducer independently refuses system-tag recolor/destroy. The hue is stored as the enum name and rendered via the `--tag-{color}` token; manage labels use `inbox.manage.*` i18n keys. The Archive view lists messages with no `location`-group tag (archived = location tag stripped → All Mail). Count rules (kit §3): Inbox = unread badge, Snoozed = total badge, others none. **Saved-search folders:** the Saved searches section renders only when `savedSearches` is non-empty — a `Search`-icon `SidebarItem` per row with a hover/focus-revealed delete `IconButton`. Props `savedSearches` / `onSelectSaved(s)` / `onDeleteSaved(id)`; rows are named re-runnable queries created from the topbar "Save search" affordance (an `IconButton` → inline name `Input`, shown only when `store.rawSearch` is non-empty). Selecting one calls `store.selectSavedSearch(s)` (sets `view='all'`, clears `tagFilter`, sets `rawSearch=s.query`) then the page's `handleSearch()` re-runs the whole `parseSearch`→`loadEmails` pipeline against the live index. Backed by JMAP `SavedSearch/{list,set,delete}` via `$lib/jmap/savedsearches.ts`; the `InboxStore` owns `savedSearches` + `loadSavedSearches`/`saveSearch(name)`/`deleteSavedSearch(id)`. The raw query string is stored verbatim and re-parsed at click time (never a resolved filter).
- **`MessageList`** — threaded/flat list shell rendering `MailRow` components, thread headers (Lucide `ChevronRight`/`ChevronDown`, NOT emoji), `Spinner` while loading, `EmptyState` when empty ("Nothing here. Inbox zero feels good."), and a load-more `Button` when `hasMore`. Takes a single **`actions: MessageListActions`** facade (candidate 7) grouping the per-row / per-thread callbacks (`onOpen`/`onArchive`/`onStar`/`onTrash`/`onToggleRead`/`onToggleThread`/`onMuteThread`/`onArchiveThread`/`onLoadMore`/`onCancelScheduled`/`onMoveFocus`); the individual callback props remain valid and are the fallback when `actions` is absent (the resolution layer prefers `actions.*`, then the prop, then a no-op for the always-wired ones). **Focused/Other split (V078):** when the page passes `onFocusTab` (plain Inbox view only), a two-tab header (`Tabs` primitive — `Focused` | `Other` with the Other-pile unread badge) renders pinned above the scroll; `focusTab`/`otherUnread`/`otherTagId` drive it. Each `MailRow` gets `isOther` (row's `mailboxIds` carry `otherTagId`) + `onMoveFocus`; the hover cluster shows an `ArrowLeftRight` "Move to Focused/Other" button (label flips on `isOther`).
- **`ReadingPane`** — kit toolbar (back `ArrowLeft` on mobile, Archive, Snooze with `SnoozePicker`, Trash, Mute, **Mark read/unread** `Mail`/`MailOpen` (A2 — rendered when `onToggleRead` given; toggles `$seen`), **Flag for follow-up** `Flag` (A2 — rendered when `onFollowUp` given; opens `FollowUpPicker` in a dropdown anchor, active when `email.flagDueAt` set), **Create task** `SquareCheck` (WAVE-5 — rendered when `onCreateTask` given; the coordinator creates a Task titled with the subject, linked to the message via `linkedMessageId`+`linkedMessageReceivedAt`), **Block sender** `ShieldBan` (A2 — rendered when `onBlockSender` given), **Recall** `Undo2` (A1 — shown only when `currentRole==='sent'` and `onRecall` given; fires the inbox coordinator's `recallEmail()` → `POST /messages/{id}/recall`), **Assist** `Sparkles` (feature.assist-gated Copilot — `$effectiveCaps.assist`; ONE `Menu` merging Summarize + Translate, WAVE-5 #7 — no sibling icons. Both call `assistComplete` and render a PLAIN-TEXT result panel above the subject, never `{@html}` — the LLM output is attacker-influenceable via the quoted body; Translate targets the current UI locale (no picker v1) and adds a "Show original" dismiss that mirrors the summary's `X`), spacer, **Print** `Printer` (WAVE-5 — opens a self-contained print window via `$lib/mail/printMessage`), Star), `clamp()` subject + a follow-up due chip when `email.flagDueAt` is set, breadcrumb tag chips with "+ tag" picker, a **conversation-view card stack** (see `MessageCard` below), a **Quick Steps bar** (`QuickStepsBar`, A2 — rendered above the reply box when `quickSteps` non-empty + `onApplyQuickStep` given), inline reply `Textarea` + Send `Button`. **Conversation-view depth:** the pane renders the whole thread as stacked collapsible `MessageCard`s via the optional **`emails: Email[]`** prop (oldest→newest; falls back to `[email]` for a single message). `email` stays the _context_ message — toolbar, subject, tag chips, follow-up, read-receipt, and the reply box act on it (what the coordinator's mutations target), normally the newest/clicked message; `emails` only drives the card stack. Expanded state (`expandedIds`) is seeded to the newest card and reseeds whenever the newest id changes (thread switch, or the cross-folder thread finishing its fetch), via the same track-last-id `$effect` pattern as the read-receipt prompt. **Read-on-expand (WAVE-5 #6):** that same seed-effect, plus `toggleCard`, fire the optional **`onMarkSeen(email)`** action for an unread card the instant it becomes expanded — the auto-expanded newest on thread-open, or an older card the user manually expands — never for a card that stays collapsed. The inbox page wires `onMarkSeen: (email) => setRead(true, email)`. Reply/Forward act on the context message (per-message reply is out of scope); delivery inspection is per-card. Preserves `ExternalSenderBanner` and (per card) `EmailAnnotations`, vote buttons, and the S/MIME badge. Takes a single **`actions: ReadingPaneActions`** facade (candidate 7) grouping all ~21 mutation/intent callbacks instead of passing each individually; the individual callback props remain valid and are the fallback when `actions` is absent (a `$derived` resolution layer prefers `actions.*`, then the prop, then a no-op for the always-rendered buttons). A button is still rendered only when its (resolved) callback is supplied — `onReplyAll`/`onToggleRead`/`onFollowUp`/`onCreateTask`/`onBlockSender`/`onApplyQuickStep`/`onRecall`/`onNotSpam` stay optional. **Read-receipt prompt (Track A privacy):** when an opened message requested a receipt, an `$effect` runs `resolveReceiptAction($lib/mail/readReceipt, policy, requested)` keyed on `email.id` — `always` auto-fires `onReadReceipt(true)`, `ask` shows a consent banner (Send / Don't send) that calls `onReadReceipt(consent)`, `never` does nothing. Props: `readReceiptPolicy` (default `'ask'`), `onReadReceipt(consent)`. The inbox coordinator owns the policy (fetched once from `/user/notification-preferences`) and POSTs the decision to `/auth/me/read-receipt`; the reducer is the hard server-side gate.
- **Shared-mailbox use-surface (#13, WAVE-5 tail):** workspace = `/inbox?mbx=<delegated mailbox id>`, deep-linkable, back = own mail. `MailSidebar`'s **SHARED MAILBOXES** section (`sharedMailboxes: DelegatedAccount[]`, from `getDelegatedAccounts()`) renders only with ≥1 grant; clicking a row `goto`s the `?mbx=` workspace. **Three redundant context-guard signals** (binding design pin — never fewer): (1) the sidebar's `sj-mail-sidebar__shared-header` block (mailbox name + Shared badge + back-to-my-mail X), (2) the page's persistent `sj-inbox__shared-strip` above the list (name + permission level + a manual Refresh — no push in shared context, v1 pin), (3) the compose From Select preset to the shared address with a caption when From ≠ own. `inbox/+page.svelte`'s `switchContext(mbx)` sets `InboxStore.accountOverride` (threaded as the trailing `acct` arg into every MAIL JMAP call — see client.ts's per-call override above) and collapses the sidebar to the 4 shared views (Inbox/Drafts/Sent/Trash) — personal-only features (Tags/Saved searches/Snoozed/Scheduled/Archive/All Mail/Newsletter bundles/Focused-Other) are hidden, not merely disabled. **DANGER STATES:** a read-only grant (no `send_as`/`send_on_behalf`/`full_access`) disables Compose + Reply/Reply-all/Forward + the inline-reply box with a tooltip (`MailSidebar`/`MessageCard`/`ReadingPane`'s `composeDisabled`/`composeDisabledReason` props) — DISABLED, never hidden; a denied call in shared context (`InboxStore.accessRevoked`, set by `loadEmails`/`refreshMailboxes`/`EmailActionStore.dispatch`'s catch) replaces the whole sidebar+list+reading body with a full-pane "access removed" `EmptyState` (never a toast-loop retry) — the only way back is `backToMyMail()` (`goto('/inbox')`). Sent-as-shared lands in the SHARED mailbox's own Sent (sending-mailbox semantics, zero new code); the From Select's send-as vs. on-behalf-of rows and the `send_on_behalf` keeps-`From=own` pin are `ComposeScreen`'s job (see Compose section) — `send.sql` is the hard enforcement (BZ003 on a mismatch), the UI pin only avoids the client ever sending a request the reducer would reject. Backend mechanism (delegate arm on `identity.ValidateAccountID`, the c4 method gate class, the `blobDownloadAuthorized` Download arm): `mailserver/internal/AGENTS.md`. e2e: `e2e/shared-mailbox.spec.ts`.

**Mail components:**

- `src/lib/components/mail/MessageCard.svelte` — one message of a conversation, rendered as a collapsible card (Gmail/Outlook stacked-thread view). Collapsed = a one-line summary (avatar, sender, preview, date); expanded = the full sender block (Reply/Reply all/Forward + a "More actions" → Inspect-delivery menu), `EmailAnnotations`, vote buttons, body, and attachment chips — the same `sj-reading-body__*` markup/styles the single-message pane used, so selectors are preserved. **Owns its own body render:** per-message HTML sanitization (`renderEmailBodyHTML` — the shared cid-rewrite+`sanitizeHTML` helper, computed only when expanded), the remote-image (tracking-pixel) block state (`showRemoteImages` + `hasRemoteImages` + a per-sender `senderTrusted` override from `imageTrust.svelte.ts`), the `Show HTML`/plain-text toggle, `isMonoBody`, the can't-decrypt panel, and its own per-card `DeliveryPanel`. **GATING NOTE:** `htmlSplit`/`displayHTML` (below) and the remote-image banner are all keyed behind `email.bodyHTML` (or `htmlMode`) truthy before touching `sanitizedHTML` — reading that `$derived.by` unconditionally forces a `renderEmailBodyHTML` call even for a plain-text-only message (a real regression the WAVE-5 #6 build caught: it broke `inbox.test.ts`'s `$lib/sanitize` mock, which only stubs `sanitizeHTML`). **Remote-image per-sender trust (WAVE-5 #5):** the blocked-images banner (shown only in `htmlMode`) gains a second button, "Always load images from this sender" (`t('inbox.images_always')`) — `alwaysLoadFromSender()` POSTs the exact sender address to `imageTrust.add()` (V091 `user_image_allow`, EXACT-ADDRESS grain — never domain, ponytail's V1 domain-grain proposal was overridden by the WAVE-5 tail-program eng review) with an act-then-undo toast; the durable undo surface is `/settings/privacy`'s "Trusted senders" list. Allowlist membership only ungates the remote-image FETCH — `sanitizeHTML` always still runs. **Quoted-text collapse (WAVE-5 #6):** `$lib/mail/quoteSplit.ts`'s `splitQuotedHTML`/`splitQuotedText` split the (already-sanitized, post-cid-rewrite) body at the first top-level `<blockquote>`/`.gmail_quote`/`div[type=cite]`/bare "On … wrote:" boundary (html) or the trailing `> `-quote run (plain text); a REAL `<button>` pill ("Show/Hide quoted text") sits in the PARENT DOM below the `sandbox=""` iframe — the iframe never gets `allow-scripts`, so an in-body toggle button is not an option. Whole-body-quote and no-boundary both skip the pill. **Attachment preview (WAVE-5):** previewable chips (`isPreviewable` = `image/*` + `application/pdf`) render a `<button>` that opens a wide `Dialog` — image via `<img>`, PDF via a same-origin `<iframe sandbox="">` — both pointed at the inline URL (the chip URL WITHOUT the `?type=octet-stream` download override), with a plain Download link in the footer; non-previewable types stay the download `<a>`. **Sender people-pane (WAVE-5):** the sender avatar is split out of the collapse-toggle into its own `SenderCard` trigger (siblings, never nested). Props: `email`, `expanded`, `onToggle`, `accountId`, `onReply`/`onReplyAll?`/`onForward`/`onResend?`/`onVoteResponse?` (`onResend` is relayed to this card's `DeliveryPanel` — it renders the Resend button only when a recipient bounced). **Vote buttons wired:** the `X-Mailserver-Voting` options render as `sj-reading-body__vote-btn` buttons; clicking fires `onVoteResponse(option)`, threaded `MessageCard → ReadingPaneActions → inbox page` where `castVote()` opens a reply to the sender with `composeStore.opts.voteResponse=option` (the send reducer emits the CR/LF-sanitized `X-Mailserver-Vote-Response` header). A vote is just a reply carrying that header — no tally/results side (deferred backend). Stacked by `ReadingPane` from its `emails` prop.
- `src/lib/components/mail/SenderCard.svelte` (WAVE-5) — sender people-pane popover: an avatar trigger (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) + an absolutely-positioned card with click-outside (ref-containment via `svelte:window`, not a popover lib). On open, looks the sender up: `contactQuery({text: email})` → `contactGet`, **exact-matches the lowercased email client-side** (contactQuery is substring ILIKE — exact-match avoids false dedup), else falls back to `galSearch(email)`. Renders `Avatar` + name + email + company/title, plus an **Add to contacts** button (`createContact({displayName, emails:[{type:'other', address}]})`) shown only when not already a personal contact. Read-only beyond the add. Tests: `SenderCard.test.ts`.

**A2 triage components:**

- `src/lib/components/FollowUpPicker.svelte` — due-date preset popover (Today 17:00 / Tomorrow 09:00 / This week = coming Friday 17:00 / Custom via `DateTimePicker`) + a "Remind me" `Switch` (default on; reminder fires at the due instant). Emits `onSelect({dueAt: Date, reminderAt: Date|null})`. Mounted in `ReadingPane`'s follow-up dropdown anchor. The reminder _firing_ is A6's `scan_due_reminders()`/`remind` handler — A2 only stores `messages.flag_due_at`/`flag_reminder_at`.
- `src/lib/components/QuickStepsBar.svelte` — renders the user's Quick Steps as a row of buttons; clicking emits `onApply(id)`; `applying` id disables its button. Mounted in `ReadingPane` above the reply box.
- `src/lib/components/mail/delivery/` — the **Delivery-path inspector** (bidirectional), opened from the `ReadingPane` overflow menu on any message. `DeliveryPanel.svelte` is a right-overlay dialog (verdict digest strip + Flow/Auth/Headers/Raw tabs, focus-trap) that branches on `trace.direction`: **inbound** → `InboundTimeline.svelte` (the `Received:`-chain hop stepper with per-hop latency bars + a "Setjoin received here" trust divider); **outbound** (Sent) → `OutboundTimeline.svelte` (a per-recipient delivery-status `<ol>` — `dot+colour+icon+WORD` status chips delivered=success/bounced=danger/deferred=warning/queued=info (per-recipient `sending` was dropped — it survives only on the overall digest), mono MX + SMTP code, expand-to-attempt-history, **auto-expands the first bounce**, problems-first order from the server, never `{@html}`) with a verdict-as-hero digest ("N of M delivered" + danger/warning pills), a Refresh control, and a retention-skew caption. **Resend after NDR:** when `onResend` is supplied AND `outboundVerdict.bounced > 0`, the outbound digest shows a **Resend** `Button` (`triage.resend`) beside Refresh; it fires the coordinator's `resendEmail()` → `composeStore.open(resendPrefill(selectedEmail))` — a **brand-new** compose seeded with the original recipients/subject (no Re:/Fwd:)/body, carrying **no id** (never an un-bounce of the immutable BZ012 queue row / its Message-ID). `resendPrefill(email)` is a pure helper in `inboxModel.ts` (webmail-only; the pipeline is unchanged). `onResend` threads page → `ReadingPaneActions` → `MessageCard` → `DeliveryPanel`. Original attachments are not re-attached (forward skips these too — out of scope). Fed by `GET /messages/{id}/trace` via `$lib/api/delivery`; types in `$lib/types/delivery.ts`. Honest claim: outbound says "Delivered to {MX}", never "read". Tests in `*.test.ts` alongside.

**Supporting modules:**

- `src/lib/stores/inboxStore.svelte.ts` — `InboxStore` ($state rune class): the inbox's reactive state seam. Owns the ~24 inbox vars (`mailboxes`/`emails`/`threads`/`selectedEmail`/`threadEmails`/`view`/`tagFilter`/`rawSearch`/`viewMode`/`loading`/`loadingMore`/`error`/`currentPosition`/`totalEmails`/`hasMore`/`online`/`quarantineCount`/`mutedTagId`/`focusTab`/`mutating`/`scheduledMap`/`bundles`/`readReceiptPolicy`/`quickSteps`/`applyingQuickStep`/`savedSearches`/`sortProperty`/`sortAsc`), the list-sort control (`sortProperty`/`sortAsc` feed `loadEmails`'s `Email/query` sort — backend whitelist `receivedAt`/`sentAt`/`size`/`subject`/`from`, asc/desc + pinned-first applied server-side; `setSort(property, ascending)` persists to `localStorage` (`sj-inbox-sort`, validated on read) then reloads; the inbox topbar renders a `Select` + direction-toggle `IconButton` wired to it; group-by-date/sender DEFERRED), the saved-search folder actions (`loadSavedSearches`/`selectSavedSearch(s)`/`saveSearch(name)`/`deleteSavedSearch(id)` over `$lib/jmap/savedsearches.ts`), the `$derived` projections (`counts`/`selectedRole`/`flatForView`/`effectiveViewMode`/`threadList`/`otherTagId`/`otherUnread`), the Focused/Other split (V078): `focusTab` (`'focused'|'other'`, default `focused`) + `setFocusTab(tab)` (sets it, clears the selection, reloads); `otherTagId`/`otherUnread` derived from the `Other` system tag (by name, like the page derives `mutedTagId`) drive the tab badge; `loadEmails` passes `focusTab` into `computeListFilter` (applied only on the plain Inbox view), the list/view coordination (`loadEmails` — standard `Email/query` + the Scheduled `EmailSubmission` path; `selectView`/`selectTag`; `buildThreads`; **`loadThread(email)`** — conversation-view depth: fetches the full cross-folder thread (`Thread/get` + `Email/get` with `FULL_EMAIL_PROPS`), sorts oldest→newest into `threadEmails` for `ReadingPane`'s stacked `MessageCard`s (NOT reused from `threads`, which only holds the current page/view — missing older + Sent); race-guarded on the selected thread id, falls back to the single message on error; the page calls it from `openEmail` + deep-link restore, and clears `threadEmails` on close; `loadBundles`; `refreshMailboxes`; on the online path, when the parsed filter carries a `text` query it also calls `searchSnippetGet(filter, ids)` and attaches `searchSubject`/`searchPreview` (BM25 `<mark>` highlights) per email — best-effort, skipped offline / for non-text views; `MailRow` sanitizes then renders them), and the Email/Mailbox WebSocket subscription via `subscribeToChanges()` — registers an `['Email','Mailbox']` handler with a 300ms debounce through its own `PushHandlerStore` (not the hub directly); **reconnect resync (#222, #225):** the store keeps two anchors for the primary account, taken ONLY from a reset (first-page / full) load: the Email state from the list's own `Email/get` (`emailGetWithState`) and the Mailbox state (`mailboxState()`, read before the list so it can only lag it). A load-more page never advances the Email anchor (earlier pages were read at the older state), and an empty result or a Scheduled/offline/shared view clears both to unknown. After a socket reconnect it calls `Email/changes` from the Email anchor and re-reads the Mailbox state, reloading if either moved (or the Mailbox anchor is unknown), falling back to a reload on `cannotCalculateChanges` (typed `isJmapMethodError` guard) or no Email anchor; a push is skipped only when EVERY inbox type it names (`Email`, `Mailbox`) equals a known anchor (no double fetch after a resync; a Mailbox-only change still reloads), and a resync superseded by a newer load does nothing; shared context stays manual-refresh-only; the page wires `onAfterPushReload` for deep-link restore — + `teardown()` (onDestroy, delegates to the `PushHandlerStore`). `loadEmails` rethrows non-abort failures so the page owns the user-facing error message. Pure helpers stay in `inboxModel.ts`. **Offline (Task 19):** standard email/query path checks `offline.effectiveOffline` — when true and view=inbox/no-tag-filter, routes through `loadList`/`searchList` from `$lib/offline/readpath`and maps`ListRow[]`to minimal`Email[]`; online path unchanged; Scheduled/Snoozed remain online-only.
- `src/lib/stores/pushHandler.svelte.ts` — `PushHandlerStore` ($state rune class, candidate 8): the single **WebSocket→action seam**. Owns ALL hub subscriptions + one optional per-registration debounce, exposing `register(dataTypes, handler, { debounceMs?, onReconnect? }) → unsubscribe` (options type `PushRegisterOptions` extends the hub's `SubscribeOptions`, #225) over the single `$lib/jmap/websocket`hub.`debounceMs`omitted/0 fires the handler synchronously on each matching`StateChange`(the immediate-refresh behavior of the PIM apps, whose own loading guards coalesce);`debounceMs > 0`coalesces a burst into one call per window (the inbox's 300ms). `onReconnect` (#222) shares that window: a reconnect and a push in one window collapse into ONE call — the push handler wins, the hook runs only when no push arrived. Every dispatch (debounced and immediate) is wrapped in try/catch so a throwing handler never crashes the timer flush or starves a co-registered handler.`teardown()`tears down every registration + cancels every pending debounce. The InboxStore, each PIM app (TasksApp/CalendarApp/ContactsApp), and RemindersWindow each own an instance and tear it down on destroy; a shared process-wide`pushHandlers`instance is also exported. Replaces the per-consumer`subscribe()`/`wsUnsubscribe`/`wsDebounce`boilerplate. Tested in`pushHandler.test.ts` (registration · debounce-batching · unsubscribe · error-isolation).
- `src/lib/stores/inboxModel.ts` — pure filter/semantics helpers consumed by `InboxStore`. `computeListFilter({view, tagFilter, mailboxes, searchFilter, focusTab?})` implements kit §4 precedence: search `tag:` operator wins over sidebar tag filter, which wins over view (except Trash); All Mail = no `inMailbox` restriction. The `archive` view (A2) sets `inArchive:true` (no `inMailbox`) — the server filters to messages with no `location`-group tag. **Focused/Other split (V078):** the optional `focusTab` adds `filter.focus` (`'focused'` hides the Other pile, `'other'` shows only it) **only** on the plain Inbox view — `view==='inbox'` with no `tagFilter`, no search `tag:` (`searchFilter.inMailbox`), and no search operator; a search/tag/archive/other view is left un-split (so `Other` mail stays findable). Omitting `focusTab` adds nothing (keeps the plain-view tests unchanged). `computeCounts`' `SYSTEM_NAMES` excludes `Other` (surfaced via the list tabs, never as a sidebar count) alongside `Snoozed`/`Scheduled`/`Muted`. `resolveViewMailbox(view, mailboxes)` maps the 8 kit views to mailbox roles/names (`archive`→null, like `all`). `replyAllRecipients(email, selfEmail)` (A2) computes Reply-All recipients: original sender → To; all other to+cc → Cc; self removed (case-insensitive, deduped). `selectionFromUrl(threadList, threadId)` returns the first email of the deep-linked thread. `computeCounts(mailboxes)` derives the sidebar badges; `isMonoBody(email)` flags machine-generated mail.
- `src/lib/stores/calendarActionStore.svelte.ts` — `CalendarActionStore` ($state rune class): the calendar's mutation seam (c12), mirroring EmailActionStore's shape. Typed `CalendarAction` union (`create`/`update`/`delete`/`rsvp`/`dragCommit`/`applyScope`/`cancelScope`) behind one `dispatch(action)`; rollback-on-failure only — deliberately NO undo surface. `dragCommit` on a recurring event stashes patch+snapshot and returns `{scopeRequired:true}` (the view opens the scope dialog); `delete` on a recurring event (the `delete` action carries the full event, not just an id — mirroring `dragCommit` — so the store can inspect `recurrenceRule`) likewise stashes and asks scope, an override row (no `recurrenceRule`) deletes directly. `ScopePending.intent` (`'edit' | 'delete'`) drives `applyScope`'s branch: edit-intent resolves `dragPatch ?? eventData()` as before; delete-intent calls `updateOccurrence(..., {excluded:true})` for `this`/`following` or `deleteEvent` for `series` (`retired-docs:superpowers/plans/archive/2026-07-08-recurring-event-occurrence-edit.md`). `applyScope` takes `eventData` as a THUNK (`dragPatch ?? eventData()` — lazy because the drag path's form may never open). Preserves the three dragSnapshot rollback paths, the pre-drag recurrence-id keying (`snap.start`, never the moved instant), and consumes stores/calendar.ts's throwing `assertSetApplied`. Host seam: `CalendarActionHost` (`reload`/`patchEventTimes`). Tested in `calendarActionStore.svelte.test.ts` (all rollback paths + the named pre-drag test + the delete-scope/intent branches). `CalendarApp.svelte`'s scope `<Dialog>` is intent-aware: `calendar.scope.deleteTitle`/`deletePrompt` (en/de/zh) + `danger`-variant buttons when `scopeIntent === 'delete'`.
- `src/lib/stores/calendarEvents.svelte.ts` — `CalendarEventsStore` rune class (module singleton `calendarEvents`) replacing the old `events` writable bag: `load(start,end)` with a monotonic load-token guard (stale responses dropped; `reset()` invalidates in-flight), `reload()`, `patchEventTimes`, `mergeShared`. Tested in `calendarEvents.svelte.test.ts`.
