- `src/lib/stores/emailActionStore.svelte.ts` — `EmailActionStore` ($state rune class): the inbox's **mutation/intent seam** (candidate 7). Owns the ~14 reading-pane / message-list mutations as a typed **`EmailAction`** union (`archive`/`trash`/`snooze`/`mute`/`focus`/`star`/`toggleTag`/`setRead`/`followUp`/`blockSender`/`notSpam`/`applyQuickStep`/`recall`/`sendReply`/`cancelScheduled`) behind one async **`dispatch(action) → DispatchResult`**. `focus` (Focused/Other split, V078) optimistically toggles the `Other` tag on the row's `mailboxIds` — via `host.otherTagId` — then POSTs `setMessageFocus(id, focused)` (`POST /messages/{id}/focus`, which also learns a per-sender override) and reloads, reverting the optimistic patch on failure. Undoable mutations (archive/trash/snooze/mute/toggleTag-removal/blockSender) return a **`Snapshot`** (the inverse `mailboxIds` patch, or a per-email `threadPatch` for mute); `recall` returns `{recall}`, `sendReply` returns `{reply}`, the rest return `null`. The page's `undo(snapshot)` replays the inverse `emailSet`. State lives in the `InboxStore`, reached through the small **`ActionHost`** interface (so the two stores stay decoupled and the action store is unit-testable against a plain host object). Optimistic mutations (`star`/`setRead`/`toggleTag`) roll back and **rethrow** on failure so the page owns the error banner. The page builds two `$derived` facades (`readingActions`/`messageListActions`) from these dispatch calls + its toast/undo glue and passes each as a single `actions`prop. Tested in`emailActionStore.svelte.test.ts`.
- `src/lib/stores/searchQuery.ts` — `parseSearch(raw, mailboxes)` parses Gmail/Outlook-style operators into JMAP `Email/query` filter conditions (all backend-supported by `jmap_email_query`; no reducer change): `tag:`/`in:`/`label:<name>`→`inMailbox`, `from:`/`to:`/`cc:`/`bcc:`/`subject:<v>` substring, `has:attachment`, `is:unread|read|starred|flagged|draft|answered`→`hasKeyword`, `before:`/`after:`/`older(_than):`/`newer(_than):<date|Nd>`→receivedAt bounds, `larger:`/`smaller:`/`size:<5M>`→`minSize`/`maxSize` (1024-based bytes), and bare words + `"quoted phrase"`→`text` FTS. Last duplicate wins; unknown `key:value` kept as text. `computeListFilter` (inboxModel.ts) forwards every present key. Follow-ups: negation `-op`, `OR`/boolean, autocomplete chips, offline-search-path operators.
- `src/lib/stores/tagHelpers.ts` — `LOCATION_ROLES`, `stripLocationTags`, `userTags`, `buildTagTree`, `snoozePresets`.

**Architecture:** two stores, instantiated once in `+page.svelte`. **`InboxStore`** owns the inbox's reactive state, the `$derived` projections, and the list/view/push coordination (its push half registers through a `PushHandlerStore` — the single WS→action seam shared by the PIM apps and RemindersWindow). **`EmailActionStore`** (constructed with the `InboxStore` as its `ActionHost`) owns the ~13 reading-pane / message-list JMAP **mutations** behind a typed `dispatch()→Snapshot` seam. The page is a thin coordinator: it instantiates both stores, binds the template to `store.*`, and keeps only the **toast/undo copy** (thin wrappers that call `actionStore.dispatch` then `showToast` with an `onAction` that replays `actionStore.undo(snapshot)`), the **compose hand-offs** (reply/replyAll/forward/scheduleReply → `composeStore`), the read-receipt/quarantine/quick-steps loads, the deep-link/keyboard glue, and the two `$derived` action facades (`readingActions`/`messageListActions`) it hands to the children. The thread-level mute/archive (`muteThread`/`archiveThread`) stay page-local (they fan a single intent across a `store.threads` bucket). The three child components (`MailSidebar`/`MessageList`/`ReadingPane`) stay presentational, receiving data + an `actions` facade via props and emitting intent.

**Filter precedence** (kit §4, implemented in `computeListFilter`):

1. Search `tag:` operator always wins.
2. Sidebar tag filter wins over the view, but never overrides Trash.
3. Otherwise, resolve the view to a mailbox (`all` → no `inMailbox`; `snoozed`/`scheduled` → name lookup; others → role lookup).
4. Bare text and `from:` fragments are AND-merged.

**A2 triage actions** (`inbox/+page.svelte`): `replyAllTo()` (Reply All — opens compose with To/Cc from `replyAllRecipients`, `a` shortcut), `setRead(read, email?)` (Mark read/unread — optimistic `$seen` patch with rollback, refreshes mailbox counts; `i`/`u` shortcuts; wired to `ReadingPane.onToggleRead` + each `MailRow` hover-cluster toggle threaded through `MessageList.onToggleRead`), `setFollowUp(sel)` (writes `flagDueAt`/`flagReminderAt` + `$flagged` via `Email/set` — A6 fires the reminder), `applyQuickStepTo(id)` (`QuickStep/apply` → reload + plain confirmation toast, no undo), `blockSender(email?)` (moves to Spam with Undo — a single triage action, not a rule; hidden in the Spam/Junk view). Quick Steps are loaded once on mount via `listQuickSteps()`.

**Act-then-undo toasts** (kit §8): Archive → "Conversation archived", Trash → "Conversation moved to Trash", Snooze → "Snoozed until …", Remove tag → "Tag removed", Mute → "Conversation muted — replies skip your inbox", Block sender → "Sender blocked" (A2). The page calls `actionStore.dispatch(...)`, captures the returned `Snapshot`, and wires the toast's `Undo` to `actionStore.undo(snapshot)` — which replays the inverse `mailboxIds`/`keywords` `emailSet`. Archive's destination = `stripLocationTags` (owned by the store). Renders `<Toast />` once in the page via M1 `showToast` service. The **inline reply Send** is an in-flight act-then-cancel: `dispatch({type:'sendReply'})` builds a reply draft (`emailSet`) + `emailSubmissionSet` with `onSendUndoWindowSeconds: 5` and returns the submission id; the page shows "Sending in 5s…" with a **Cancel** action wired to `actionStore.cancelReply(id)` (`emailSubmissionCancel`) — not a compose hand-off. The **Schedule send** button in the reply footer opens the full composer prefilled as a reply (the Delivery section hosts the schedule picker).

**Deep links (S13):** Selecting a thread calls `goto(\`/inbox?t=${threadId}\`)`; `$page.url.searchParams.get('t')`is read on mount and after WS reloads to restore selection via`selectionFromUrl`. Closing the reading pane (`onBack`) navigates to `/inbox`(clears`t`).

**Newsletter bundles:** `NewsletterBundle.svelte` (grouped-row treatment by `List-Id`); wired to server-side B5 `Email/bundles` backend method (backend-wave2). Per-user opt-out via settings.

**Keyboard shortcuts** (`src/lib/hooks/useKeyboard.svelte.ts`):
`c` compose · `r` reply · `a` reply-all · `f` forward · `e` archive · `#` delete · `s` star · `z` snooze/undo · `i` mark read · `u` mark unread · `j`/`k` next/prev · `/` search · `Ctrl+K` command palette.

---

### Compose (`src/lib/components/ComposeScreen.svelte`)

Full-screen overlay (absolute, inset:0, z-index:100) inside the mail app's `position: relative` root. Opened by `composeStore` (`src/lib/stores/compose.ts`) on `c` shortcut or compose button; closes on send, discard, or minimize.

**Multi-compose sessions (QoL #18):** `composeStore` in `compose.ts` is no longer a one-window singleton — it holds `sessions: ComposeSession[]` (each `{id, opts, minimized, subject, recipient, saveError}`). `+layout.svelte` renders ONE `<ComposeScreen session={s}>` per session, **keyed by id**, so every window keeps its OWN live `ComposeStore` + TipTap editor. Minimize CSS-hides the panel (`display:none`) — it is **never unmounted** (that would lose in-flight uploads + unsaved edits). Invariant: **exactly one session maximized** — `open(opts)` minimizes the current + maximizes the new (swap; returns the new id, or `null` at the 3-session cap → `inbox`'s `openComposeInContext` toasts `compose.cap_reached`), `restore(id)` swaps symmetrically. Each ComposeScreen pushes `{subject, recipient, saveError}` to its session via `setMeta` (dedupes to avoid an effect↔store loop). The minimized dock is **`ComposeDock.svelte`** (rendered once in `+layout`): one `<button>` bar per minimized session (subject/recipient truncated, **roving tabindex** + Arrow-key nav, an autosave-error badge when `saveError`, a close X). On the auto-minimize swap, focus moves into the new maximized compose (its own focus effect) and a polite `aria-live` region in `+layout` announces `compose.auto_minimized_announce`. Field ids are per-window (`cmp-{sessionId}-to` etc.) so concurrent windows don't collide. ComposeScreen falls back to `sessions[0]` when rendered without a `session` prop (unit tests). Store spec: `compose.test.ts`; dock spec: `ComposeDock.svelte.test.ts`.

**OOO advisory (QoL #7):** a single consolidated out-of-office banner above Subject — `t('compose.ooo_summary')` ("N recipients are out of office"), expandable to a per-person list (localized "until {date}" or indefinite), `aria-live=polite`, dismissible per compose session (sticky — no re-nag on the mailtips refocus refetch), never blocks Send. Fed by the existing mailtips fetch (`compose.fetchMailTips` → `GET /auth/me/mailtips`), whose OOO branch now routes through the **`recipients_ooo_status(p_mailbox_id, p_emails)`** reducer (dates only, resolves via `resolve_identity` so external/unknown addresses are absent — no directory-enumeration oracle; the prior raw-SQL vacation query that leaked the auto-reply subject is gone). Warnings of `type:'ooo'` drive this banner; other mailtips (`large_audience`/`external_recipient`/`no_send_as`) stay inline badges.

**External identity labels:** the existing default-on organization toggle `feature.external_sender_warn` and default-on per-user `users.external_sender_warning` setting control both directions; effective access is their AND. The user-facing Switch lives in `/settings/privacy`, persists through `GET/PUT /api/settings/external-sender`, and refreshes the JMAP session after save. `effective_capabilities.features.external_labels` is the fail-closed browser capability. Inbound `Email.isExternal` renders an `External` Badge beside the sender in expanded and collapsed `MessageCard` identity rows plus the existing warning banner; compose renders localized `[EXTERNAL] recipient` MailTips for external To/Cc/Bcc addresses. Both the handler and SPA filter recipient warnings when disabled. Name-spoof and first-time annotations retain their independent feature flags.

**DLP compose-time policy tip (Feature #8):** a `.dlp-tip` banner below the OOO advisory, driven by `compose.dlpTip` (`fetchDlpCheck`/`debounceDlpCheck`, composeStore.svelte.ts — same recipient-seam `$effect` as mailtips PLUS a subject/bodyText `$effect`, 1s debounce vs mailtips' 500ms since a body payload travels, `POST /auth/me/dlp-check`). This dry-runs the **exact same `dlp_check()` seam send.sql step 5a enforces** (`dlp_evaluate_draft` reducer) — the tip can never disagree with send-time enforcement; pinned by `dlp_evaluate_draft.pg` tests 12-14. `action='block'` → `role="alert"`, `aria-live="assertive"`, never dismissible, and disables Send (extends the existing upload-gate disabled expression + reuses the `send-upload-gate` status slot for the gate copy). `warn`/`quarantine`/`notify_admin` all render at the **warn tier** — `role="status"`, `aria-live="polite"`, dismissible — since only `block` actually refuses to send (quarantine/notify_admin still deliver, with a consequence). Dismissal is scoped to the CURRENT `policyId` (tracked via a plain `lastDlpPolicyId` compared by value, not object identity — `dlpTip` is replaced wholesale on every fetch) so a _different_ policy hit re-surfaces even if the prior one was dismissed. Styles: `--info-bg`/`--info-fg` (warn) vs `--danger-bg`/`--danger-fg` (block) — token-only, mirrors ooo-advisory. The endpoint never returns the regex/matched text, only policy name + action + a coarse `reasonKind` (evasion-oracle guard). Known gap (documented, accepted): attachments are NOT evaluated by this dry-run (v1 non-goal) — send.sql's own attachment-metadata bug fix (real `message_attachments` JSONB via `p_source_message_id`) means an attachment-type/size DLP policy can now block at send with no prior tip; see `reducers/README.md`'s DLP row.

**Draft-content state (`src/lib/stores/composeStore.svelte.ts`):** `ComposeStore` ($state rune class, instantiated **per mount** — NOT a singleton; distinct from the `composeStore` open/minimize/opts writable in `compose.ts`). A6 slice-1 of the ComposeScreen god-component extraction: owns the draft-content group (`fromEmail`/`to`/`cc`/`bcc`/`replyTo`/`subject`/`bodyHtml`/`bodyText`/`categories`/`newCategory`/`attachments`/`signature`/`useSignature`/`signatures`/`selectedSignatureId`/`scheduledAt`/`readReceipt`/`voteOptions`/`voteResponse`/`useVoting`/`priority`/`sensitivity`/`sendTags`/`plainTextOnly`/`smimeEncrypt`/`smimeSign`/`mailboxes`), the `attachmentBytes` derived, and the read-only helpers `parseAddrList`/`getDraftsId`/`getSentId`/`getBodyHTML`/`getBodyText`/`buildMsg`. `resetOwnedState()` is the single draft-state reset used only after successful send, held-state acceptance, or explicit discard; it cancels/revokes attachment resources but deliberately does not own editor DOM, focus, toast/undo timers, menus, window close, identity/signature configuration, or responsive layout. Validation/upload/submission failure never calls it and therefore preserves the draft. **System-folder selection:** `Mailbox/get` intentionally includes delegated tags beside personal tags; personal compose selects the role match without delegated `myRights.maySubmit=true`, while explicit shared compose selects the send-capable delegated match. Picking the first role alone can stage a personal draft under another account and surface `mailboxIds empty`. **Editor seam (arch-A3 echo-loop guard):** the RichTextEditor `content` prop is read once at mount, body writes/reads go imperatively through the editor handle, and the store's body-reading methods take that handle as a **parameter** (`getBodyHTML(editor)` / `getBodyText(editor)` / `buildMsg(editor, userEmail)`) — the store never owns the `bind:this` ref, and there is no reactive content `$effect`. The component instantiates `new ComposeStore()`, binds the template to `compose.*`(Svelte 5 class-field`bind:value`), and keeps everything else (send/draft/GAL/mailtips/attachment-upload/signature-load/UI-toggle logic + all 6 `$effect`s); later slices continue the extraction. Tested in `composeStore.svelte.test.ts` (`buildMsg`payload + address parsing + mailbox lookups +`attachmentBytes` + reset/failure preservation).

**Draft hydration guard:** `ComposeStore.beginHydration()` returns a per-instance generation plus a fingerprint of draft fields that background discovery does not legitimately change. `applyHydration()` validates and applies the synchronous store portion, then returns an updated ticket that also guards the delayed post-`tick()` editor write. `ComposeScreen.loadDraft` therefore applies a response only while both phases remain current; typing or `resetOwnedState()` makes it inert. `fromEmail` is deliberately outside the generic fingerprint because background mailbox/account discovery may initialize or correct it; only the user-driven `handleFromSelectChange()` calls `invalidateHydration()`. Its failure path uses the same ticket, so an old rejected load cannot publish `draftLoadError` into a newer edit/reset while a current failure still uses the existing banner and preserves the draft. Mailbox/signature background loads are deliberately non-invalidating, and one compose window cannot invalidate another. `ComposeScreen.test.ts` drives delayed `emailGet` against the real TipTap editor and real Svelte `tick`, including background From initialization versus manual From intent, pre-response edits, the store/editor phase boundary, two parallel compose instances, current single application, stale/current failure, failure preservation, and exact-once success/held/discard reset.

**Architecture:** Message column (`.sj-compose-main`) + 312px options sidebar (`.sj-compose-options`, stacks below at `<1024px`). 52px header (Pencil icon, title, Minus/close IconButtons). Full-screen replaces the prior floating draggable modal. The overlay is a `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"` with Escape-to-close, a Tab/Shift+Tab focus trap, auto-focus of the To field on open, and focus return to the trigger on close.

**Message column (left/top):**

- **From-selector**: mono-font `<select>` of the canonical primary email + delegated send-as addresses (`data.delegates`) + send-as aliases (`data.aliases`) from `/auth/me`. A fresh compose always selects `own` and submits the primary `users.email`; every non-primary address is an explicit user choice. The server only returns non-primary aliases on the caller's enabled primary mailbox, and the `send` reducer independently authorizes the chosen From (send.sql §1.5), so the picker cannot offer an alias the personal accountId would reject. On change (and once on a fresh compose, after `loadSignatures`), `pickSignatureForAlias` auto-selects the signature: an exact alias match wins, else the **A1 `default_for` discriminator** picks the role-default — a reply/forward (`composeStore.opts.replyTo` set) prefers `'reply'`/`'both'`, a fresh compose prefers `'new'`/`'both'` — falling back to the legacy `is_default` flag then the auto stub. Skipped when restoring a draft (the saved body is authoritative). The signature menu marks the selected row with `aria-current`. **#13 shared-mailbox rows:** appends a primary `sendAsShared` row plus one row per `sendAsAliases` address when the caller holds `send_as`/`full_access`; each alias token pins both the alias From and that shared `accountId`, so draft/send/Sent-copy use the same mailbox the reducer authorizes. Read-only and send-on-behalf-only accounts receive no alias list. An `onBehalfShared` row remains available only for `send_on_behalf` accounts without direct send-as/full-access. The `<select>` binds an **opaque token** (`own`/`d:<email>`/`a:<email>`/`s:<id>:as`/`s:<id>:alias:<encoded-email>`/`s:<id>:onbehalf`), not `fromEmail` directly. `handleFromSelectChange` resolves the token to `compose.fromEmail` + `compose.sharedAccountId` (null = personal; threaded as the trailing `acct` into every JMAP call `ComposeScreen` makes — mailboxGet/emailGet/emailSet/emailSubmissionSet/emailSubmissionCancel). Opening compose from a shared workspace or a reply/forward on a shared message presets the token via `composeStore.open({accountId, from})`; a caption (`cp-from-caption`, `compose.sendingAsShared`/`compose.sendingOnBehalf`) renders under the row whenever From ≠ own.
- **To/Cc/Bcc**: free-text `<input>` with GAL autocomplete (debounced 200ms, `galSearch`), wired as an ARIA combobox (`role="combobox"`, `aria-expanded`/`-controls`/`-activedescendant`; dropdown `role="listbox"`/`option`) with ArrowUp/Down + Enter + Escape keyboard selection. Cc/Bcc revealed via inline text-accent buttons. **A4 merged suggestions:** the same dropdown renders, ahead of GAL results, (1) matching **personal contact groups** (`loadGroups` on open; picking one calls `expandGroup` and appends every member — Outlook "send to group") and (2) **cached past recipients** (`suggestRecipients` from `contactRanking`, instant/localStorage), each with a per-row **X** (`removeRecipient`, `aria-label` `compose.removeRecipient`). Keyboard nav indexes the flat merged list (groups → cache → GAL). `recordSend(email, name)` runs for every recipient on send.
- **Subject**: `<input>` with 998-char max, bold weight.
- **TipTap rich text editor** (`RichTextEditor.svelte` + `RichTextEditorToolbar.svelte`): bold/italic/underline/lists/links + **inline image** (TipTap `Image`, URL-only, `allowBase64:false`; toolbar opens an image-URL dialog mirroring the link dialog) + **table** (TipTap `TableKit` from `@tiptap/extension-table`, `3.26` line, configured `table:{resizable:true}` — `resizable` lives under `table:`, not top-level; toolbar has an insert-table rows×cols dialog cloned from the image dialog + contextual add-row/delete-row/add-column/delete-column/delete-table `IconButton`s shown only when `editor.isActive('table')`; table tags/attrs already sanitizer-allowlisted, so no `sanitize.ts` change; editor-only gridline CSS in `RichTextEditor.svelte`, inline per-cell borders for received-mail parity deliberately skipped) + **font family / size / text color** (TipTap `TextStyle`/`Color`/`FontFamily`/`FontSize`, all bundled in `@tiptap/extension-text-style` on the 3.26 line; toolbar `Font`/`Size` selects + `<input type=color>`). Imperative methods `insertImage(src,alt)`, `setFontFamily(family)`, `setTextColor(color)` (A1).
- **@-mentions in the body** (`mentionExtension.ts`): typing `@` opens a GAL autocomplete; picking a person inserts a `<span class="mention">@Label</span>` chip AND adds them to the **To** recipients. Built on `@tiptap/extension-mention` (`^3.29.2`, pulls first-party `@tiptap/suggestion` for the `@`-trigger / caret `clientRect` / range-replace) — not hand-rolled caret parsing. `createMentionExtension(controller)` configures the Mention node (`{id,label}`, `renderText`/`renderHTML` → `@Label`, cloned from `templateVariableExtension.ts`) with a `suggestion` whose `items` = `galSearch(query, 8)` and whose `command` inserts the chip + calls `controller.onPick`. RichTextEditor registers it **only when the `onMentionPick` prop is given** (compose-only; other RichTextEditor uses stay plain), drives the popup from the suggestion lifecycle into `$state`, and renders it with the compose `.gal-dropdown` visual language anchored to the caret via `position: fixed` (the editor box's `overflow-y:auto` would otherwise clip it). ComposeScreen wires `onMentionPick → compose.mentionRecipient(label,email)`, which **forces `galTarget='to'` first** (a stray Cc/Bcc focus would otherwise leak the mention into the wrong field) then `appendRecipient`. Out of scope: recipient dedupe, mailtips refresh on mention-add, Cc/Bcc targeting. Tests: `mentionExtension.test.ts` + `composeStore.svelte.test.ts` (`mentionRecipient`).
- **Insert → Signature** (A1): a send-bar pen-tool button opens a menu listing the user's signatures (loaded from `/settings/signatures`); selecting one appends its sanitized HTML to the body.
- **Insert → Template** (Quick Parts): a send-bar `FileText` button (`insert-template-btn`) opens a menu listing the account's compose templates (loaded once on open via `emailTemplateList()` from `$lib/jmap/emailTemplates`, over `EmailTemplate/list`); `insertTemplate(tpl)` drops the template body **at the cursor** via `editorRef.getEditor()?.commands.insertContent(sanitizeHTML(tpl.bodyHtml))` — the body is user-authored HTML flowing into the editor + outbound mail, so it MUST pass through `sanitizeHTML` (never inserted raw; guarded by `ComposeScreen.templates.test.ts`). Subject fills in only when the compose subject is still empty. Templates are managed at `/settings/templates`.
- **Attachments**: drag-drop zone, name+size chips, remove ×. **Upload progress (WAVE-5 #3):** each chip carries its own lifecycle (`preparing`→`uploading`→`done`/`failed`, `ComposeStore.handleFiles`/`#uploadOne`) — an indeterminate `Progress` bar during the pre-upload SHA-256 hash, a real-percentage bar during the `putBlob` XHR (`onprogress`), a per-chip Retry on `failed`, and a Cancel-`×` that aborts the in-flight XHR (`cancelUpload`, `AbortController`) and removes the chip rather than leaving a zombie upload. Send is gated ("Uploading N of M…") while any chip is `preparing`/`uploading` (`compose.uploading`, now `$derived` off per-chip status, not a single flag) and blocked on any `failed` chip (`compose.failedUploads`); `buildMsg` only ever includes `done` chips. A same-bytes dedupe fast-path (matching `blobId` already `done`) skips the redundant PUT and jumps straight to 100.
- **Inline body images (WAVE-5 #2):** paste/drag an image (or the toolbar Insert-image dialog's "upload from computer" input) into the body — whitelisted types only (`INLINE_IMAGE_TYPES`: png/jpeg/gif/webp; SVG is script-bearing and the compose preview is NOT sandboxed, so it falls through to the ordinary file-attachment path instead of being inlined). `ComposeStore.stageInlineImage` is `handleFiles`'s inline sibling — same row shape (`ComposeAttachment` gains `inline`/`cid`/`previewUrl`), same `#uploadOne` machinery, so `uploading`/`failedUploads`/`attachmentBytes` (the shared 25 MiB budget) cover it for free; a rejected file (bad type or over budget) is forwarded to the normal attach-as-file path (`onNonInlineFiles`) rather than dropped, so an oversized/mixed paste shows the SAME `attachmentTooLarge` toast either way. `RichTextEditor`'s `handlePaste`/`handleDrop` (editorProps) intercept image files ahead of the compose container's own paste/drop-to-attach handlers (`stopPropagation`) and insert an `<img>` via `InlineImage` (`inlineImageExtension.ts` — the stock TipTap Image node plus one extra `uploadId` attribute, `data-upload-id`, used only to locate the node; status/progress live purely in the reactive `ComposeAttachment` row, never as node attrs, so an upload's many progress ticks never touch the ProseMirror doc/undo history). A live upload shows a `position:fixed` progress overlay tracked over the `<img>` (`inlineUploads` prop, same rect-tracking technique as the mention dropdown above); a FAILED upload's `<img>` is removed from the body (`removeInlineImage`) and the row survives as an ordinary failed file-attachment chip instead (reuses that list's retry/remove UI — no separate in-body error state). `pruneOrphanedInlineImages(html)` (called from `handleUpdate` on every edit) drops/cancels a row whose `<img>` the user deleted directly (backspace), so it can't eat the budget or keep Send gated invisibly. At submit, `buildMsg` rewrites each `done`, still-referenced inline image's `blob:` preview URL to `cid:<id>@setjoin.inline` **before** `sanitizeHTML` (DOMPurify's default URI allowlist has no `blob:` scheme but does allow `cid:`) and adds `{cid, disposition:'inline'}` to its attachment entry — the backend (Content-ID emission, inbound `cid:`→`/api/blobs/<sha>` rewrite via `renderEmailBodyHTML`) needed no changes.
- **Mailtips**: debounced 500ms poll; banners for large audience, external recipient, vacation auto-reply, no-send-as.
- **Send bar**: `Button variant="primary"` (Send/Schedule send, disabled when To empty), `IconButton` for attach/discard, undo-window hint, draft-save-error retry.
- **Undo-send**: countdown from the configurable `onSendUndoWindowSeconds` preference (5–300s); the send toast `durationMs` is kept in sync with it; `emailSubmissionCancel` reopens compose with `{ draftId }` to restore the draft.
- **Draft autosave**: 5s debounce via `emailSet`; `editingDraftId` tracks in-progress draft.

**Options sidebar (right/bottom at `<1024px`):**

- **Delivery**: `Select` "Send now"/"Custom…"; selecting "Custom…" reveals an inline M1 `DateTimePicker` that writes `scheduledAt`. `Switch` read receipt.
- **Meeting**: `Switch` "Include meeting invite" → date/time/duration selects for iTIP/iMIP booking.
- **Tag on send**: top-level tag chips (multi-select, outline treatment); wired to B3 `onSuccessUpdateEmail` in submission.
- **More disclosure** (`ChevronDown`/`ChevronUp`): Priority select, Sensitivity select, Voting buttons Switch, S/MIME **Encrypt** Switch, S/MIME **Sign** Switch (A1; per-message `smimeSign` flag → `signSMIME: true` in the submission create map → signing handled Go-side in `EmailSubmission/set` before the reducer; D5 compose UX: sign failures surface as per-creationId `notCreated[*].type` markers mapped to human copy — `smime_no_cert` → "Add an S/MIME certificate in Settings → Security to sign mail." + Settings link, `smime_no_matching_cert` → "Can't sign as {From} — there's no certificate for that address.", `smime_sign_failed` → "Couldn't sign this message. Try again."; draft is PRESERVED on all three; raw marker codes are never shown. Toggle-time no-cert hint: when Sign is turned on and `hasDefaultCert` is false, a `cp-smime-hint` paragraph is shown immediately — before Send — so the user isn't surprised at send time. **S/MIME Encrypt (SP1 Phase 4):** the Encrypt switch sets `encryptSMIME: true` on the submission create so the Go handler runs the fail-closed cert-availability check; its three `notCreated[*].type` markers map the same way — `smime_encrypt_no_cert` → "Can't encrypt to {addresses} …" listing EVERY recipient without a usable cert (from the entry's `addresses` array; falls back to a localized "some recipients"), `smime_encrypt_no_sender_cert` → "Add your own S/MIME certificate in Settings → Security …", `smime_encrypt_no_journal_cert` → "… contact your admin" (never blames the user); unknown encrypt markers fall through to the generic `compose.send_failed`. A second `cp-smime-hint` shows when Encrypt is on and `encryptNoCertRecipients` is non-empty (populated by the future recipient cert-presence API; the send-time check is the authoritative gate).), **Plain text only** Switch (A1; drops the HTML body part in `buildMsg()` so recipients get text only), Categories chip input.
- **Attachment reminder** (A1): on Send, `mentionsAttachment(subject, body, locale)` (`$lib/utils/attachmentReminder.ts`) scans subject+body (EN/ZH/DE keyword sets, URLs stripped to avoid `…/attachments/…` false positives); with zero attachments it warns once (sets `error`) and a second Send goes through. Re-armed whenever the attachment set changes.
- **Assist panel** (gated on `effectiveCaps.features.assist`): 4 action buttons (Draft it/Polish/Translate/Shorten) → `POST /assist/complete` (B4); result writes into TipTap body.

**Send toast:** `showToast` with schedule-mode-specific copy (Cancel for immediate, Undo for scheduled), meeting room booking suffix. `durationMs` follows the configurable undo window (`onSendUndoWindowSeconds`, default 30s).

**Minimize-to-pill:** `composeStore.minimize()` shows fixed bottom-right pill (Pencil icon, title, close); click restores via `maximize()`.

**Reply/forward:** Reply context loaded from `emailGet`; prepopulates To/subject/body with quoted content. Forward prepopulates body + attachments.

---

### Calendar (`src/routes/calendar/+page.svelte`)

- **Views**: month, week, **work week** (Mon–Fri, 5 columns; `viewMode='workweek'` — Monday-start via `(getDay()+6)%7`, reuses the week grid/filtering/drag verbatim), day, agenda — toggled by `viewMode` store.
- **View options (sidebar, webmail-only, no backend)**: **working-hours shading** — two `<Input type="time">` persisted in `localStorage['sj-cal-work-start'|'sj-cal-work-end']` (defaults `09:00`/`17:00`); hour-slots outside the range get a `.off-hours` tint (`isOffHours(h)`; an inverted/empty range shades nothing). The same persisted values seed the scheduling-assistant working-hours prefill. **Second time zone** — a native `<select>` (IANA list from `Intl.supportedValuesOf('timeZone')`, stored value validated so a bad entry can't blank the grid) persisted in `localStorage['sj-cal-tz2']`; when set, a second read-only `.time-gutter` renders before the day columns in week/work-week/day views, labelled via `formatHourTz(day,h,tz)` (anchored to a real date so DST is correct). Display-only.
- **Multi-calendar**: sidebar list of calendars with visibility toggles (`toggleCalendarVisibility`); separate color per calendar.
- **Event CRUD**: create/edit/detail modal with title, calendar selector, start/end date-time, all-day toggle, location, description, recurrence, attendees (required + a separate **Optional attendees** field → `role:'optional'`; the three attendee buckets — required / optional / resource — stay disjoint on read and are concatenated on write; the detail list shows the role next to participation status via `attendeeRoleLabel`), delegation, a per-event color, a "Show as" free/busy status (`Select` → `CalendarEvent.freeBusyStatus` 'free'|'busy'|'tentative', default 'busy'; the organizer's own busy status, orthogonal to attendee RSVP; round-trips through `event_get`/`event_set`), and a **"Private" toggle** (`Checkbox` → `CalendarEvent.privacy` 'public'|'private', default 'public'; the v1 UI is binary — 'secret'/CONFIDENTIAL exists in the type but is not surfaced; non-owner shared viewers are server-side-redacted). Color: `<input type="color">` in the editor patches `CalendarEvent.color` (nullable — empty clears it, inheriting the calendar color); the grid renders each event via `eventColor(ev)` = `ev.color ? safeColor(ev.color) : getCalendarColor(...)`, so the user/shared-calendar-controlled `ev.color` passes the same `safeColor()` (#hex-only) CSS-injection guard as calendar color.
- **Event reminders ("Remind me")**: a `Select` in the event modal (mirrors the freeBusyStatus Select) — none / at start / 5 / 15 / 30 min / 1 hour / 1 day before. `buildValarm(start, reminder)` computes an **ABSOLUTE trigger** client-side (`start − N`) and writes `CalendarEvent.valarm = [{trigger:<ISO>, action:'DISPLAY'}]` (empty array clears it). The value is RECOMPUTED on every save (create AND update) from the possibly-edited start, so a start-time change moves the reminder; the reducer's `valarm` patch is patch-present-guarded. Editing an event reverses the stored trigger back to the nearest preset via `reminderFromValarm`. Firing is the existing `scan_due_reminders()` → `remind` handler ledger (fires once at the client-computed absolute instant); recurring-event (RRULE) alarms + `ACTION:EMAIL` are DEFERRED.
- **Print / agenda (webmail-only, no backend)**: a toolbar **Print** button → `printCurrentView()` filters the already-loaded `$events` by `$visibleCalendarIds` + the current `getViewRange`, then `printAgenda(list, title)` (`$lib/calendar/printAgenda.ts`, mirrors `$lib/mail/printMessage.ts`): `window.open` → `document.write` a static agenda table → opener-driven `win.print()`. EVERY field (title/location/description/attendee names — all user/shared-calendar-controlled) is `escapeHtml`-escaped; no raw event text is written. No server ICS-export endpoint (CalDAV already serves own events).
- **ICS file import**: a toolbar **Import** button → an Import `Dialog` with `<input accept=".ics">` (mirrors the migration wizard's file input) + a target-calendar `Select` (writable calendars only) + a conflict-strategy `Select` (skip / rename = keep both / overwrite). `runImport()` reads the file text and calls `importEvents(calendarId, ics, strategy)` (store → `calendarEventImport` client → JMAP `CalendarEvent/import`), then refreshes. Ownership is enforced server-side (the `calendar_import_ics` reducer verifies calendar ownership before any insert and routes each event through `jmap_calendar_event_set_create`). Webcal-URL subscribe is DEFERRED.
- **Recurrence (A3)**: `RecurrenceEditor.svelte` (`lib/components/calendar/`) — freq/interval/BYDAY/end controls that compose a real RRULE via the pure `lib/calendar/rrule.ts` (`buildRrule`/`parseRrule`, the single home for RRULE string logic). Editing a recurring event opens an **occurrence-vs-series prompt** (This occurrence / This and following / All events); the first two call the `updateOccurrence` store action (`CalendarEvent/setOccurrence`), the last patches the master.
- **Shared calendars (A3)**: "Open shared calendar" toolbar action → `openSharedCalendar(ownerEmail)` (store) → `Calendar/getShared` + `CalendarEvent/getShared`; returned calendars/events merge into the stores tagged read-only with a "Shared" sidebar badge. **Only the owner email is sent — never a viewer id; the viewer is the authenticated user server-side.**
- **Calendar sharing (A3 + sharing-spec §6-A)**: `ShareCalendarDialog.svelte` — per-calendar grant/revoke with a level picker (Free/busy → `freebusy`, Can view → `read`, Full details/Editor → `write`, Owner → `admin`) via `shareCalendar`/`revokeShare`/`loadShares` (`Calendar/shareSet`/`shareGet`). **Default-visibility section** (spec 2026-06-27-sharing-delegation §6-A): rendered only when `GET /api/me/sharing` reports `defaultsEnabled: true` (the server-computed `sharing.defaults` toggle — the load fails closed to hidden); audience `Select` (Everyone in my org / A department via the user-facing `directoryTree()`; a group audience is kept display-only — no user-facing group source exists) + the shared `LevelSegment` 无/闲忙/详细 radiogroup; Save upserts the user-scope default via `setMyDefault` (`PUT /api/me/sharing/default`); a status line names the most-specific inherited org/OU/group baseline ("… — your setting overrides it"); on save failure the prior persisted value stays selected (§6-D). Client: `$lib/api/sharing.ts`. Tests: `ShareCalendarDialog.test.ts`.
- **Rooms (A3)**: `RoomFinder.svelte` — capacity-filtered bookable-room picker (`listResources` → `CalendarEvent/resourceList`); picking a room appends a `role:'resource'` attendee (the gateway books it on create).
- **RSVP**: accept/decline/tentative via `rsvpEvent`. **Delegate respond (A3)**: when the open event's organizer is a manager the user holds a delegation for (`listDelegations` → `Calendar/delegateList`), a "Respond on behalf of {name}" control calls `rsvpOnBehalf` (passes the manager email; the gateway resolves the manager mailbox and gates on `mailbox_delegates`).
- **Send update (A3)**: organized meetings with attendees show a "Send update" button → `sendMeetingUpdate(eventId)` (`CalendarEvent/sendUpdate`) which bumps SEQUENCE and re-issues the iTIP REQUEST.
- **Scheduling assistant**: `showSchedulingModal` — attendee email list + duration + work hours + date range → `findFreeSlots` → shows available slots to pick. An in-editor "Add Scheduling Assistant" affordance opens the SAME dialog prefilled from the open event's attendees/time (reused, not rebuilt).
- **Free/busy**: backend `free_busy` JMAP method integration.
- **Identities**: loaded via `loadIdentities`; used for organizer field.
- **WebSocket push**: reloads on `Calendar`/`CalendarEvent` change events, registered through a `PushHandlerStore` (the shared WS→action seam; no debounce — `refreshEvents`' loading guard coalesces). TasksApp/ContactsApp likewise register `['Task']`/`['Contact']` handlers through their own `PushHandlerStore`.
- **Container-responsive**: extracts to `<CalendarApp>` component for split-view slot mounting (M6).

### Contacts & GAL (`src/routes/contacts/+page.svelte`)

- **Address books**: sidebar list; switch between personal books and GAL.
- **GAL search** (`$isGAL`): calls `searchGAL` which hits the LDAP GAL endpoint; results shown in grid.
- **GAL/directory address set (ARCH-004)**: the directory/GAL is **org-wide and address-aware** — `gal_search` / `directory_members` / `directory_search` return each person's FULL address set (`users.email` primary ∪ `aliases.email` ∪ `users.ad_upn`) under an `addresses` array of `{email, type: 'primary'|'alias'|'upn', isPrimary}`, deduplicated primary-first (Exchange GAL parity). The webmail renders the whole set, not just one email: `DirectoryMemberList` lists the primary plus a secondary list of aliases/UPN (each tagged with its kind), the GAL **list card** shows the primary + a `+N more` indicator, and the GAL **detail modal** lists every address with a Primary/Alias/UPN kind label. Personal contacts (which carry `emails`, not `addresses`) are unchanged. Pure shaping in `src/lib/utils/directoryAddresses.ts` (`normalizeAddresses` / `addressKindKey`); `addresses?: DirectoryAddress[]` added to `ContactCard` and `DirectoryMember`. Labels: `directory.address.{alias,upn,primary,other,emails,more}` (en/de/zh).
- **Directory tree mode** (`directoryMode`): `DirectorySidebar.svelte` shows org-chart department tree; `DirectoryMemberList.svelte` shows members of selected dept (with their full address set); loads from `src/lib/stores/directory.ts`.
- **Contact CRUD**: create/edit/detail modal with displayName, firstName, lastName, company, jobTitle, department, multi-value email array, multi-value phone array, notes, avatar.
- **Contact photo render**: the list/grid card, the detail modal, and the duplicate-merge cards render the stored contact photo via the `Avatar` `src` prop (blob at `/blobs/{blobId}`). Local `avatarSrc(c)` helper narrows `ContactCard.avatar` (`string` bare-blobId | `{blobId}`) to the URL; empty/dead/non-image blob → Avatar's initials fallback. (The `SenderCard` avatar is deliberately NOT wired — it needs a sender→contact resolve that carries the photo; see the ponytail note in `SenderCard.svelte`.)
- **Avatar upload**: `uploadBlob` for contact photo.
- **vCard import**: import button → `fetch('/carddav/.../import')`.
- **Contact import — CSV + vCard (A4)**: "Import CSV" topbar button opens `CsvImportModal.svelte`, which handles **both** `.csv` and `.vcf` (file `accept=".csv,.vcf,text/csv,text/vcard"`; it branches on the chosen file's extension via a `format` state). **CSV** is the 3-step wizard: step 1 POSTs the file to `/contacts/import/csv/preview` (multipart) for `{headers, mapping, sample}`; step 2 lets the user correct the auto-mapping per target field (firstName/lastName/fullName/email/company/jobTitle/phone/notes, index `-1` = not imported) + a dedupe `strategy` (`skip`/`overwrite`); step 3 POSTs file+`addressBookId`+`strategy`+`mapping` JSON to `/contacts/import/csv`. **vCard** is self-describing → it SKIPS the preview/column-map step (no `mapping`), reusing the same `map` step for just the strategy picker (+ a `contacts.vcf.hint` line), then POSTs file+`addressBookId`+`strategy` to `/contacts/import/vcf`. Both show `{imported, skipped, errors}` and emit `onDone` so ContactsApp refreshes. Backend (Go `internal/contacts` handler → `internal/migration` `ImportCSV`/`ImportVCF`) owns parsing + address-book ownership checks; on `imported>0` the handler bumps the contact state token (`notify_contact_state`) so other sessions / `Contact/changes` see the new rows. Keys: `contacts.import.{title,choose_file}`, `contacts.vcf.hint`.
- **Personal contact groups (A4)**: a "Groups" sidebar section (non-directory view) lists user-created groups with member counts; "New group" + per-group edit/delete open `ContactGroupModal.svelte` (name + member picker over the `contacts` store list + a free-email input; computes an add/remove member delta on save). Selecting a group filters the main pane to its expanded recipients via `expandGroup`. Backed by the `contactGroups` store and the `ContactGroup/*` JMAP methods. Distinct from admin/SCIM org distribution lists — these are strictly per-mailbox.
- **Find & merge duplicates**: a "Find duplicates" topbar button (non-directory, non-GAL view) calls the store's `findDuplicates()` and switches the main pane to a duplicates view. Each cluster (contacts sharing an email) renders the involved contacts as radio-selectable cards (default keeper = the fuller card by `completeness()`), a shared-email header, and a **Merge** button. Merge drops the first non-kept member into the keeper (`store.merge(keepId, dropId)`), toasts, then refreshes the list + re-detects; clusters >2 merge one pair and re-detect. Backed by `duplicates`/`duplicateContacts` stores over the `ContactCard/findDuplicates` + `ContactCard/merge` JMAP methods. Labels: `contacts.duplicates.*` (en/de/zh).
- **View modes**: list vs. grid toggle.
- **Search**: `searchText` filters contacts; debounced query.
- **OrgChart** (`OrgChart.svelte`): organizational chart component.
- **Contact ranking** (`src/lib/utils/contactRanking.ts`): tracks send frequency to weight GAL autocomplete suggestions. Entry shape `{[email]: {count, name}}` (legacy bare-`number` shape still read for back-compat). Exposes `recordSend(email, name?)`, `getSendCount`, `rankContacts`, plus the A4 auto-complete cache: `suggestRecipients(query, limit)` (frequency-ranked, prefix/substring over name OR address) and `removeRecipient(email)`. Consumed by the compose recipient autocomplete.
- **Container-responsive**: extracts to `<ContactsApp>` for split-view slot (M6).

### Tasks (`src/routes/tasks/+page.svelte`)

- **View modes**: Kanban (columns by status) vs. list toggle; `viewMode` store.
- **Drag-and-drop reorder + kanban move (QoL #20)**: native HTML5 DnD (no drag lib). Cards/rows are `draggable`; dropping ON a card/row reorders (a fractional `sortOrder` computed by the pure `computeReorderSortOrder(prev,next)` helper in `stores/tasks.ts`, unit-tested in `tasks.test.ts`), dropping on empty column space moves to that status appended at the end. Both ride the existing `Task/set` patch path (`updateTask(id,{status?,sortOrder})`) — no new endpoint. Backed by the V103 `tasks.sort_order` (DOUBLE PRECISION, global/mailbox-wide, backfilled from the old derived order); `jmap_task_get`/`jmap_task_query` lead their ORDER BY with `sort_order ASC`. The Start/Done buttons remain the keyboard/a11y fallback.
- **Status filter**: `statusFilter` — needs-action, in-process, completed, cancelled.
- **Search**: `searchText` drives `Task/query` filter.
- **Task CRUD**: create/edit/detail modal with title, description, status, priority (0–9 numeric), due date, reminder, categories, **assignee**, **recurrence**, **parent task** (subtasks).
- **Mutation authority / Restore copy:** `src/lib/tasks/actionCoordinator.svelte.ts` owns personal/shared-list create, update, delete, move, reorder, and restore-copy action lifecycles. Every call captures an immutable `ActionTarget`, coalesces the same action key, rejects a conflicting action on that task while allowing other tasks, and attempts the captured-origin refresh exactly once; its target-scoped completion separates a confirmed mutation from an optional post-commit refresh error so a refresh outage never invites a duplicate create/delete. A completion from an old view cannot close or write an error into the current dialog/view. `taskLists.ts` exposes the no-implicit-refresh adapter mode plus `refreshListTaskTarget(listId)`, which fetches the captured list but publishes only if it remains current. Save failures remain inside the open dialog, use `role=alert`, preserve the draft, and receive focus. Delete offers **Restore copy**, never Undo: it creates a new identity with only title, description, status, priority, due/reminder, and categories; it does not promise original ID, start, assignment, recurrence, linkage, checklist, hierarchy, comments, or activity. Tests: `tasks/actionCoordinator.test.ts`, `TasksApp.behavior.test.ts`, `stores/taskLists.test.ts`.
- **Subtasks (ONE level, Outlook/Gmail parity)**: a **parent-task `<Select>`** in the create/edit modal (bound to `form.parentId`) whose options come from the already-loaded `$tasks` store — top-level tasks only (`!t.parentId`), excluding self, and empty when the edited task already HAS children (so no 2-level chain can form). No server call, no autocomplete: the candidate set is bounded and local, so a native `<Select>` replaces the assignee GAL pattern. On **create** `parentId` is omitted when blank (the create reducer parses it as raw `BIGINT` — an empty string would error); on **edit** it is sent present-possibly-empty so `NULLIF(...,'')` in `jmap_task_set_update` clears it back to top-level. The reducer rejects self/cross-tenant parents. The **list view** renders top-level rows with their children indented beneath (client-side parent→children map mirroring `groupByStatus`), and each parent shows a `$derived` "N/M done" roll-up (`tasks.subtasks_progress`). Kanban is unchanged (flat by status). i18n: `tasks.parent`, `tasks.parent_none`, `tasks.subtasks_progress` (en/de/zh).
- **Assignee picker**: a debounced (200ms) GAL-autocomplete text field (`galSearch` → `ContactCard[]` dropdown; mirrors the compose recipient picker but single-value — a task has ONE assignee). Selecting a directory person stores their email; free-typing an email is allowed too. The form sends **`assigneeEmail`** (a string) — never `assigneeUserId`; the reducer (`jmap_task_set_create/update`) resolves the email to an internal user (case-insensitive) and fails the object (`notCreated`/`notUpdated`) on an unresolvable/non-internal address. Sent **present-but-empty** (not omitted) so an edit can unassign. Displayed as `assigneeName || assigneeEmail` on the kanban card, list row, and detail.
- **Recurrence**: a preset `<Select>` (Does not repeat / Daily / Weekly / Monthly / Yearly) ↔ an RFC-5545 **`recurrenceRule`** string (`FREQ=DAILY`…; '' = none), threaded through `createTask`/`updateTask` (`taskSet` passes `Partial<Task>` verbatim — no whitelist). Seeded from the task's stored rule on edit; an unrecognised rule shows a read-only "Custom" option and is preserved untouched. Completing a recurring task auto-spawns the next occurrence server-side; the UI only sets the rule.
- **In-task checklist (QoL #21)**: a compact `<Checkbox>`+`<Input>` list in the create/edit modal (below Categories) editing `form.checklist` (`{id,text,done}[]`); saved as `data.checklist` on the existing `Task/set` path (whole-array replace — the `task_checklist_normalize` reducer helper validates each item + assigns a server `id`, reused by create + update). The kanban card and list row show a `n/m` progress badge (`checklistProgress`, `tasks.checklist_progress`). Backed by the V104 `tasks.checklist` JSONB column (top-level array CHECK; item shape validated in-reducer). Distinct from the heavyweight one-level `parent_id` subtasks. i18n: `tasks.checklist*` (en/de/zh).
- **Activity feed + comments (QoL #17)**: the **detail modal** carries a collapsible `<details>` **Activity** section — the unified feed (`TaskComment/get` → `taskActivityList`) of comments (verbatim body) + system change-log rows (`status_change`/`assignee_change`/`due_change`, rendered client-side from `kind`+`meta` via `activitySystemText`). A plain `Textarea` + submit posts a comment (`TaskComment/set` → `taskCommentCreate`); a lightweight caret-position **`@`-mention** trigger reuses the same debounced `galSearch(q,8)` as the assignee picker (no TipTap), collecting picked emails into `commentMentions` sent with the comment (the reducer resolves + best-effort notifies each). i18n: `tasks.activity*`, `tasks.comment_*` (en/de/zh).
- **Status grouping**: `groupByStatus` helper groups tasks by status for Kanban columns.
- **Priority display**: `priorityLabel` + `priorityColor` helpers.
- **Overdue detection**: `isOverdue` helper.
- **Date formatting**: `formatDateShort`.
- **Container-responsive**: extracts to `<TasksApp>` for split-view slot (M6).
- **Shared/team task lists (Phase 1, plan `2026-07-08-shared-team-task-lists.md`)**: feature-gated on the session capability `urn:ietf:params:jmap:tasks.sharedLists` (read once in `onMount`, no extra round trip). `src/lib/stores/taskLists.ts` owns the lists array (`taskLists`), the active-view discriminated union (`activeView`: `personal`|`assigned`|`list{listId}`, switched via `setActiveView`), the assigned-to-me rows (`assignedTasks`), and the on-screen list's tasks (`listTasks`/`listState`) — a monotonic load-token guard (the `stores/tasks.ts` pin, ported) protects `loadActiveView` from an out-of-order response. **The existing kanban/list board is reused, not forked**: `TasksApp.svelte`'s `boardTasks` derived picks `$listTasks` vs `$tasks`; its coordinator adapter selects list-scoped `TaskList/taskSet` or personal `Task/set` from the captured target, never from the post-await current view. The assignee field in the create/edit form swaps to a member-roster `<Select>` when a list is active (assignment must land on a member, BZ133 server-side) — the personal view keeps the free-email GAL picker. **Assigned-to-me** (`$activeView.kind === 'assigned'`) is a separate list-mode-only render (no kanban — the only legal mutation is status) with a status `<select>` wired to `setAssignedStatus` (optimistic update, toast-surfaced rollback on failure). Sidebar nav: My Tasks / Assigned to me (open-count badge) / Shared lists (color dot + openCount + owner-only kebab — Rename/Members/Delete via the `Menu` primitive) / + New list. List rename/create use a small `Dialog`; delete is `confirm()`-gated (not act-then-undo — it revokes every member's access and re-personalizes tasks, the same bar `/settings/rules`' delete uses). `TaskListMembersDialog.svelte` (new component) owns roster management: owner-only add-by-email row, self-leave/owner-remove buttons gated identically to the reducer's own authz (compares the roster row's email against `loadSession()`'s `username`). Push: the existing `push.register(['Task'], …)` handler also calls `onTaskListPush(null)` — the shared-list seam (`notify_task_list_state`) rides the SAME `task:{mailbox_id}` channel, and the push framework abstracts it to the generic "Task changed" signal with no per-list id, so `onTaskListPush` refetches whatever's on screen rather than risk missing an update. i18n: `tasks.lists.*`/`tasks.assigned.*`/`tasks.assignee`/`tasks.unassigned`/`tasks.createdBy` (en/de/zh — zh reviewed against the Feishu 任务 vocabulary: 清单/成员/负责人). Tests: `stores/taskLists.test.ts` (view switching, monotonic-token races, captured-target refresh, push refetch scoping, optimistic-status rollback).

### Directory (`src/routes/directory/+page.svelte`)

- Standalone directory browser; org-chart + member list view of the company directory.
- Uses `DirectorySidebar` + `DirectoryMemberList` components and `src/lib/stores/directory.ts`.

### Reminders (`src/lib/components/RemindersWindow.svelte`, A6)

- The Scheduled-Actions reminder surface — a fixed bottom-right panel that lists active reminders fired by the `remind` handler (calendar VALARM / task `reminder_at` / flagged email), each with a Dismiss `IconButton` + three inline snooze `Button`s (`5m`/`1h`/tomorrow); Dismiss All in the header; the panel renders **only when the list is non-empty**. Lucide `CalendarClock`/`SquareCheck`/`Flag`/`BellRing` by source type, i18n (`reminders.*`, keys already present in all three locales), token-only styling, `<section aria-label>`. **SECURITY: reminder title/body render as TEXT (never `{@html}`)** — they carry attacker-influenceable event/task text. Backed by `RemindersStore` (`$lib/stores/reminders.svelte.ts`, `$state` runes class: `load`/`dismiss`/`dismissAll`/`snooze`, all optimistic + re-sync on failure; `presetToISO(preset)` resolves a snooze preset to an absolute instant). Mounted **once in the authenticated shell** (`src/routes/+layout.svelte`). Live refresh: on mount `store.load()`, plus a `PushHandlerStore.register(['Email','Task','Calendar'], …, 500ms)` that re-fetches on any of those pushes — a reminder fire (`bump_reminder_state`) emits a `state`-bearing payload on the mailbox `tag:` channel, which the gateway surfaces as an Email/Mailbox/Thread StateChange, so an Email-domain listener catches every fire (a dedicated `Reminder` StateChange key would target it precisely — deferred). Backed by client.ts `reminderGet`/`reminderDismiss`/`reminderSnooze` (`Reminder/{get,dismiss,snooze}`).

### Approvals (`src/routes/approvals/+page.svelte`)

- **User-facing approval inbox**: lists all pending-for-me and submitted `approval_requests` from `GET /me/approvals`. Each item shows consumer type chip (JIT role / External send / Group moderation / Leave / Expense), subject, status badge (Pending/Approved/Denied/Expired), requester display, expiry countdown for pending. Pending-for-me items have Approve and Deny buttons that call `POST /me/approvals/{id}/decide` (inline confirm dialog with optional comment field). Error mapping: BZ107 → "You are not the designated approver", BZ109 → "Self-approval is not allowed", BZ106 → "Request is no longer pending". Auto-refreshes on WebSocket push.
- **Cancel** (My requests, own pending row): `POST /me/approvals/{id}/cancel` — engine-level, all consumers (spec §10.1 gap closed by V110; the button pre-existed in `ApprovalDetail.svelte` but silently 400ed until the endpoint shipped — it used to POST `decide` with `decision:'cancel'`). BZ125 → calm "already decided/cancelled" inline message, row removed.
- **HR leave/expense (V110, 4th+5th consumers)**: `NewHrRequestDialog.svelte` — "New request" button in the queue header opens a leave/expense form (native `<input type="date">`, dynamic expense line items, receipt upload via the existing `jmap/client.ts uploadBlob` content-addressed helper); posts `POST /me/approvals/hr` `{kind, form}`; 400/422 detail shown inline. `HrEvidence.svelte` — detail-panel evidence renderer wired into `ApprovalDetail.svelte`'s dispatch (leave: type/dates/reason; expense: line-item table + receipt download links + total). HR forms and the safe `guest.invite` evidence payload are the only consumer payloads that ride `GET /me/approvals`; mail replay payloads remain withheld.
- **Guest invite evidence (Guest G1 Task 7):** `ApprovalQueue.svelte` and `ApprovalDetail.svelte` label `guest.invite` explicitly and render the safe list payload's `requester_name`/`invited_email` evidence (plus optional `message` in detail); the base list response does not promise top-level requester display fields. No token or mail replay body can enter this payload.
- Tests: `approvals.test.ts`, `approvals/hr-approvals.test.ts`.

### Drive (`src/routes/drive/+page.svelte`, P1)

- **Office foundation + Drive History (V220/V224, default OFF):** `VersionDrawer.svelte` progressively loads `/drive/files/{id}/history` into Current/Timeline/Details while retaining existing version/branch actions when an older or disabled server returns 404. Office choices are also progressive: web exposes only reducer-authorized online providers; Electron additionally asks the native bridge for installed apps after the server authorizes local-open. Online launch POSTs the short-lived token to the configured HTTPS editor, never in its URL; native local-open passes only `(spaceId,nodeId,appId)`, never a renderer path or credential. `/admin/(system)/office` is the provider task hub: configure, SSRF-safe qualify with a real synthetic save receipt, and explicit activate remain distinct `office.view`/`office.manage` actions. `/admin/(system)/apps` is the `apps.view`/`apps.manage` curated Collabora CODE test card: it requests a fixed install, polls authoritative operation state, shows digest/license/support ownership and stops at `connected_inactive`. Neither UI can directly enable an unqualified provider; every mutation requires an audit reason.

Capability-gated the same way as mail/calendar/contacts/tasks (the `appForPath` route guard in `+layout.svelte`, `'drive'` in `AppId`/`APP_ORDER`/`APP_PATHS`/`RAIL_APPS`), behind `feature.drive` (DEFAULT OFF).

- **`DriveApp.svelte`** (`src/lib/components/drive/`) owns layout + routing only (arch-hardening C7 shrank it from a data-fetching-and-mutation monolith): `SpaceList` (spaces from `GET /api/drive/spaces`, personal pinned first by SERVER order — the component never re-sorts; classification badge is icon+text together, never color-only; a `Progress` quota bar renders when `quotaBytes` is set), `DriveTree` (self-fetching lazy per-folder tree — `GET /api/drive/spaces/{id}/tree` with no `parent` for root, `?parent=` per expand, cached in a `Map`; `role="tree"`/`"treeitem"`, `aria-expanded`; Arrow-Right/Left expand/collapse, Enter opens), `FileList` (presentational; row actions rename/trash/restore/destroy call back to `DriveApp`, which performs the API call + reloads; trash-view toggle), `VersionDrawer` (timeline with a Conflict badge on any version whose `conflictOf` is non-null, Download for `regular`-classification spaces only — secure/gxp show a "View" button (P3's `SecureViewer`) instead of a download link), `MembersSheet` (self-fetching roster + manager-gated grant/revoke; its add flow reuses JMAP `directorySearch`, resolves the selected employee card's decimal `users.id`, filters current members, rejects malformed IDs, and ignores stale async responses; closing/reopening or switching `spaceId` resets the pending person, role, results, errors, and in-flight generation so grant intent never crosses contexts; guests never enter this picker because the shared SQL visibility seam excludes the guest discriminator and the authoritative Drive reducer separately forbids ad-hoc guest grants), `UploadProgress` (per-file tray, now backed by `uploadOrchestrator.svelte.ts` below). 30-second `GET /api/drive/changes` poll is the §4.2 floor (the SSE nudge is P2's sync engine). Version-save (the gxp change-note gate + the BZ149 save-as-branch offer) and upload bookkeeping moved out into their own modules (below); `DriveApp` passes them getters/callbacks for the bits that stay its own concern (selection state, tree reload, the shared banner).
- **Transport**: `$lib/drive/api.ts` — typed fetch wrappers over every `/api/drive/*` route, reusing `$lib/http`'s auth/timeout/DPoP machinery (`httpRequest`/`typedFetch`) rather than reimplementing it; every non-2xx response is projected onto a `DriveError { status, code, detail, extensions }` (the Task 16 RFC 7807 `code` extension) so callers branch on `.code`. `fetchVersionContent`/`fetchVersionPreview` (arch-hardening C8) are the typed wrappers over `GET .../content` and `GET .../preview` sharing one implementation, calling `httpRequest` directly (they return a raw `Response`, not JSON, so they bypass the problem transport below). **Arch review #A1 (webmail half):** `ChatError`/`MeetError`/`DriveError` were three verbatim ~80-line copies of the same "RFC 7807 body JSON-encoded as the Error message, decoded back into a typed error" trick (needed because `httpRequest`'s outer catch always rewraps a thrown error as `new Error(parseError(err).message)`, erasing a thrown subclass's identity) — differing only in `DriveError`'s `holderId` field. Collapsed onto one `$lib/http.ts` export: `ProblemError { status, code, detail, extensions }` (a generic extension bag — `holderId`, BZ149's hard-lock holder, is now `extensions.holderId`) + `problemFetch(base) => {fetch, fetchVoid}`, a factory each of `chat/api.ts`/`meet/api.ts`/`drive/api.ts` calls once (`meet/api.ts` calls it twice — `BASE`/`PUBLIC_BASE` — since the guest-lobby surface is a second base path). `ChatError`/`MeetError`/`DriveError` are now plain `export const X = ProblemError; export type X = ProblemError;` aliases (the value+type dual-declaration pattern) — every existing `instanceof`/`.status`/`.code`/`.detail` call site keeps working unchanged, since it IS that class, not a copy. The three apps' BZ→i18n conventions (`driveErrorMessage`'s blind `drive.errors.BZ*` lookup, `chatErrorReason`'s deliberately small 2-code map, meet's inline flow-control `e.code===` branches in `actions.svelte.ts`/`store.svelte.ts`/`LobbyPanel.svelte`) were deliberately NOT reconciled — chat's map is a pinned plan decision (bespoke copy for exactly 2 codes, not a convention gap), and meet's branches drive genuinely different ACTIONS per code (toast vs view-transition vs silent no-op), not just different strings, so forcing them into `driveErrorMessage`'s pure code→string shape would fight both a real design decision and a real behavioral difference.
- **Errors**: `$lib/drive/errors.ts` — `driveErrorMessage(e, fallbackKey?)` (arch-hardening C5) is the ONE blind BZ-code→i18n mapping (`drive.errors.BZ*`) every Drive surface now calls, replacing 11 near-identical local copies (3 divergent behaviors: no localization, a narrow per-site allow-list, a wider-but-still-partial allow-list) that had drifted across components.
- **Upload**: `$lib/drive/upload.ts` — `splitManifest` hashes a file in ≤4 MiB chunks (per-chunk: one-shot `crypto.subtle.digest`; whole-file: incremental `@noble/hashes/sha2.js` `sha256.create()`, since `crypto.subtle` cannot stream and a 6 GiB file can never be buffered whole — spec §12-2, Locked resolution R6). `uploadFile` is the idempotent HEAD-then-PUT loop (a chunk that already exists — I-4 proof-of-possession — is skipped) followed by `putVersion`. `$lib/drive/uploadOrchestrator.svelte.ts` (arch-hardening C7, singleton `uploadOrchestrator`) is the single owner of upload-tray bookkeeping (push a progress entry, wire `uploadFile`'s `onProgress`, settle the entry to done/error, map the error via `driveErrorMessage`) that 2 of `DriveApp`'s 5 `uploadFile` call sites each re-implemented near-identically; the other 3 (restore-as-of-version, save-as-branch, restore-as-new-version) never pushed a tray entry and stay direct `uploadFile` calls. `$lib/drive/versionSave.svelte.ts` (arch-hardening C7, `VersionSaveFlow`) is the version-save state machine `DriveApp` used to own inline: the gxp change-note gate (spec §2.1) and the BZ149 save-as-branch offer share one shape (intercept save → collect input via a prompt → resume) so they're one unit; `DriveApp` keeps only the dialog markup + the getters/callbacks for its own concerns.
- **Version manifest contract:** every chunk reaches `putVersion` as lossless `{sha256,size}` data. The reducer validates the size sum and fixed-size boundaries; the typed API and `upload.test.ts` guard prevent browser uploads from silently dropping `size` and turning every non-empty upload into BZ141/HTTP 422.
- **Watches**: `$lib/drive/watches.svelte.ts` (arch-hardening C7, singleton `driveWatches`) is the single reactive cache of the caller's own change-subscriptions (§5.6) — replaces `DriveApp`/`VersionDrawer`/`SubscriptionsSheet` each running a private `api.listWatches()` fetch into its own local cache, which meant toggling a watch in one (e.g. the VersionDrawer bell) never updated the other two until their own next mount/open.
- Tests: `src/routes/drive.test.ts` (space-list ordering + badge, tree a11y, version-drawer conflict badge, quota-bar `aria-valuenow`, BZ148 upload error in the `aria-live="polite"` banner), `src/lib/components/drive/MembersSheet.test.ts` (self-fetch, employee search/select/grant/reload, existing-member filtering, short-query floor, stale-response suppression, search error, close/reopen + space-switch reset, revoke/leave), `src/lib/stores/directory.test.ts` (current-account sentinel resolution, explicit-account preservation, missing-primary failure), `src/lib/drive/upload.test.ts` (chunk-split math, per-chunk/whole-file hash correctness, 0-byte file), `src/lib/i18n/drive-keys.test.ts` (en/de/zh key-parity + source-scan completeness gate).
- **P2 — the shared sync engine + desktop sync-status UI.** `src/lib/drive/sync/` (`types.ts`/`names.ts`/`schema.ts`/`protocol.ts`/`engine.ts`) is a **pure, adapter-pure TS package** consumed by BOTH this webmail's vitest suite (the only place it's exercised in-browser-toolchain) AND the Electron desktop shell's main process (`clients/desktop/electron/src/drive/`, five `../` up, `.js`-suffixed relative imports so both the Vite and esbuild toolchains resolve the same source) — zero Svelte/SvelteKit/Node/Electron imports anywhere in the directory; the only mode-varying seam is the `ContentStore`/`MirrorContentStore` interface FolderMirror (desktop) implements — **P3's `VaultStore` (`sync/vaultstore.ts`) is the second implementation**, see below. `src/lib/drive/mirrorBridge.svelte.ts` — a runes store over `window.setjoinNative.driveMirror` (absent on a plain browser/mobile shell; degrades via its own `DRIVE_MIRROR_VERSION` check, same pattern as `vault`/`VAULT_VERSION`), polling `status()` and owning the auth handoff: mints a drive-scoped bearer token via `POST /api/drive/native-token` (the SPA's own cookie session) and hands it to the Electron main process via `driveMirror.refreshToken` — once at startup and again whenever any space reports `'auth-expired'`. `SyncStatusPill.svelte` (icon+text badge, `aria-live="polite"`, mounted in `DriveApp.svelte`'s toolbar) + `SelectiveSyncSheet.svelte` (per-space mirror toggle, top-level-folder excludes, issue ledger, pause/resume/resync/open-folder — built on the existing `Dialog` primitive, the `MembersSheet.svelte` precedent). i18n: `drive.sync.*` flat keys, en/de/zh, same commit as the UI (per this repo's i18n-in-commit rule). Known gap: no nested `role="tree"` sub-folder exclude picker yet (top-level folders only — the server-side exclude match already prefix-matches, so this stays correct when a deeper picker lands).
- **Drive ACL/access-governance UI and sync (V184–V193).** `NodeAccessDrawer.svelte` self-fetches the exact access projection and is the only grant/inheritance/request interaction owner; presets remain bounded and every mutation is epoch-fenced. `DriveQuickView.svelte`/`SpaceList` expose Shared with me, Shares, and Requests as virtual views without inventing folders. The UI distinguishes path-only ancestors, configured/inherited sources, request-only state, stale impact, and disabled governance in en/de/zh. `sync/engine.ts` keeps content cursor, opaque invalidation cursor, and ACL epoch separate; it consumes revocation first, runs an epoch-consistent full visibility snapshot on unknown/pruned cursors, and removes unauthorized mirror/vault bytes before exposure. `SelectiveSyncSheet` reports quarantine/full-reconcile state. Unit, contract, Electron fake-server, and `drive-acl.visual.spec.ts` pixel tests pin desktop/mobile, light/dark, keyboard, and mismatch behavior.
- **Drive ACL mutation recovery.** Grant/request wrappers send the same UUID in
  the header and body. A 409 never auto-retries a changed mutation: grant and
  revoke retain explicit user intent after refreshing exact access; inheritance
  refreshes access and re-runs the bounded impact preview before confirmation.
- **P3 — the secure-vault ContentStore + in-app viewer (ARCH-017).** `sync/vaultstore.ts`'s `VaultStore` implements `ContentStore` (not the desktop-only `MirrorContentStore`) over `window.setjoinNative.drive` (the offline-core byte plane) + the generic `db` bridge: `fetchChunk`/`pushChunk`/`hasChunk` are thin native calls (a `tls:`-prefixed rejection is re-thrown as `DriveTlsError`, grill G's last hop before a localized hint); `putLocal` fetches only missing chunks (idempotent) and records a `dc_manifests` row (TS-owned metadata — Rust owns only `dc_chunks` bytes, never a raw `DELETE` from here); `pin`/`unpin`/`enforceQuota` implement pin-exempt LRU eviction against the space's device-side `vault_quota_mb` (distinct from the server's `quota_bytes`), evicting via `drive.deleteChunks` — never a direct SQL delete of chunk rows. `sync/nativeToken.ts`'s `getNativeToken()` mints/caches (re-mints ~60s before expiry) the same `POST /api/drive/native-token` bearer P2's `mirrorBridge` mints, via `nativeToken.ts`'s cache, never a second auth path. `src/lib/drive/opener.svelte.ts` is the spec §8.1 `SecureOpener` R4 seam — ONE v1 impl (`inAppRenderer`): reactive state (no imperative "mount a component" convention exists in this codebase — every dialog/sheet here is state-driven from its host), so `DriveApp.svelte` watches `inAppRenderer.current` and renders `SecureViewer.svelte` against it. `SecureViewer.svelte` (`src/lib/components/drive/`) dispatches by content-type: image → RAM-blob `<img>`; SVG **never** via `<img>` (routes through `sanitize.ts` + a `sandbox=""` `srcdoc` iframe instead, reusing `MessageCard.svelte`'s precedent — SVG can carry script/`foreignObject`); pdf / a `ready` office rendition → `<embed type="application/pdf">` (the browser's own PDF viewer — native feature over a pdf.js dependency); html/eml → the same sandboxed iframe; text → escaped `<pre>`; AV → a RAM blob, or (vault-mode AV, or any content over the 512 MiB RAM cap) the native loopback ticket URL (`http://127.0.0.1:<port>/t/<hex>` from `drive.serveStart`/`serveGrant`, revoked on close) — no plaintext temp file either way. Office renditions always re-ask the server (`GET /api/drive/versions/{id}/preview`, parsing a 404 problem's `detail` for the live `status=` word) rather than trusting the viewer's snapshotted `previewStatus` prop, since `getPreview` (Go) is the single source of truth and also owns the lazy `'none'→'pending'` flip. Currently only `{kind:'online'}` sources are wired from the UI (`VaultStore`'s `{kind:'vault'}` path is exercised at the unit level only — no "pin for offline" action exists yet to populate the vault from this screen). i18n: `drive.viewer.*` + `drive.version.view`, en/de/zh. CSP: the gateway header, the SvelteKit meta CSP, and Electron's `SESSION_CSP` all carry the `media-src http://127.0.0.1:*` / `connect-src`+`img-src blob:` / **`object-src 'self' blob:`** delta the viewer needs (`mailserver/internal/middleware/security.go`, `svelte.config.js`, `clients/desktop/electron/src/security/session-lockdown.ts`) — script-src is untouched everywhere. **object-src is the pdf/office `<embed>`'s own gate** (not img-src/media-src — `<object>`/`<embed>`/`<applet>` resource loading is object-src's job specifically), so a bare `'none'` there silently blanked every ready pdf/office preview with no error (B5, 2026-07-11 codebase review, fixed). Tests: `sync/vaultstore.test.ts` (node:sqlite-backed, real SQL), `sync/nativeToken.test.ts`, `components/drive/SecureViewer.test.ts`, `e2e/drive-secure.spec.ts` (I-11(b): blob/loopback-only render + the server secure gate + G3 view-audit dedupe — I-11(a) no-plaintext-on-disk is the Rust marker-scan gate in `offline-core/tests/drive_test.rs`, I-11(c) downloadURL-never-invoked is the Electron `session-lockdown.test.ts` spy).

### Guest G4 Drive view-only behavior

`SecureViewer.svelte` reads the fail-closed session `allowDownload` hint and
combines JMAP guest identity with the independently loaded `/auth/me`
`accountType`, so a failed capability load cannot expose member affordances. A
view-only guest requests only office-PDF preview bytes, always renders
`WatermarkOverlay`, and shows a metadata-only fallback for every other format
without calling raw content. `MessageAttachments.svelte` mirrors that rule: no
image thumbnail or voice/audio raw fetch, office files open the watermarked
viewer, and download/open links remain hidden. Members and download-enabled
guests keep existing behavior; SQL remains authoritative. The Guests admin
profile copy explicitly says watermarking deters casual capture but cannot stop
screenshots or another camera.
The Chat conversations envelope reports `attachmentsEnabled:false` for a guest
claim, so Composer file, drag/drop, paste-image, and voice controls stay absent;
the reducer-backed pre-storage BZ140 remains the malicious-client authority.

---

### Chat (`src/routes/chat/+page.svelte`, A1 Task 13 — shell only)

Capability-gated the same way as mail/calendar/contacts/tasks/drive (the `appForPath` route guard in `+layout.svelte`, `'chat'` in `AppId`/`APP_ORDER`/`APP_PATHS`/`RAIL_APPS`), behind `feature.chat` (DEFAULT OFF, enforced server-side at the reducer/route layer — the client guard here is UX only).

- **`ChatApp.svelte`** (`src/lib/components/chat/`) owns the shell: first-run member empty state (no conversations — Create-channel / New-DM / Browse-channels CTAs), while a guest with zero memberships gets the honest `Waiting for access` empty state with none of those member-creation actions. The same projection applies after a guest has conversations: create/browse/New-DM, Add people, Start call, moderation, broadcast, and channel-management affordances are hidden/disabled; New DM remains unavailable until a server-authoritative `canInitiateDm` projection exists. Guests can still compose in channels the server authorizes. It owns the 2-region desktop layout (`ChannelList` | a main-pane placeholder Tasks 14/15 fill with `MessageList`+`Composer`+`ThreadPane`), mobile stack nav (`ChannelList` self-collapses via its own `cl--conversation-open` class once a channel is active — mirrors `MailSidebar`'s self-managed mobile collapse rather than the parent reaching into the child), and the three member dialogs (create-channel with a public/private `Select` defaulting public; new-DM by numeric user id(s); browse-channels with one-click Join). Member sessions retain `subscribeEvent<ChatWireEvent>('ChatEvent',...)` on the established bidirectional JMAP WebSocket hub. Authenticated guests instead use `$lib/chat/guestEventSource.ts`'s read-only `/api/jmap/eventsource?types=*&ping=30` client: it listens only to named `chat` events, parses object JSON, drops malformed/non-object frames, and closes on component teardown. Its optional `onOpen` callback fires on the initial connection and every native reconnect; ChatApp routes each one to `chatStore.onSocketOpen()` (active-channel replay first, then conversation reconciliation), closing the missed-event gap. Both transports feed the same `chatStore.applyEvent` owner: it serializes per-channel message replay and gives each auth generation one conversation-metadata request in flight plus one dirty-bit trailing rerun, so a burst never scales by event count while an invalidation arriving during an old snapshot is not lost. Reset makes the old cycle and its queued rerun inert; a failed attempt preserves readable state and still performs its already-dirty trailing retry. `federation_removed` applies immediately fail-closed before either request and cannot be erased by stale/failed responses. The store returns only the active-roster refresh intent the view owns. The guest path never asks the server to accept JMAP Request frames on a mailbox-less WebSocket. `store.svelte.test.ts` pins burst bounds, failed-first retry, auth-generation reset and fail-closed revocation; `guestEventSource.test.ts` pins two open events/valid-dispatch/malformed-drop/listener-removal/close; `ChatApp.test.ts` pins the guest-vs-member split plus missed-row backfill and conversation refresh on initial open/reopen.
- **Chat replay cursor + session isolation (Guest G2):** `GET /chat/channels/{id}/messages/since` keeps its established bare `ChatMessage[]` body and adds `X-Setjoin-Chat-Cursor`, the channel high-water mark from the same server snapshot. `$lib/chat/api.ts` lifts body+header into the client-only `ChatReplay` envelope; `ChatStore` keeps the cursor per channel independently of `messagesByChannel` and advances it monotonically even when the response has zero rows because every intervening edit/delete is below a guest's immutable history floor. Subsequent backfills start after that high-water mark; rendered message arrays never receive a cursor placeholder or hidden message id/content/type. Every replay/backfill/reconcile request is issued through one promise queue per channel: the second request cannot reach the server until the first response is applied, so PostgreSQL snapshot order and client application order are identical. Per-invocation generations are deliberately not used because HTTP/2/server scheduling can invert snapshot order; seq cannot break the tie for reaction snapshots that change without bumping the message seq. A separate monotonic **auth-session generation** is mandatory: `reset()` increments it, clears every public/private user slice (conversations/messages/active channel/read/presence/typing/uploads/pending/scheduled/translations/poll caches/space promises/replay queues+cursors), aborts uploads and timers, and makes queued/in-flight old-session continuations inert. A numeric channel reused by the next identity therefore starts at cursor 0. `api.test.ts` pins header parsing/rolling fallback; `store.svelte.test.ts` pins the hidden-gap flow, serialized snapshots, full reset, old-session replay rejection, and cursor-zero reuse.
- **`ChannelList.svelte`** — presentational list (SpaceList's prop shape: `conversations`/`activeChannelId`/`onSelect` + header action callbacks). Resolves a DM/group_dm's title from `chat_channel_roster` (memberIds minus self) and polls `GET /chat/presence` every 60s for the DM counterparts it renders (ponytail: "visible" == "in this list", no virtualization). Unread badge caps at 99+; a mentioned conversation gets the danger palette instead of the default accent pill (distinct styling, not color-alone — same badge shape either way).
