Two `Card`s: **Calendar** — active-default banner (most-specific of user ▸ group ▸ OU ▸ org), the user's own default rows (revoke = `DELETE /api/me/sharing/default/{id}`), and each explicit `calendar_acls` grantee (revoke routes through the existing `revokeShare` `Calendar/shareSet` path); **Mail** — each `mailbox_delegates` delegate with revoke (`DELETE /api/me/sharing/delegates/{userId}`) + the **"Delegate access"** button opening `ShareMailboxDialog` (§6-B, `$lib/components/ShareMailboxDialog.svelte`: skeleton rows · "No one else has access" with the add row still visible · grantee email + permission `Select` over read/send_as/send_on_behalf/full_access/manage_calendar with one-line helpers · grant → `POST /api/me/sharing/delegates` (server forces the caller's primary mailbox) · inline `role=alert` reducer errors · `aria-live` grant/revoke announces; tests `ShareMailboxDialog.test.ts`). §6-D states: skeleton lists (`role=status`), "Only you can see this", load-fail alert + Retry, revoke announces "Access removed" via `aria-live=polite`. Linked from the Mail card on `/settings`. i18n under `sharing.*` (en/de/zh). Tests: `routes/settings/sharing/page.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `/settings/booking`                | `routes/settings/booking/+page.svelte`           | **Self-service booking pages owner config** (feature #6). List + create/edit form over `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/booking/pages` (client `$lib/api/booking.ts`; a 404 on the list call is treated as `BookingFeatureOffError`, not a generic load-fail retry — the admin toggle is off, there's nothing to retry). M1 primitives (Card/Button/Input/Textarea/Switch); weekly availability editor is 7 day-rows of native `<input type="time">` pairs (no picker lib); questions add/remove list; copy-public-link (clipboard + `aria-live` confirmation) and a randomize-slug button. Linked from `/settings`. i18n under `booking.settings.*` (en/de/zh). Tests: `routes/settings/booking/page.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `/book/[slug]`                     | `routes/book/[slug]/+page.svelte`                | **PUBLIC self-service booking page** (feature #6) — setjoin's first unauthenticated route besides login/setup/recover (added to `+layout.svelte`'s auth-guard redirect exemption). Standalone: no app shell, no session/JMAP fetch, only `/api/public/booking/*` (client `$lib/api/publicBooking.ts`). Fetches page meta + a rolling 7-day slot window, renders open slots grouped by the BOOKER's browser-local calendar day (`Intl.DateTimeFormat`) while noting the page's own timezone; picking a slot reveals the details form with a visually-hidden honeypot `website` input (`aria-hidden`, `tabindex="-1"`) present for bots that fill every field blindly. A 409 (slot taken between render and submit) re-fetches slots and announces via `aria-live="assertive"`; a metadata/slot transport failure renders a localized alert plus Retry and hides stale slot controls until the real public endpoints recover; success shows the double-opt-in "check your email" state. An unknown/disabled slug renders a generic not-found state (server-side enumeration parity — see `docs/adr/DES-007-public-booking-surface.md` — is what actually matters; the client just reflects whatever the server returned). i18n under `booking.public.*`. Tests: `routes/book/booking.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `/book/confirm`                    | `routes/book/confirm/+page.svelte`               | **PUBLIC email-verification + cancel landing** (feature #6). `?token=` completes the double opt-in (server creates the calendar event + sends the iTIP invite); `?cancel=` cancels an already-confirmed booking. Renders confirming/confirmed/cancelled/invalid-token states; the raw one-time cancel token (only ever seen via the confirm response body) renders as a client-side "Cancel this booking" action on the confirmed state. Standalone, no app shell. Tests: `routes/book/booking.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `/security-event`                  | `routes/security-event/+page.svelte`             | **Offline #2b remote-wipe landing screen.** Reached via `?reason=remote-wipe` after `installWipeHandler` completes the wipe-ack cycle. Reassure-don't-alarm copy: explains what happened and invites the user to sign in again from any device. Distinct from ordinary logout — no session-expired copy. No auth required; page is fully static (no API calls). Tests: `routes/security-event/page.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `/blocked`                         | `routes/blocked/+page.svelte`                    | **Dual-purpose blocked screen (CA-2 + CA-3b).** When rendered without a `block` prop: CA-2 device-lockdown wall — `blockedCopy()` (`$lib/blocked.ts`) drives native vs browser copy/actions; native shell shows "Request approval" (POSTs `/device/self-enroll` with `attachDeviceProofHeaders`, phase machine idle→requested→error) + "Check again" (`/.well-known/jmap` liveness probe); plain browser shows "Install the setjoin app". Correlation ref chip (`?ref=` query param). Focus moves to `<h1>` on mount for screen-reader announce. When rendered with a `block: PolicyBlock` prop (CA-3b): three modes driven by `policyMode` — `hard` ("Access blocked by policy" + Contact IT + reason callout + copyable correlation-ID chip + "Sign in as different user"); `step_up` ("Additional verification needed" + "Verify now" button); `report_only` (dismissible `role="status"` info banner, user continues). Unknown mode falls back to `hard`. `contactEmail` resolved from `$jmapSession.helpEmail` or `admin@<org-domain>` fallback (never `window.location.hostname`). No auth required for either mode. `aria-live` + `prefers-reduced-motion` guards. Helper: `$lib/blocked.ts` `blockedCopy(reason, mode, native) → {titleKey, bodyKey, actions[]}` — pure testable seam; `blocked.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

---

### Admin console (`src/routes/admin/`)

Two-layer access control: (1) rail icon renders only if the user holds the console's permissions; (2) every admin API call is guarded by `RequirePerm` on the gateway — hiding the icon is never the security boundary.

Four RBAC-gated console groups (permission→view matrix verified in `console_matrix_test.go` and `matrix.test.ts`). Console IDs match Go `admin.ConsoleID` constants. The canonical nav assignment lives in `src/lib/admin/consoles.ts` (mirrors Go `admin.ConsoleScopes` in `mailserver/internal/admin/consoles.go`). `consoles.test.ts` also requires every console/nav `labelKey` to exist in en/zh/de, so the sidebar cannot silently render a raw translation key.

The Identity console uses `IdentityModelConsole.svelte` with dedicated
`identity/{sources,people,conflicts,transitions,audit}` list/detail routes. A
detail route loads only its owning inventory and stable ID. The cross-domain
overview fails closed if any required normalized evidence inventory is
unavailable, so partial evidence cannot be mistaken for a zero-result success;
the owning section offers a deterministic retry. Identity-v2 client calls opt
into the RFC 7807 body and preserve safe `code`, `remediation`, and `retryable`
evidence in the operator error instead of discarding the BZ next action.

**Mail admin (scope `domains.read|queue.read|reports.read`) — `admin/(mail)/`:**

| Chat compliance | `admin/(access)/chat-compliance/+page.svelte` | **Chat records-lifecycle + oversight console** (Chat A4a Task 12 + A4b Tasks 9/10; nav item `perm: 'chat.compliance_view'`, manage affordances gated `chat.compliance_manage`, eDiscovery tab gated `chat.ediscovery` — the backend re-enforces all three, `internal/admin/admin_chat_compliance.go` Tasks 11a/11b + `admin_chat_ediscovery.go` Tasks 5/6/7). Five tabs (three A4a + two A4b): **Overview** — one workerPool round-trip (`GET /admin/chat-compliance/overview`): channels under legal hold (with the matter), gxp/WORM channel count, per-channel-override count, the last `chat.purge_run` (totals + **`drained`** — a stale/`false` last run is how a silently-failed nightly cron becomes visible), next scheduled run, and a read-only **Preview impact** (`GET /admin/chat-purge/preview`). **Retention policies** — `chat_retention_policies` CRUD (`RetentionPolicyEditor.svelte`): the 7-day `effective_at` grace is surfaced ("takes effect on «date»"); an edit that can only GROW the destroyed set shows a **before-save re-arm warning** (client-side cheap early-out mirroring the server's authoritative N4/FIX-3 `exposesAnyChannel` check); **Save is gated on having fetched the impact preview**, and the final save goes through `ConfirmDestructive` (typed `DESTROY`) stating the TOTAL backlog and the nightly-drain rate — never a one-night implication (N7); `DeletePolicy`'s `409 {code:'exposure', exposedChannels}` (FIX 3) reopens the same typed-DESTROY confirm and retries with `?confirm=DESTROY`. **Channels** — the participant-projecting `GET /admin/chat-channels?q=&userId=` list (a DM/group-DM row renders "Alice ↔ Bob" from `participants`, never a blank name cell; the hold column reflects the two-arm `chat_hold_active` predicate since A4b Task 8, so a case-held channel shows held with no boolean set); the `?userId=` deep-link's custodian affordance now routes to the **Holds tab's case-hold flow** (the A4a snapshot-loop `POST /admin/chat-holds/custodian` route was deleted in A4b Task 5); row click opens `ChannelComplianceSheet.svelte` (classification upgrade to gxp — typed-confirm, no downgrade ever offered; per-channel retention override, immediate-by-design, a **shortening** edit gated by `ConfirmDestructive`, a lengthening/clearing edit is not; legal hold set/release — release requires the **matching matter** pre-filled from `legal_hold_reason`, `ConfirmDestructive`-gated, disabled if cleared; members; the audit pane — recent acts, **Verify chain** → `verdict.ts`'s `computeChatVerdict` renders **exactly three outcomes** (✓ PASS, incl. a neutral "reconciled from the purge record" sub-line; ✗ CHAIN BROKEN — the tail-truncation sub-case renders `liveHead`/`notarizedHead`; ✗ RECORD ALTERED) — **there is no fourth ⚠/"probably fine" state**; **Download evidence** streams the CSV, `GET …/audit?format=csv`). **Holds** (A4b Task 9, `CaseHoldsPanel.svelte`) — durable case/custodian holds over the mail-owned `ediscovery_cases` registry (case picker via `GET /admin/chat-ediscovery/cases`; that route exists on the chat surface because mail's own cases route is `requireMailPerm`-gated): set a custodian- or channel-scoped hold (`POST /admin/chat-ediscovery/holds`, exactly-one-scope form), list active/released holds, release (`POST …/holds/{id}/release`, reason required) — manage affordances `chat.compliance_manage`-gated. **eDiscovery** (A4b Task 10, `EDiscoveryPanel.svelte`, rendered only with `chat.ediscovery` — the D-A SoD split) — case-scoped search (`POST /admin/chat-ediscovery/search`; hits carry deleted/revision/filename/scheduled badges + provenance) → channel reader (`GET …/channels/{id}/messages`) with the **record-floor banner** (`admin.chatCompliance.ediscovery.recordFloorDisclaimer`, the L8 sentence verbatim — the record begins above a purge floor; no negative claims) → export (`GET …/export`, streamed ZIP download). **Export deliberately has NO `ConfirmDestructive`** — an export destroys nothing; typed-DESTROY is reserved for destructive acts, and diluting it on a read would train admins past it. Classification badge and the retention-days label are never rendered raw: `admin.chatCompliance.class.*` / the pinned **destroy-ceiling** (regular) vs **WORM-floor** (gxp) copy pair (`channels.ts`'s `retentionLabel`/`channelDisplayName` helpers). `ConfirmDestructive` (new M1 primitive, `$lib/components/ui/`) is reused across all five confirms above. Client: the "Chat compliance admin" + "Chat eDiscovery admin" sections of `$lib/admin/client.ts`. i18n `admin.chatCompliance.*` incl. `.holds.*`/`.ediscovery.*` (en/de/zh, `chat-compliance-keys.test.ts`). Perm rows in `capabilityCatalog.ts` (catalog now 27 — `chat.ediscovery` added); nav row in `consoles.ts` stays gated `chat.compliance_view` (single-string `perm` field — an eDiscovery officer is granted `chat.compliance_view` alongside, the disclosed D-A decision). Tests: `chat-compliance.test.ts`. |

**RoleMembersPanel** (`src/lib/components/admin/RoleMembersPanel.svelte`):

- **Protection gating**: per-member `manageable?: boolean` flag from the role-members endpoint (`GET /admin/roles/{id}/members`) disables the Remove button and renders the same focusable `<span tabindex="0" class="sj-protection-indicator">` + `<Badge label={t('admin.protection.badge_protected')} />` inside a `<Tooltip>` (same a11y pattern as the Users page). `manageable === false` (the explicit false check, not just falsy) is the gate — an absent flag defaults to manageable for backwards compat. Test: `src/lib/components/admin/RoleMembersPanel.test.ts` (enable button, disable button, badge presence, badge absence, focusable indicator `tabindex=0`, `aria-describedby` a11y guard, `canEdit=false` suppresses the button). **i18n keys** (`admin.protection.*` in en/de/zh): `badge_protected` ("Protected" / "Geschützt" / "受保护"), `badge_self` ("You" / "Sie" / "本人"), `reason_protected` (tooltip copy for an outranking admin row), `reason_self` (tooltip copy for the caller's own row). Key parity enforced by `index.test.ts`.
- **Single-assign scope picker (scope-set wave)**: the single-assign dialog now has a **3-radio scope picker**: "Whole org" (default, null scope) / "Limit to a department" (OU scope — loads `DepartmentTreePicker`) / "Use a scope-set" (set scope — loads a `<select>` populated from `adminListScopeSets()`). The selected `{scopeType:'ou'|'set', scopeId}` or null is threaded into `POST /admin/roles/{id}/members`. Members in the scope column display the set name (from `setName` in the API response) or the department name (from `deptMap`).
- **Bulk add (P3)**: multi-user typeahead picker renders an "Add N members" `Button` once ≥1 user is selected. The dialog includes a `DepartmentTreePicker` (same two-tier radio pattern as single-assign): "Whole domain" (default, null scope) or "Limit to a department" → the selected `{scopeType:'ou', scopeId}` is threaded into the `POST /admin/roles/{id}/members:bulk` body as a uniform OU scope applied to every target. Submit receives `{ jobId }` (202). Panel switches to a poll loop (`GET /admin/jobs/bulk_role/{jobId}` every 2 s). On `status='done'` the per-user results JSON renders inline: columns email / result ∈ assigned/updated/blocked, and for blocked rows: reason chip ∈ sod_conflict/domination/self/last_super_admin. A "View in Jobs console" link points to `/admin/background-jobs`. Enqueue errors (400/403) surface via `showToast`.
- **Bulk remove (P3)**: multi-select checkboxes on each member row enable a "Remove selected (N)" `Button`. Submit hits `DELETE /admin/roles/{id}/members:bulk` → same 202 + `jobId` poll + report table flow as bulk add. Protected rows (manageable=false) disable their checkbox and are excluded from the count.
- **i18n keys** (`admin.roles.bulk.*` in en/de/zh): `add_members` / `remove_selected` / `processing` / `view_jobs` / `result_assigned` / `result_updated` / `result_blocked` / `reason_sod_conflict` / `reason_domination` / `reason_self` / `reason_last_super`.

**Access Reviews — `roles/+page.svelte` Reviews tab** (`src/lib/components/admin/AccessReviewsPanel.svelte`, `AccessReviewCampaign.svelte`, `src/lib/admin/accessReviews.ts`):

- **`AccessReviewsPanel.svelte`** — Reviews tab body. Lists campaigns (name, status badge, item counts `reviewed/total`, created-by, timestamps) in an M1 Table; "Start Review" `Button` opens an inline form (name Input + domain-scope selector); selecting a row sets `bind:campaignId` to drill into `AccessReviewCampaign`. Loading/error/empty states. i18n keys `admin.access_reviews.*`.
- **`AccessReviewCampaign.svelte`** — Campaign detail panel. Shows campaign metadata + an items table (principal, role, scope, `is_sso` badge, decision radio keep/revoke, note Input, decided-by/at). Bulk-decide all undecided → `PUT /admin/access-reviews/{id}/items`. Per-item decide → `PUT /admin/access-reviews/{id}/items/{itemId}`. "Close & Apply" → `POST /admin/access-reviews/{id}/close` (Dialog confirm before the destructive apply step). States: `undecided`/`keep`/`revoke` with distinct M1 Badge variants; `applied_at` shows when a revoke was actually applied (vs skipped due to SSO). Full a11y: table cells use `role="radiogroup"` for decision pairs, focus-managed close Dialog. Tests: `AccessReviewsPanel.test.ts`, `AccessReviewCampaign.test.ts`.
- **`src/lib/admin/accessReviews.ts`** — Typed REST client: `listReviews()`, `getReview(id)`, `startReview(name, domainScope?)`, `decideItem(campaignId, itemId, decision, note?)`, `decideBulk(campaignId, decision)`, `closeReview(campaignId)` → `AccessReviewCampaign` / `AccessReviewItem` / `BulkResult` types (`reviewed`/`total`/`isSso`/`isSelf`/`appliedAt`).
- **i18n**: `admin.access_reviews.*` keys in en/de/zh (`tab`, `start`, `close`, `campaign_name`, `status.*`, `decision.*`, `applied_at`, `not_applied`, `bulk_decide`, `confirm_close` et al.).

**JIT activation (P3 — eligible-vs-active, time-boxed admin roles):**

- **Exchange replacement console** (`src/routes/admin/(mail)/exchange-replacement/+page.svelte`) is one unified six-view surface—overview, immutable runs, 31 registry outcomes, per-capability mappings, evidence documents and settings/deletion. Every mutation is gated on its exact `exchange.assessment.*` permission and fresh server authority; unknown/partial/stale evidence stays visible, replacement-ready remains OFF, and publication asks only for the projection digest because the server creates/encrypts/signs the receipt. `e2e/exchange-replacement.spec.ts` runs against a real static production build and covers full-permission and read-only Chinese-mobile journeys.

- **My-roles surface** (`src/routes/admin/(access)/my-roles/+page.svelte`, nav id `admin.jit.my_roles_title` in the **access** console). Self-service: lists the caller's roles from `adminMyRoles()` (`GET /admin/me/roles`), each a **dot+icon+label** status — Standing (indigo, `ShieldCheck`) / Eligible·dormant (grey, `Moon`, with a concept `Tooltip`) / Active·N-min-left (green, `Clock`) / Active·≤5-min (amber, `AlertTriangle`). Live 1 s countdown drives the status flip; a polite `aria-live` region announces the soonest-expiring window; an expiry **one-time toast** fires when a window lapses. Per-state action: Activate / Deactivate / Extend. Reachable by any authenticated user (the backend route is `requireAuth`-only) — the admin layout guard (`admin/+layout.svelte`) never bounces `/admin/my-roles` and routes **dormant admins** (`$user.isAdmin` + zero live `$adminConsoles`, **D10**) here instead of `/apps`, where a calm "no active admin powers" banner + "Activate a role" CTA replaces a shown-then-403 console. Status logic is pure + unit-tested in `src/lib/admin/jitStatus.ts` (`roleStatus`/`remainingMinutes`/`splitWindow`/`DURATION_PRESETS`, `jitStatus.test.ts`). Render test: `my-roles/page.test.ts`.
- **`ActivateRoleDialog.svelte`** — segmented **window-length** control (presets `DURATION_PRESETS`) capped at the role's policy max (over-max buttons are `aria-disabled` + a keyboard-reachable reason `Tooltip`, never `disabled`); **Reason** textarea required-marked iff `policy.requireJustification` (validated on submit, focus kept, inline `role="alert"` error carrying the BZ104/BZ105 problem `detail`); submit spinner. **Cold-start (D6):** a `tokenRefreshRequired:true` response triggers `refreshSession()` (POST `/auth/refresh` → `checkAuth`, in `$lib/stores/auth`) with a visible "Refreshing your session…" step before `onActivated`. `refreshSession` is an injectable prop (mocked in tests). Test: `ActivateRoleDialog.test.ts` (over-max disabled+reason; required-justification blocks activate; tokenRefreshRequired triggers refresh).
- **Grant eligible/active toggle** (`RoleMembersPanel`, §7E): when the `rbac.jit` feature toggle is on (read once via `adminFeatureList()`), the single-assign dialog shows an **Active (standing)** vs **Eligible (JIT)** radio (tooltip on Eligible) → sends `eligible:bool` in `adminAssignMember`. Off → hidden (standing-only, today's UX). Bulk-assign stays standing-only.
- **Per-role policy editor** (`RoleActivationPolicyEditor.svelte`, §7F): embedded in `RoleMembersPanel` when `rbac.jit` on + `canEdit`; max-window `Select` + require-justification `Switch`, with the org default shown inline as the inherited fallback. `adminGetActivationPolicy` / `adminPutActivationPolicy` (`/admin/roles/{id}/activation-policy`).
- **JIT settings tab** (`JitSettingsPanel.svelte`, §7G; a `roles.manage`-gated **JIT activation** tab on `roles/+page.svelte`): the `rbac.jit` `Switch` with a **confirm-on-enable** Dialog + flip note (ON instantly dormants every eligible admin; OFF is the strict no-op) + the org-default editor (`adminGetJitDefaults` / `adminPutJitDefaults`, `/admin/system/jit-defaults`).
- **API client** (`$lib/admin/client.ts`): `adminMyRoles`, `adminActivateRole`, `adminDeactivateRole`, `adminGetActivationPolicy`, `adminPutActivationPolicy`, `adminGetJitDefaults`, `adminPutJitDefaults` + the `MyRole`/`ActivateResult`/`ActivationPolicy`/`JitDefaults` types; `adminAssignMember` (`roles.ts`) gained an optional `eligible` flag.
- **i18n**: `admin.jit.*` keys in en/de/zh (status labels, durations, dialog, grant toggle, policy editor, settings/toggle/defaults).

"Lowering" (full→lean or removing a required-reason area) opens a `ReasonPrompt`confirm dialog requiring a non-empty reason before`PUT` is called (`reason` field in body). |
| Alerts    | `alerts/+page.svelte`   | Active alerts (admin-kit §5.2): Card flush rows with severity Badge+dot, alert text, mono time, Acknowledge ghost button + Undo toast                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Auth log  | `auth-log/+page.svelte` | Authentication event log viewer (M1 Card flush + Table: Time/Email/Event/Provider/IP)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

**Admin layout:** `admin/+layout.svelte` — M2-derived app rail, four-console sidebar (SidebarItem), content column ≤980px, mobile hamburger drawer, RBAC route guard, Assist toggle (gated on `effectiveCapabilities.features.assist`). |

---

### Shared / library (`src/lib/`)

**JMAP client (`src/lib/jmap/`):**

- `client.ts`: `jmapCall`, `loadSession`, `mailboxGet`, `emailQuery`, `emailGet`, `searchSnippetGet(filter, ids)` (`SearchSnippet/get` — per-match `{emailId, subject, preview}` where subject/preview are ParadeDB BM25 HTML with `<mark>` highlights; default core+mail caps, same as `emailGet`), `threadGet`, `calendarEventGet`, `contactQuery`, `galSearch`, `uploadBlob` (one-shot hash+PUT, e.g. avatar upload — internally `sha256Hex(file)` + `putBlob(blobId, file)`; **`sha256Hex`/`putBlob` are exported separately (WAVE-5 #3)** so compose's upload UI can surface the two phases: the whole-file digest as an indeterminate "preparing" state, the `PUT /api/blobs/{sha}` as a real-progress "uploading" state via `XMLHttpRequest` — `fetch` can't report upload progress and has no native abort-via-signal for the request body — `putBlob` takes `{onProgress, signal}`), `emailSubmissionCancel`, `emailSubmissionGet`, `setMessageFocus(id, focused)` (Focused/Other split, V078 — `POST /messages/{id}/focus` via `httpRequest`, mirrors recall; the gateway also learns a per-sender override), `getAccountId`, `findFreeSlots`, `identities` load, `assistComplete(messages, locale?)` (B4 `POST /assist/complete` with `credentials: 'include'`; returns `data.content`, throws parsed `detail` on non-2xx). **Contact groups (A4):** `contactGroupGet(ids?)`, `contactGroupQuery(filter?)`, `contactGroupSet(create, update, destroy, members?)`, `contactGroupExpand(groupId)` — thin `ContactGroup/{get,query,set,expand}` wrappers (`CONTACT_CAPS`); `ContactGroup`/`ContactGroupMember`/`ExpandedRecipient` types in `$lib/types/jmap.ts`. **Find & merge duplicates:** `contactFindDuplicates()` (`ContactCard/findDuplicates`, `{accountId}`) returns `DuplicateCluster[]` (`{email, contactIds: string[]}`) — the reducer returns a **bare array** (not a `{list}` envelope) with `contactIds` as JSON numbers, which the client stringifies; `contactMerge(keepId, dropId)` (`ContactCard/merge`) unions drop into keep, `assertOk`-guarded. **Calendar ICS import:** `calendarEventImport(calendarId, ics, strategy)` (`CalendarEvent/import`, `CAL_CAPS`) → `{imported, skipped}`; `reminderGet`/`reminderDismiss`/`reminderSnooze` (`Reminder/{get,dismiss,snooze}`, `REMINDER_CAPS`) back the reminders window.
- `offline-client.ts`: `emailSet`, `emailSubmissionSet` — IndexedDB-queued offline wrappers.
- `offline/eligibility.ts`, `offline/scheduler.ts`, `offline/statusProjection.ts`
  (connectivity E2): pure, side-effect-free admission and status model plus the
  bounded optional-work scheduler. Eligibility intersects server policy, device
  preference, live network/power/app state, and work class; scheduler tests pin
  priority/deadline, coalescing, fairness, retry/backoff, cancellation, and
  deterministic ordering. Status projection exposes only calm product state and
  redacted reason codes—never SSID, carrier, IP, URL, message, or filename.
- `quicksteps.ts` (A2): `listQuickSteps()`, `saveQuickStep({id?,name,icon,actions})`, `deleteQuickStep(id)`, `applyQuickStep(id, messageId)` — thin `QuickStep/{get,set,apply}` wrappers; resolve `accountId` via `getAccountId()`; surface the gateway's method-level error envelope (name "error" `{type, description}`) as a thrown `Error`. The action allowlist (move_to/categorize/flag/mark_read/forward_to) + message ownership are enforced server-side in the reducer.
- `websocket.ts`: **always-on, self-arming multi-listener push hub** — `subscribe(types, cb) => unsubscribe` over one process-global socket (`disconnect()` is explicit teardown only, e.g. logout; NOT ref-counted). The first `subscribe()` opens the socket (call-order irrelevant); each incoming `StateChange` is fanned out to every registered listener whose declared dataTypes intersect the changed type-map, each inside its own try/catch (a throwing listener never aborts the fan-out). The producer emits a JMAP-correct, accountId-keyed `StateChange` (`changed: { <accountId>: { Email, Mailbox, Calendar, Contact, Task, … } }`); the hub **unwraps one level** via `unwrapTypeMap` so listeners receive the per-account **type map** (`changes.Email`, `changes.Calendar`, `changes.Contact`, `changes.Task`) — reading type keys off the accountId wrapper is the historical dead-push bug (guarded by `websocket.test.ts`). Advertised `dataTypes` are the **union** of every live listener's types (re-advertised on reconnect); advisory only (the gateway pushes all state regardless). **Reconnect resync (#222):** `subscribe(types, cb, { onReconnect })` — `onReconnect` fires (error-isolated) on every socket RE-open after a drop, never on the first open (`disconnect()` resets it so a fresh login is a first connect); StateChanges pushed while the socket was down are lost, so state-caching listeners resync from their last known state there. **Control-channel (offline #2b): `subscribeControl(type, handler) => unsubscribe`** — separate subscription table for raw control payloads (`'offline.wipe'`, `'capsChanged'`); `__handleRawMessage(raw)` (exported for tests) parses incoming WS frames and dispatches to control subscribers when the JSON `"type"` field is a known control type, otherwise to the JMAP state hub. Control payloads bypass the accountId-unwrap path and are delivered verbatim. Consumers: `inbox/+page.svelte` via `wipe.ts` `installWipeHandler`/`installCapsChangedHandler`. Consumers: `inbox/+page.svelte` (`subscribe(['Email','Mailbox'])`), `CalendarApp.svelte` (`subscribe(['Calendar'])` → `changes.Calendar || changes.CalendarEvent`), `ContactsApp.svelte` (`subscribe(['Contact'])` → `refreshContacts`), `TasksApp.svelte` (`subscribe(['Task'])` → `refreshTasks`) — each unsubscribes in `onDestroy` (D2). **`@type`-keyed pushes (non-StateChange server frames, `{"@type":<T>,"event":{...}}`): `subscribeType(atType, handler) => unsubscribe`** hands the raw envelope; **`subscribeEvent<T>(atType, handler) => unsubscribe`** (arch review #A2) is the typed sibling that unwraps `payload.event` ONCE at the hub before fan-out, dropping a missing/non-object `event` there instead of at each consumer — this is the seam a raw-envelope-cast-as-event bug shipped through once (`MeetApp.svelte`'s `MeetEvent` handler). Member ChatApp sessions and every other WebSocket consumer use `subscribeEvent`, not the raw `subscribeType`: `MeetChatPanel.svelte`/member `ChatApp.svelte` (`ChatEvent`), `MeetApp.svelte` (`MeetEvent`), Drive consumers (`DriveWatch`/`DriveAlarm`). Mailbox-less guest ChatApp sessions are the deliberate exception and use the read-only named-event client documented in the Chat section above.

**Stores (`src/lib/stores/`):**

- **Auth-session teardown:** `auth.ts` is the authoritative boundary for logout, explicit `reset()`, authenticated→unauthenticated loss, and `/auth/me` identity/impersonation changes. Each path calls the existing `websocket.disconnect()` and every callback registered through `registerAuthSessionTeardown`; `ChatStore` self-registers its complete reset, avoiding an auth→feature-store import cycle. `auth.test.ts` pins logout disconnect, registered reset teardown, and identity-switch teardown.
- `auth.ts`: token store, `login(email, password, rememberDevice?)`, `loginWithCaptcha`, `logout`, authenticated `setupTOTP`/`confirmTOTP`, pending-token `setupTOTPEnrollment`/`confirmTOTPEnrollment`, `verifyTOTP`, `startWebAuthnLogin`, `fetchPasskeyCredentials`, `base64urlToBuffer`/`bufferToBase64url` (WebAuthn buffer/base64url conversion, reused by settings passkey enrollment), `ForcePasswordChangeError`, `MFARequiredError`, `MFAEnrollmentRequiredError`, `ThreatBlockedError`, `impersonateUser`, `refreshSession` (POST `/auth/refresh` then `checkAuth` — re-mints the token + reloads live caps; used by JIT cold-start activation, **D6**), `adminConsoles`, `adminPermissions` + `hasPermission(perms, perm)`. `MFAEnrollmentRequiredError` carries the pending token; setup maps `{provisioning_uri}` to `{secret, provisioningUri}` for both login and settings. `adminConsoles`/`adminPermissions` are derived from the `/jmap/session` `urn:setjoin:params:jmap:mail` → `admin: {consoles, permissions}` block; admin write controls gate per-permission via `disabled={!hasPermission($adminPermissions, '<perm>')}` — this replaced the retired always-false `isReadOnly` stub so a read-tier admin sees disabled controls, not buttons that 403 (server still enforces). **Wired across all ~27 admin pages** (and the AppAccessEditor / IPRulesDialog / SodEnablementPanel child dialogs, which gate their own write controls on the action's perm), each on the exact perm its route requires — e.g. users delete→`users.delete` / impersonate→`users.impersonate` / suspend+edit→`users.suspend`, domains→`domains.write`, quarantine→`security.write`, retention/ediscovery→`compliance.write`, identity/conditional-access/EAP→`identity.write`, mam→`mam.write`, system→`system.config`/`system.upgrade`/`backup.write`. Console-access-only routes (S/MIME trust, dry-run preview/test) and read/export controls are intentionally left enabled. `login()` sends `remember_device` in POST body; parses threat responses (403 captcha/blocked/geo_blocked/spray_detected/lockout → `ThreatBlockedError`; 429 rate_limited/tarpit → `ThreatBlockedError`; 401 problem JSON detail for error message). `AuthUser` includes `totp_enabled?: boolean` and `totp_enabled_at?: string`. Possession scheme v7 (§3.1): the reauth handoff is METHOD-AWARE — gated arms answering 403 `reauth_required` open the shared `MfaReauthHandoff.svelte` (both settings pages), which offers the account's ENROLLED proofs: C1 proves with WebAuthn-UV (`provePossessionWebAuthn` → `/auth/mfa/possession/webauthn/begin|finish`, UV requested, assertion + `operation` binding) or a current TOTP code (`possessionTOTPProof` → `/auth/mfa/possession/totp`); the password challenge (`beginMFAReauth(password, operation, targetRef?)` → `/auth/mfa/reauth`) appears only for the C2/C3 cohorts, and every issuance declares the §1.3 operation (plus `target_ref` iff `webauthn.revoke`). Passkey registration moved into `enrollPasskey(reauthToken?)` (grant rides the `X-MFA-Reauth` header on begin and the body member on finish; `reauth_required` surfaces as `ReauthRequiredError`). `setupTOTP` returns the setup-issued `enrollmentId`; `confirmTOTP(code, {reauthToken, enrollmentId})` presents it back. `fetchPasskeyCredentials` passes through the optional `uv_capable` member (absent ⇒ never offer the WebAuthn-UV proof). Auth-generation fencing: both settings pages drop pending ceremony/grant state and refuse late responses whenever `checkAuth` observes a changed account identity — a UX fence only; the server's tuple binding enforces.
- `session.ts`: `jmapSession` (writable, raw session response), `effectiveCaps` (derived — maps server `{apps,features}` payload to enabled apps plus non-app flags), `isGuestSession`, `firstEnabledApp`, `loadSessionStore()`. Guest policy maps `allowMeetCreate`, `allowMeetHost`, and G4 `allowDownload` only from explicit `=== true`; absent session/toggle/profile data fails closed. `guestInvite` remains the AND with `guestAccounts`, and guest `apps.drive` remains false because attachment preview is not a standalone Drive app. Types live in `$lib/types/jmap.ts`.
- `compose.ts`: `composeStore` (open/close/minimize/maximize/toggle) + `ComposeOpts` type.
- `calendar.ts`: calendars, events, selectedDate, selectedCalendarIds, viewMode, visibleCalendarIds, all CRUD + rsvp + findFreeSlots + loadIdentities + `importEvents(calendarId, ics, strategy)` (ICS file import → `calendarEventImport`).
- `contacts.ts`: addressBooks, contacts, selectedAddressBookId, galResults, viewMode, searchText, isGAL, all CRUD + searchGAL. **Duplicates:** `duplicates` + `duplicateContacts` (by-id map) writables; `findDuplicates()` (detect clusters → fetch involved contacts for the keep/drop UI) and `merge(keepId, dropId)` (client merge → reload selected book → re-detect). Tested in `contacts.test.ts`.
- `contactGroups.ts` (A4): `contactGroups` writable + `loadGroups` (query→get), `createGroup(name)`, `renameGroup(id,name)`, `deleteGroup(id)`, `setMembers(groupId, add, remove)`, `expandGroup(id)`, `resetGroups` — each delegating to the `ContactGroup/*` client helpers. Personal (per-mailbox) contact groups; consumed by `ContactsApp`/`ContactGroupModal` and the compose recipient autocomplete.
- `tasks.ts`: tasks, viewMode, searchText, statusFilter, all CRUD + groupByStatus + priorityLabel + priorityColor + statusLabel + formatDateShort + isOverdue.
- `directory.ts`: directoryTree, directoryMembers, directorySearch, setDirectoryAccountId. The current-account `-1` sentinel resolves client-side through `getAccountId()` before JMAP; the server deliberately rejects negative account IDs. Explicit address-book account IDs are preserved.
- `tagHelpers.ts`: `LOCATION_ROLES`, `stripLocationTags`, `userTags`, `buildTagTree`, `snoozePresets`.
- `inboxStore.svelte.ts`: `InboxStore` ($state runes class) — the inbox's reactive state owner (~23 vars + `$derived`counts/selectedRole/flatForView/effectiveViewMode/threadList +`loadEmails`/`selectView`/`selectTag`/`buildThreads`+ the Email/Mailbox push sub via`subscribeToChanges()`/`teardown()`). Instantiated once by `routes/inbox/+page.svelte`; the page keeps the JMAP mutations + DOM glue.
- `inboxModel.ts`: `computeListFilter`, `resolveViewMailbox`, `selectionFromUrl`, `computeCounts`, `replyAllRecipients`, `isMonoBody` (machine-mail detection: `Email.mono` flag or monitoring/CI/no-reply sender heuristic) — pure filter/semantics helpers consumed by `InboxStore`.
- `searchQuery.ts`: `parseSearch` — `tag:`/`from:`/bare-text search-operator parser.
- `scheduling.svelte.ts`: scheduling assistant reactive state.
- `reminders.svelte.ts` (A6): `RemindersStore` ($state runes class) — `load`/`dismiss`/`dismissAll`/`snooze` (optimistic) + `onPush` (re-fetch on a `reminder_fired`/`reminder_changed` push). `presetToISO(preset)` resolves a `5m`/`1h`/`tomorrow` snooze preset. Backed by `Reminder/{get,dismiss,snooze}` (`$lib/jmap/client` `reminderGet`/`reminderDismiss`/`reminderSnooze`).

**Navigation config (`src/lib/nav/navConfig.ts`):**

- `AdminConsoleId` type (`mail | system | logs | auth`), `NavApp` and `NavAdmin` interfaces, `RAIL_APPS` and `RAIL_ADMIN` constant arrays. Single source of truth for all nav item definitions; imported by `AppRail.svelte` and `GlobalDrawer.svelte`. Extract here instead of duplicating in each component.

**Admin console model (`src/lib/admin/consoles.ts`):**

- `ConsoleId` type, `ConsoleNavItem`/`ConsoleDef` interfaces, `CONSOLES` constant array (four consoles with all nav items — system console includes the `offline` nav item added in offline #2a), `navForConsoles(ids)` filter helper. Mirrors the Go `admin.ConsoleID` values. Single source of truth for the sidebar, rail icons, and route guards.

**Admin route guard (`src/lib/admin/guard.ts`):**

- `consoleForPath(pathname)` — longest-prefix match resolving any `/admin/...` path to a `ConsoleId` (null if unknown). `allowedPath(pathname, consoles)` — true when the path's console is in the user's set; unknown paths default to allowed (no false lockout). `firstConsolePath(consoles)` — first navigable path for a console set. UX-only layer-1 guard; the real security boundary is the server-side 403.
- **JIT dormant-admin gate (D10):** the layout guard (`admin/+layout.svelte`) special-cases `/admin/my-roles` as always-reachable (self-service, `requireAuth`-only) and routes a **dormant admin** — `$user.isAdmin` true but zero live `$adminConsoles` — to it (not `/apps`), so they land on the activate surface, never a shown-then-403 console. Console entry stays gated on **live** capabilities, never `is_admin`.

**Threat model (`src/lib/admin/threat.ts`):**

- `ThreatFlow` type (admin-kit §7 fixture shape: id, time, src, country, dst, service, risk, rule, action). `riskBars(level)` — returns 1–3 filled bars for low/medium/high. `actionBadgeVariant(action)` — maps Block/Tarpit/CAPTCHA/Allow to M1 Badge variant names. `parseThreatResponse(data)` — maps backend-wave2's `{rows,total,limit,offset}` envelope to typed `ThreatFlow[]`. `ThreatApiResponse`, `ParsedThreatResult` interfaces, `RiskLevel` and `ThreatAction` types.

**Audit & alerts model (`src/lib/admin/audit.ts`):**

- `AuditEntry`, `AuditApiResponse` interfaces — shapes for GET /admin/audit. `severityBadgeVariant(level)` — maps danger/warning/info to M1 Badge variant names. `alertSeverity(consecutive, suppressed)` — derives alert severity Badge variant from alert state. `auditActionChip(action)` — normalizes audit action string for code-chip display. `formatAuditTime(iso)` — RFC3339 to 24h HH:MM:SS. `formatAuditTarget(type, id)` — formats target as "type #id".

**Admin components (`src/lib/components/admin/`):**

- `DepartmentTreePicker.svelte` — Reusable OU-scope picker for RBAC grant dialogs and any future netdisk/mailbox-sharing UI. Used in both the single-assign dialog and the bulk-assign dialog (`RoleMembersPanel`). Contract: `<DepartmentTreePicker bind:value />` where `value = {scopeType:'ou', scopeId:number} | null` (null = whole domain). Two-tier radio (default "Whole domain" first). WAI-ARIA tree widget: `role=tree`/`treeitem`, roving tabindex, ↑↓→←/Enter/Space/Home/End keyboard nav, `aria-selected`/`aria-expanded`. Subtree affordance: selected node shows `+ all N sub-departments` badge (count from tree data) + `--accent-subtle` descendant tint + confirmation chip + Tooltip (focusable-button). Polite live region echoes selection for SR. Five states: loading (skeleton rows) / empty (warm message + directory-console link) / error ("Couldn't load departments" + Retry; value stays null) / deep-tree (search/filter box when >50 nodes) / selected (badge + tint + chip). Reuses `adminDeptTree()` from `$lib/admin/client`. Tested in `DepartmentTreePicker.test.ts` (18 tests).
- `IPRulesDialog.svelte` — Dialog over backend-wave2's `/admin/threat/ip-rules` CRUD. Lists IP rules (CIDR, action Badge, description, expires) in M1 Table; delete with inline confirm; add-rule form (mono Input CIDR, Select action, description Input, optional datetime-local expires). Opened from the threats page "Policies" button.
- `AppAccessEditor.svelte` — Shared seven-app presentation (Mail/Calendar/Contacts/Tasks/Chat/Drive/Meet). Member mode is the B8 Dialog over `/admin/users/{id}/app-access`: editable Switches bound to `user_app_entitlements`, global-toggle badges via `effectiveAccess()`/`GLOBAL_FEATURE_MAP`, and `PUT /admin/users/{id}/app-access/{app}` saves. Guest G2 mode is embedded in the guest detail sheet, takes server-derived entitlements plus the profile name, disables all seven Switches, and never fetches or writes the member endpoint.
- `AssistDrawer.svelte` — Ops copilot drawer per admin-kit §6 (UI only; consumes backend-wave2's `POST /assist/complete`). Header (Sparkles accent icon + title + close), transcript (User/assistant message bubbles with minimal `asRich` renderer: `**bold**`→strong, `` `code` ``→inline code chip), input form (text Input + Send Button), empty state with intro line + 3 suggested-question buttons. Gated on `effectiveCaps.assist`; toggle button in admin layout (fixed bottom-right, Sparkles icon). Caller passes conversation as `{messages}` array; handles 403/422/502/503 with user-facing error copy. No IndexedDB history in M7. See `src/lib/admin/assist.ts` (client + `asRich`).
- **`SignInTrace.svelte`** (CA-1, `src/lib/components/admin/SignInTrace.svelte`): CA sign-in log tab inside the admin conditional-access page. Renders the 6-outcome chip legend + a paginated sign-in log table (`GET /admin/ca/signin-log`); rows expandable to per-policy trace with per-event chips; axis toggle (by user / by correlation); copyable correlation-ID; `aria-live` announcer for copy events. Consumes the outcome/decision maps from `signin-trace.ts` (same file). Tests: `signin-log.test.ts` (5 states: loading / empty / populated / error / expanded-row).
- **`signin-trace.ts`** (CA-1, `src/lib/components/admin/signin-trace.ts`): **the 6-outcome chip label map** — single source of truth for `OUTCOME_LEGEND`, `outcomeChip(o)`, and `decisionVariant(d)`. Each `OutcomeMeta` carries `{outcome, variant, icon, key, tip}` copied verbatim from `design-system-additions.md §1`; unknown outcomes get a neutral fallback chip (never crash, never mis-color). Consumed by `SignInTrace.svelte`, the CA-1 what-if simulator, and any future CA surface that renders outcome chips. Tested in `signin-trace.test.ts`.
- `ReasonPrompt.svelte` — Confirm dialog used before a sensitive audit-policy lowering (full→lean or removing a required-reason area). Renders a `<Dialog>` with a labeled text `<Input>` for the mandatory reason; the confirm Button is disabled until the input is non-empty. Emits `onconfirm(reason)` / `oncancel()`. Tested in `ReasonPrompt.test.ts` (confirm disabled when empty, enabled when non-empty, cancel fires, confirm fires with reason string).

**Admin offline components (`src/lib/admin/offline/`)** (offline #2a):

- `OfflinePolicyEditor.svelte` — Policy list + create/edit form (priority-ordered `offline_policies` rows). CRUD backed by `/admin/offline/policies/*`. Live-region `aria-live="polite"` save/delete announcements (class `.sr-only`). Uses M1 `Card`, `Button`, `Input`, `Select`, `Switch` primitives; act-then-undo for delete, Dialog confirm for irreversible cases.
- `PrecedenceExplainer.svelte` — Static explainer card showing the user > group > global precedence stack with a concrete example. Informational only; no data fetch.
- `DeviceApprovalQueue.svelte` — Admin device approval queue. Loads `GET /admin/offline/devices/pending`; renders per-device rows (platform, device name, user, approval state) with Approve/Deny/Revoke action buttons; polls on push `capsChanged`. Gated on `mam.read`/`mam.write`.

**Log badge helpers (`src/lib/admin/logHelpers.ts`):**

- `logLevelBadgeVariant(level)` — maps log levels (error/warn/info/debug/trace) to M1 Badge variant names. `eventBadgeVariant(event)` — maps auth events (login/login_failed) to Badge variant names. Pure functions; tested in `logHelpers.test.ts`.

**Assist helpers (`src/lib/admin/assist.ts`):**

- `asRich(text)` — minimal rich-text renderer: `**bold**`→`<strong>`, `` `code` ``→inline code chip (`<code class="sj-code-chip">`). No headings, lists, links, tables. Pure function; tested in `assist.test.ts`.
- `assistComplete(req)` — calls backend-wave2's `POST /assist/complete` via `adminFetch`. Request: `{messages: AssistMessage[], context?, locale?}`; response: `{content: string}`. Throws on 403/422/502/503 with user-facing detail.

**App-access helpers (`src/lib/admin/appAccess.ts`):**

- `GLOBAL_FEATURE_MAP` — maps app names (calendar/contacts/tasks/chat/drive/meet) to their global feature toggle keys. `effectiveAccess(global, perUser)` — derives effective app access from global toggle state and per-user entitlement; returns `{effective, limitedGlobally}`. Pure functions; tested in `appAccess.test.ts`.

**User badge helpers (`src/lib/admin/userHelpers.ts`):**

- `roleBadgeVariant(role)` — maps admin role names (super_admin/user_admin/auditor/user) to M1 Badge variant names per admin-kit §4.5. `statusBadgeVariant(status)` — maps user status (active/locked/pending) to Badge variant. `statusBadgeLabel(status)` — human-readable status label. Pure functions; tested in `userHelpers.test.ts`.

**Admin client (`src/lib/admin/`):**

- `transport.ts` is the acyclic request leaf: `adminFetch` owns the `/api` prefix, JSON/FormData header behavior, caller-header merge, `cache:'no-store'`, `/login` redirect and generic admin-403 classification. Domain modules import it directly; it imports no admin domain.
- `office.ts` owns the narrow runtime decoders for the named Office/App wire contract, including provider readiness, installed revisions, operation recovery, and reducer-owned actions. Malformed or unknown shapes fail closed before reaching a page.
- `health.ts` owns the smallest extracted stable domain slice (`HealthCheck`, `HealthResponse`, `adminHealth`) and the admin dashboard imports it directly. `client.ts` narrowly re-exports those four established names for compatibility; their definitions must not return to the monolith. `transport.test.ts` pins the 417-name client surface, moved ownership map, source deletion, direct domain imports and cycle-free admin graph.
- `client.ts` retains the remaining typed functions for admin REST endpoints (users, domains, mailboxes, depts, quarantine, DLP, retention, features, deploy, MAM, SCIM, migration config, threat flows, IP rules, audit log, alerts); this is intentionally not a broad barrel migration. **Audit-hardening additions:** `VerifyChainRow{id, valid, occurredAt, action}` + `verifyAuditChain(fromId, toId)` → `{chain: VerifyChainRow[]}` (calls `GET /admin/audit/verify`); `AuditPolicy{level, reasonRequiredAreas}` + `getAuditPolicy()` / `putAuditPolicy({level, reasonRequiredAreas, reason})` (calls `GET/PUT /admin/audit/policy`).

**Validation (`src/lib/validation.ts`):**

- `validate`, `required`, `maxLength`, `minLength`, `isEmail`, `isNumber`, `normalize` — form validation helpers used across all forms.

**Error handling (`src/lib/errors.ts`):**

- `parseError` — normalizes errors to `{message, code}`; never exposes raw Go errors to UI.

**Sanitize (`src/lib/sanitize.ts`):**

- `sanitizeHTML(html, opts?, out?)` — DOMPurify wrapper; applied to all HTML email bodies before iframe injection, to the outbound compose HTML body in `buildMsg()` before persist/send, and to Assist output before it is written into the editor. A `uponSanitizeAttribute` hook (a) strips `url()`/`expression()` declarations from the `style` attribute (CSS-exfil/tracking defense-in-depth), and (b) **drops remote http(s)/protocol-relative `<img src>`/`srcset` unless `opts.allowRemoteImages` is set** — closing the tracking-pixel + IP/User-Agent leak on open (relative `/blobs/…`, `cid:` (rewritten upstream), and inline `data:` images are kept). `hasRemoteImages(html)` is the banner heuristic. `renderEmailBodyHTML(email, {allowRemoteImages}, out?)` (WAVE-5) is the single cid-rewrite + `sanitizeHTML` render path shared by `MessageCard` and the print helper — do NOT re-inline the cid loop. The inbox coordinator blocks remote images by default and exposes a per-message "Load images" banner in `ReadingPane` (`remoteImagesBlocked`/`onLoadRemoteImages`), resetting the opt-in on message change.
- **Tracker-image classification + load-but-strip (U3 Item A):** in the SAME sanitize pass, each remote `<img>` is classified tracker-vs-regular — a curated best-effort `TRACKER_DOMAINS` list (SendGrid/Mailchimp/Customer.io/HubSpot/Klaviyo/…, extend as new ESP pixels get reported) OR a zero-area heuristic (`isZeroArea`: `width`/`height` of 0/1(px), or a `display:none`/`visibility:hidden`/≤1px inline style) — catches self-hosted pixels regardless of domain. `allowRemoteImages: true` now loads REGULAR remote images but still strips tracker-classified ones (load-but-strip); `opts.loadEverything: true` is the per-message escape-hatch that also loads trackers (rare false positive). The per-call count rides the optional third `out: {trackersBlocked}` param (kept the string-returning signature for `sanitizeHTML`/`renderEmailBodyHTML` — no churn on existing string-comparison callers) — `MessageCard` combines `renderEmailBodyHTML` + `out` into one `$derived.by` and shows "N trackers blocked" + the escape-hatch link in the remote-image banner (`htmlMode && (remoteImagesBlocked || trackersBlocked > 0)`).
- **Link-wrapper flagging (U3 Item B):** each `<a>` is classified in the same pass (`classifyAndTagLink`) — resolves the REAL destination first (`/link-protect?token=…` → base64-decode the token's JSON payload's `.u` field, client-side, best-effort/try-catch, no signature verification needed since it only informs a tooltip; unrelated to the server-side `linkprotect` click-time threat-intel verdict, which this does not duplicate), then flags when the resolved host looks like a wrapper/tracker (`url\d+\.`/`click\.`/`links\.` subdomain prefix, or `TRACKER_DOMAINS`) OR the anchor's visible text itself parses as a URL whose host differs (phish/tracker signature). A flagged link gets `title="via <host>"` (native tooltip, works inside `sandbox=""` — no post-render JS) with the `href` left UNCHANGED — no auto-unwrap.

**Logger (`src/lib/logger.ts`):**

- `logger.log` / `logger.error` — structured console logger (no-op in production).

**i18n (`src/lib/i18n/`):**

- `index.ts`: `locale` store, `t()`, `setLocale`, `loadLocale`, `META` (locale metadata). `T.svelte`: reactive i18n component. `icu.ts`: ICU MessageFormat parser. `en.ts` / `de.ts` / `zh.ts`: translation bundles.

**Keyboard hooks (`src/lib/hooks/useKeyboard.svelte.ts`):**

- `registerShortcut` + `EMAIL_SHORTCUTS` map (c/r/a/f/e/#/s/z/i/u/j/k/slash/Ctrl+K).

**DB / offline (`src/lib/db/offline.ts`):**

- IndexedDB mutation queue; `isOnline`, `onNetworkChange`.

**Offline mail store (`src/lib/offline/`)** (offline #1a Task 8):

- `work-contract.ts` — E0 compile-time contract for connectivity-aware optional work: exact `off|observe|execute` rollout order, bounded sync-step/checkpoint shapes, scheduler limits, Critical reserved-capacity constants, and the version-1 privacy-safe native capability snapshot. It is contract-only: no scheduler, bridge registration, route, feature key, or background execution is enabled. Unknown bridge versions/enums fail closed. The colocated test is part of `scripts/verify-connectivity-aware-sync.sh quick`.
- `mailPagePersistence.ts` — CQ-DP1 mail page/checkpoint composition for the optional coordinator. Native message/tag/FTS/body effects and the matching checkpoint use one existing `transactionBatch`; every write repeats a stable account/job/generation/lease/state predicate, with lease time sampled once before the batch. Dirty fields, watermarks and pending local edits are rechecked at the SQL write. The final checkpoint always contributes one matched row on admission, so aggregate changes cannot mistake a page write for a stale checkpoint. No native API, schema or mail engine is added. `steps.ts` reserves the resumable checkpoint and stages only prefixes fitting the unchanged 64-statement/1 MiB SQL-plus-JSON-parameter budget; the 64-record/2 MiB/5-second work ceiling stays unchanged. Tags use bounded 256-parameter chunks and FTS reads the current row; a single unadmittable record fails finitely with a nonretryable storage result. Window prefixes follow query order even if Email/get returns a different order. The memory/legacy adapter retains `stagedPage.ts` compensation, batch-less refusal and undo retention; real SQLite tests in `mailPagePersistence.test.ts` do not claim native shell or physical-device durability. Flags and rollout admission are unchanged.
- `store.ts` — `OfflineStore` interface; `isOfflineAvailable()` (true iff `window.setjoinNative?.db` exists); `getStore()` (returns the native store singleton, or null on plain browser); `StoredMessage`/`SyncIssue` types; `MAIL_APP_KEY='mail'`. The `OfflineStore` interface exposes `init`/`upsertMessage`/`getMessage`/`listByReceivedDesc`/`deleteOutOfWindow`/`deleteMessage`/`mergeServerDelta` (F1+F4 column-level LWW merge)/`restoreFieldsIfUnchanged` (E4-I r11 atomic per-field conditional restore: compare current values to staged expectations and write the restore values only where equal, in ONE synchronous store operation; used by the staged-page rollback)/`getVersion`/`bumpLocalVersion`/`clearDirty`/`enqueueOptimisticEdit` (F5 transactional outbox)/`searchBody`/`getMeta`/`setMeta`/`recordSyncIssue`/`listSyncIssues`/`clearSyncIssue`/`wipe`.
- `store-native.ts` — `makeNativeStore(db: OfflineDbBridge): OfflineStore` — native impl over the generic bridge. `init()` opens the `'mail'` appKey store then applies the E4-I versioned TS migrations (`migrations.ts`) through authorized paths only: native `migrateStore` when advertised, else the authorized one-shot `execSchemaInit` seam with the same composed statements (idempotent; U6 dev-store replay rides the same batch); DDL never rides `transactionBatch` (frozen N66 contract) and an already-current store re-opens without consuming the one-shot seam. Schema tables: `messages`, `message_tags`, `bodies`, `mailboxes`, `sync_issues`, `meta_mail`, `action_queue`, `messages_fts` (FTS5), plus E4-I `optional_jobs`/`attachment_transfers`. `mergeServerDelta` does per-column targeted UPDATE (never whole-row overwrite) respecting per-field dirty sets (F1). `enqueueOptimisticEdit` wraps the edit + op in ONE transaction — `transactionBatch` when the shell advertises it, else the legacy BEGIN/COMMIT exec path until the native flip (F5 atomicity). Version watermark tracked in `messages.version`; dirty fields in `messages.dirty_fields` (JSON array).
- **Rule:** `OfflineStore` is the ONLY seam above `src/lib/offline/`; no code outside this directory calls `window.setjoinNative.db` directly. Feature default-OFF (`feature.offline_mail`); `isOfflineAvailable()` checks bridge presence.
- `caps.ts` — Offline capability gate (#2a). `cappedWindow(userDays, caps)` — returns `Math.min(userDays ?? caps.maxOfflineDays, caps.maxOfflineDays)` clamped to 0 when `caps.allowed=false`; the single place that applies the policy cap. Consumed by `routes/settings/+page.svelte` (offline storage card, computed once as `$derived(cappedWindow(offlineWindowDays, offlineCaps))`). Pure function; tested.
- Tests: `store.test.ts` (in-memory fakeDb mock, 5 tests covering availability, upsert/list/search, F1 column-level LWW merge, F4 stale-delta skipping, F5 transactional outbox rollback).
- `profile.ts` — **offline #3 Task A1** crypto-profile discovery. `CryptoProfile = 'standard' | 'SMx'`; `fetchCryptoProfile(): Promise<CryptoProfile>` — fetches `/.well-known/setjoin` (same-origin), reads `doc.crypto_profile`, returns `'SMx'` when the value is `'smx'` (case-folded), `'standard'` on any error or unknown value. Consumed by the native offline store key-derivation path (selects AES-256-CBC for standard, SM4 for SMx). Plain browser callers get `'standard'` (offline store is native-only). No tests needed beyond the type guard — pure mapping, no branching logic beyond the one toLowerCase comparison.
- `sync.ts` — windowed sync engine (offline #1a Task 13) + **incremental delta path (offline #3 §8.5)**. `METADATA_PROPS` (13 JMAP fields). `syncWindow({window, now?})` → `{upserted, evicted, autoMerged}|null`: owner-gated (Web Locks via `withSyncOwnership`); JMAP `Email/query{after}+Email/get(METADATA_PROPS)` → F2 write-barrier (skip ids with un-acked ops in `action_queue`); F1/F4 `mergeServerDelta` for cached rows, `upsertMessage` for absent; `deleteOutOfWindow(cutoff)` evicts stale rows; `autoMerged` counts `'merged'` returns (F1 conflict auto-resolution tally); calls `offline.notifyAutoMerged(n)` once per pass for the quiet aggregate toast. `requestSync(window?)` debounced 250ms coalesced trigger. `syncDelta(client: JmapClient, store: OfflineStore): Promise<void>` — incremental `Email/changes` apply path: calls `client.emailChanges({sinceState, maxChanges:500})`, applies created/updated/destroyed to the store; on `cannotCalculateChanges` (BZ011 / pruned sinceState) falls back to `syncWindow`. `JmapClient` interface inline (duck-typed: `emailChanges(args)`). Plain browser: offline store unavailable → `syncDelta` is effectively unreachable (native-only).
- `continuity.ts` / `recovery.ts` — U5/U6 mail-specific continuity and recovery orchestration above the generic native byte plane. The client presents compact signed witnesses, stores the server-signed receipt bytes exactly, content-addresses raw MIME/blobs, enforces per-message and aggregate bounds, computes honest coverage, and uploads only after an approved incident plus user action. Uploads use fixed 256 KiB resumable chunks and exact acknowledged offsets; cancellation aborts server and local state. No whole-message buffer crosses the native bridge, and this layer never decides recovery authority.
- `state.svelte.ts` — sync phase machine (Svelte 5 `$state`). `offline` singleton: `phase` (idle/provisioning/syncing/error), `progress`, `errorMessage`, `autoMergedCount`, `lockState`/`enrolled`/`needsUnlock`/`controlsActive`/`effectiveOffline`. Methods: `setSyncPhase`, `setSyncProgress`, `setSyncError`, `notifyAutoMerged`, `setLockState`, `setWorkOffline`, `announce`, `refreshIssueCount`. Consumed by `sync.ts` (dynamic import, keeps sync.ts runes-free), OfflineStatus, BiometricUnlock, SyncIssuesInspector.
- Tests: `sync.test.ts` (5 tests: window-cutoff metadata props, eviction, F2 write-barrier SKIP, autoMerged tally + aggregate notify, no-merge no-op); `state.test.ts`.
- `readpath.ts` — offline read-path selector (offline #1a Task 16/19). `loadList({limit, offset?})` → `ListRow[]`: routes to `store.listByReceivedDesc` when `offline.effectiveOffline`, otherwise `emailQuery`+`emailGet`. `searchList(query, limit)` → `string[]`: routes to `searchOffline` (FTS5 MATCH) when offline, otherwise `emailQuery`. One tested swap point; no scattered `if (offline)` in callers. Tests: `readpath.test.ts`.
- `biometric.ts` — vault unlock/lock/idle-relock (offline #1a Task 17c). `isBiometricEnrolled()`, `unlockMail()` → `'ok'|'failed'|'cancelled'`, `unlockWithPassphrase(passphrase)` → `'ok'|'wrong'`, `lockMail()`, `installIdleRelock(minutes, onRelock)` → disposer. All inert on plain browser (`isOfflineAvailable()` guard). Tests: `biometric.test.ts`.
- `window.ts` — single-sourced offline-window enum (#1a §7, consolidated in #1b): `OfflineWindow` (`7d|30d|90d|180d|365d|all`), `OFFLINE_WINDOWS`, `DEFAULT_OFFLINE_WINDOW` (`30d`), plus `windowDays`/`cutoffMillis`/`windowLabelKey`/`isOfflineWindow`. Bounds the native cache only (browser = online-only). The MAM-cap clamp lives in `caps.ts` `cappedWindow` (the single source); the old `effectiveWindowDays` was removed.
- `leader.ts`, `reconcile.ts`, `search.ts` — `withSyncOwnership` (Web Locks); conflict helpers (`newTempId`/`resolveId`/`recordSyncIssue`/`listSyncIssues`/`clearSyncIssue`); `searchOffline` (FTS5). Each has a colocated `*.test.ts`.
- `wipe.ts` — **offline #2b remote-wipe handler.** `installWipeHandler(deps)` and `installCapsHandler(deps)` subscribe via `subscribeControl` from `$lib/jmap/websocket`: on `'offline.wipe'` the handler calls `getNative().db.wipe('mail')` (drops the SQLCipher store), then `POST /api/offline/wipe-ack` (triggers `offline_device_wipe_ack`, sets `wipe_acked_at`), then navigates to `/security-event?reason=remote-wipe`. On `'capsChanged'` the caps handler refreshes `/api/user/offline/caps` so watermark/effective-window state updates without a reload. The root layout invokes that bootstrap only while authenticated, and `installCapsHandler` independently requires the native offline store before either its initial fetch or subscription; plain browsers and public login/setup/recovery/Guest-acceptance pages never issue the native user-caps request. The Settings and Storage pages apply the same native-only fetch guard. Tests: `wipe.test.ts` plus the Guest acceptance browser network guard.
- **Storage-UI libs (offline #1b):**
  - `sizeFit.ts` — pure size helpers: `bytesForWindow(estimate, window)`, `fitWindowToSpace(estimate, freeBytes)` (largest window that fits), `formatBytes(n)`; `SizeBucket`/`SizeEstimate` types. Tested.
  - `storeFacade.ts` — the #1b → #1a coupling seam onto the offline-core `'mail'` store: re-exports `isOfflineAvailable` (from `store.ts`) + `getCacheSizeBytes`/`getFreeSpaceBytes`/`clearOfflineCache`/`syncNow`. On-disk + free bytes come FROM THE NATIVE BRIDGE (`db.sizeBytes('mail')`/`db.freeBytes()`), never `navigator.storage.estimate()` (the deleted IndexedDB-quota path). Plain browser → safe stubs (false/0/no-op); `syncNow` dynamic-imports `sync.ts` so #1b builds standalone before #1a merges.
  - `$lib/api/offline.ts` — REST client: `getOfflineSizeEstimate()` → `GET /offline/size-estimate`; `getOfflineWindow()`/`saveOfflineWindow(w)` read-modify-write the `offlineWindow` key over `GET/PUT /settings/view` (preserves sibling view-prefs).
- **Offline UI components** (`src/lib/components/`):
  - `OfflineStatus.svelte` — topbar Work-Offline toggle + sync-phase pill (provisioning/syncing/stale/error/idle); sync-issues badge that opens SyncIssuesInspector; lock indicator; quiet auto-merge toast via `showToast`. Mounted in `routes/inbox/+page.svelte` topbar. Tests: `OfflineStatus.test.ts`.
  - `BiometricUnlock.svelte` — locked-inbox gate (replaces mail pane body when `offline.needsUnlock`). Handles enrollment card (first-time), biometric/passphrase unlock arc, idle-relock reassurance copy, `failed`/`cancelled` states. Tests: `BiometricUnlock.test.ts`.
  - `SyncIssuesInspector.svelte` — Dialog-hosted genuine-conflict list (auto-merged flag/keyword/mailbox changes from F1 never appear here; only whole-body/draft conflicts). Per-issue retry/discard; discard behind a confirm Dialog. Empty state explains auto-merge. Tests: `SyncIssuesInspector.test.ts`.
  - **`WatermarkOverlay.svelte` (offline #2b)** — tiled text watermark overlay (user email × date). Mounted as an absolute overlay inside `ReadingPane`; gated on `isNative() && caps.offline.watermark`. `aria-hidden=true`, `pointer-events:none`, `user-select:none` — purely decorative, invisible to AT and screen-capture tools (DLP deterrent only; not a security control). Tests: `WatermarkOverlay.test.ts`.
- **Lock arc + idle-relock wiring** (`routes/inbox/+page.svelte`, offline #1a Task 19): `$effect` installs `installIdleRelock(15, ...)` when `offline.controlsActive`; onRelock calls `lockMail()` + `offline.setLockState('locked')` + `offline.announce('offline.live.relocked')`; disposer returned from `$effect` tears it down on destroy. `BiometricUnlock` is the gate — the full mail body (MailSidebar/MessageList/ReadingPane) renders only when `!offline.needsUnlock`. Gate is purely presentational; the JMAP client is unaffected.
- **Read-path wiring** (`src/lib/stores/inboxStore.svelte.ts`, offline #1a Task 19): `InboxStore.loadEmails()` routes through `loadList`/`searchList` (from `$lib/offline/readpath`) when `offline.effectiveOffline` and the view is inbox with no tag filter; maps `ListRow[]` to minimal `Email[]` for the list (full body fetched on open). The scheduled/Snoozed views remain online-only in #1a. The online standard path is unchanged.
- **e2e** (`e2e/offline-mail.spec.ts`): two always-run browser assertions (no `work-offline-toggle`, no unlock gate on plain browser); full native-shell flow skipped unless `SETJOIN_NATIVE_E2E` env is set (requires the native-shell harness — follow-up for #2a). Both plain-browser assertions also depend on a live gateway (`baseURL: localhost:8080`) — flagged as a follow-up in TODOS.md.

Returns `{send, prompt, consent?}`: `never`/non-receipt → `{send:false, prompt:false}`; `always` → `{send:true, prompt:false, consent:true}`; `ask` → `{send:false, prompt:true}`. `ReceiptPolicy` type (`'ask'|'always'|'never'`). The webmail's only client-side receipt logic; the reducer is the authoritative gate. Tested in `readReceipt.test.ts`.
- `searchSnippet.ts`: `sanitizeSnippet(html)` — strict XSS gate for `SearchSnippet/get` BM25 highlights. HTML-escapes everything (`&<>"`) then re-allows ONLY the two literal `<mark>`/`</mark>` tags by turning their escaped forms back into real tags — an injected `<script>` or `<mark onclick=…>` stays inert. `MailRow` `{@html}`s the sanitized `searchSubject`/`searchPreview` (falling back to the plain preview when absent; encrypted rows keep the Lock placeholder). Tested in `searchSnippet.test.ts`.
- `printMessage.ts` (WAVE-5): `printMessage(email)` — builds a self-contained doc (header: subject / from / date; body = the `renderEmailBodyHTML` sanitized HTML else escaped plain text; minimal print CSS) and hands it to `openPrintWindow` (`$lib/util/printWindow`). Remote images are NOT loaded (defaults to blocked); header fields + text fallback are `escapeHtml`-escaped (shared primitive); the sanitized body is script-free. Wired to the `ReadingPane` Print toolbar button.

- `src/lib/util/printWindow.ts`: `escapeHtml(s)` — the shared 4-replace XSS-escaping primitive; `openPrintWindow(doc)` — opens a bare `window.open('','_blank')` (synchronous, popup-blocker-safe), `document.write`s `doc`, and opener-drives `win.print()` on load (avoids an inline `<script>` the CSP would block). Shared by `printMessage.ts` (mail) + `printAgenda.ts` (calendar); each caller builds its own HTML head/title/styles. Tested in `printWindow.test.ts`.

- `src/lib/utils/directoryAddresses.ts` (ARCH-004): `normalizeAddresses(addresses, primaryFallback?)` — pure shaping of a directory/GAL person's `addresses` set into `{primary, secondary, hasMultiple}` (dedup case-insensitive, primary first, bare-`email` fallback for legacy payloads); `addressKindKey(type)` — maps `alias`/`upn` to its i18n label key. Consumed by `DirectoryMemberList` + the ContactsApp GAL card/detail. Tested in `directoryAddresses.test.ts`.

A2 `flagDueAt`/`flagReminderAt`), `Mailbox`, `Calendar`, `CalendarEvent` (incl. `valarm?: EventAlarm[]` reminder alerts), `EventAlarm` (`{trigger, action?}`), `AddressBook`, `ContactCard` (with ARCH-004 `addresses?: DirectoryAddress[]` for GAL/directory cards), `DirectoryAddress` (`{email, type, isPrimary}`), `Task`, `Identity`, `JmapSession`, `FreeSlot`, `QuickStep`/`QuickStepAction` (A2), etc. `JmapMethodName` includes `QuickStep/{get,set,apply}`.

## 5. Work Guidance

- **Design system first**: use design token CSS variables exclusively. Never hard-code colors, shadows, or spacing. Do not introduce Liquid Glass (`backdrop-filter`, `blur`, aurora) patterns.
- **No business logic in the SPA**: mutations call JMAP methods or REST endpoints. All decisions (delivery, rules, quota, state) are in reducers.
- **Error states are required**: every async operation needs a loading state (disable buttons, show spinner), error state (user-facing message — fact + next step, no jargon, no raw Go errors), and success feedback.
- **Loading states**: every async op disables buttons, prevents duplicate submissions, and shows a spinner or skeleton.
- **Form validation**: all fields need HTML5 attributes (`maxlength`, `required`, `type`) + JS validation via `$lib/validation.ts`. Limits documented with source (RFC, DB column, config).
- **Voice**: sentence case; verb-first buttons; act-then-undo toasts (not confirm dialogs) for reversible actions; dialogs only for irreversible admin actions with concrete consequence copy; no emoji in chrome; 24h clock.
- **Admin RBAC**: never rely on hiding a UI element as the security boundary. The gateway enforces permissions; the SPA merely avoids showing unauthorized options.
- **Keep `package-lock.json` consistent** with dependency changes; include it in a commit only when the user requests committing.

### Architecture review (2026-06-19) — decisions & deferred candidates

A `/improve-codebase-architecture` pass over `src/` produced two action plans and three deferral ADRs. Recorded here so future work + reviews don't re-derive them:

- **Push transport → multi-listener hub. DONE.** `lib/jmap/websocket.ts` is now the always-on / self-arming `subscribe(types, cb)→unsubscribe` hub (Phase 0, committed). Phases 1-4 (D2) then wired the **Task** (full chain — `notify_task_state` push-only seam + `JMAPStateTokens.Task` + `classifyBatch` task branch + `TasksApp` consumer) and **Contact** (consumer-only — producer `notify_contact_state` was already live) reactivity end-to-end. Replaced the historic single-callback singleton (`if (ws) return` dropped the 2nd `connect()` callback); the inbox↔calendar drop it fixed was **latent**, not a live two-pane bug (the split-view dock is unwired — see CONTEXT.md → _Latent vs live defect_).
- **Quarantine preview hardening — DONE.** Three independent layers on the quarantine HTML preview — **markup** (client `sanitizeHTML` on both `quarantine/+page.svelte:82` and now `admin/(mail)/quarantine/+page.svelte`, closing the admin gap), **scripts** (`sandbox=""` srcdoc iframes, pinned by `ReadingPane.test.ts` + the new `admin/(mail)/quarantine/quarantine.test.ts` — exact `sandbox===''` + no `allow-*`), **resources** (a beacon-killing CSP `<meta>` `default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'` prepended **after** sanitize to **both** quarantine srcdocs — blocks tracking pixels incl. `data:`; ReadingPane uses a lighter gate — `sanitizeHTML` strips remote `<img>` by default with an opt-in "Load images" banner, so legit newsletters render on demand without leaking on open). The false-safety Go blocklist (`admin_quarantine.go`'s `sanitizeHTML`) is **deleted**: quarantine stores raw bytes (forensic fidelity), the client sanitizes at render. The admin page banks an inert escaped-text **"View source"** toggle (raw `data.html` in a `<pre>` via Svelte text-escaping, never `srcdoc`/`{@html}`) as the no-regression byte path — landed in the same change as the deletion. Full `.eml` download is a recorded forensic upgrade (out of scope — needs S3 client in `QuarantineHandler`). See CONTEXT.md → _Layered containment for untrusted HTML_.
- **Deferred (ADRs, with triggers):** client JMAP **sync-ledger** → [ARCH-008](../../docs/adr/ARCH-008-defer-client-jmap-sync-ledger.md) (full-reload-on-token is the sound baseline; revisit only after the hub + Task/Contact consumers create real debounce duplication). PIM **collection-store factory** → [ARCH-009](../../docs/adr/ARCH-009-no-pim-collection-store-factory.md) (measured ~12 identical lines, not ~70% — per-app save/form is intrinsic divergence). **App registry** → [ARCH-010](../../docs/adr/ARCH-010-defer-app-registry.md) (`navConfig.ts` already owns the nav seam; the dock dimension is unwired; `AppId` frozen at 4). ARCH-010 also banks one ready cleanup: delete the dead `labelEn`/`labelZh` from `navConfig.ts`.
