## 6. Verification

```bash
# Verification catalog: select affected code lanes, preserving required CI/evidence.
# Pure documentation moves use content/link/budget checks, not this whole block.
cd mailserver/webmail
npm run check    # 0 errors, 0 warnings
npm run lint     # ESLint flat config for TypeScript/Svelte; 0 warnings
npm run lint:css # Stylelint standard CSS + Svelte styles; 0 errors/warnings
npm run build    # clean build in build/
npm run test:coverage # Vitest unit tests + measured no-regression thresholds
```

ESLint uses `eslint.config.mjs` with TypeScript and Svelte's recommended AST
rules. Intentional `{@html}` must be fed by the existing sanitizer/escaping
boundary and carry a narrow explanation; do not globally disable the rule.

Stylelint uses `stylelint.config.mjs`, extends the standard CSS and Svelte HTML
configs, recognizes Svelte's `:global()` pseudo-class, rejects unscoped or stale
disable comments, uses content-based caching, and accepts zero warnings. Use
`npm run lint:css:fix` only for the tool's mechanical safe fixes, then inspect
the diff and run the affected CSS/UI checks plus any required integration gates.

Webmail loading performance has one root entry point:

```bash
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh --doctor
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh          # deterministic quick gate
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh diagnostic
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh ci       # 5 samples + 1 warmup
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh release  # 20 samples + 2 warmups
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh hosts    # read-only host eligibility
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh release --dry-run  # resolved plan, writes nothing
bash scripts/verify-webmail-performance.sh rehearsal --prior-artifact <retained prior bundle>
```

The deterministic gate owns the build-bound Brotli budgets, compressed
sidecars, system-first CJK/physical shards, active-locale closure, worker cache
boundary/generations, and production Go responder. Browser lanes run through
the real `mailserverd` gateway and fixed mailbox corpus, retain exact cold/warm
product marks plus CacheStorage/network/screenshots, and fail on missing,
duplicate, malformed, skipped, failed, or flaky evidence. Each attempt gets a
unique database and immutable run directory; only a complete timing pass advances
`test-results/webmail-performance/latest.json`. The `rehearsal` lane is a
SEPARATE command from the timing run: it drives one browser session from the
actual retained prior signed artifact (`asset-ledger.v1.json` + `web/` bundle),
flips the live gateway to the candidate mid-session, proves the prior
acknowledged generation stays recoverable, and writes only its own
`rehearsal-receipt.v1.json` (`kind: rehearsal`) — never `latest.json`, never a
timing receipt, and never passable as the 20-sample run (or vice versa: the
timing modes reject `--prior-artifact`). Full recipes and the boundary
between local timing evidence and deployment rehearsal are in
`docs/guides/WEBMAIL_PERFORMANCE_VERIFICATION.md`.

The coverage command also writes `test-results/vitest.json`. The repository
evidence gate matches exact registry suite/test ids into
`test-results/test-evidence.json`; missing, ambiguous, skipped, or failed named
cases fail. `scripts/report-changed-coverage.mjs` writes the enforced
changed-line result to `test-results/changed-coverage.json`; the repository gate
enforces 90% for normal paths and 95% for configured critical paths. The
2026-07-23 measured Webmail baseline is 83.20% statements, 70.00% branches,
82.67% functions, and 84.80% lines; `vite.config.js` explicitly includes every
`src/**/*.{js,ts,svelte}` production file and enforces 80/70/80/82 floors
respectively. Lowering a floor or narrowing that denominator requires explicit review under
`docs/standards/AI_ASSISTED_TESTING.md`.
The explicit `scripts/verify-all.sh browser` release lane writes separate
Playwright JSON reports for the live suite and static CSP suite, rejects missing,
skipped, failed, ambiguous, or flaky required cases, records the mutable suite's
explicit `E2E_SERVER` origin separately from the CSP suite's isolated static-build
loopback origin in `test-results/playwright-evidence.json`, and retains the Guest
terminal-lifecycle screenshots under `test-results/guest-e2e-evidence/`. The same
live suite also release-gates the deterministic Chinese mobile Guest activation
baseline in `e2e/guest-invite-accept-ui.spec.ts`; its screenshot and interaction
assertions catch wrapping, overflow, disabled-state, policy-copy, and click-path
regressions before an intentional baseline update can be accepted.

Token smoke test: `src/lib/theme/tokens.test.ts` — asserts `[data-theme="dark"]` block and `--focus-ring` exist, **and guards M8 cleanup**: `compat.css` absent, legacy Inter/JetBrains-Mono font files absent, no `backdrop-filter`/`blur()` in theme CSS. It also now **resolves tokens through their `var()` chains and asserts WCAG AA contrast**: `--text-caption`/`--text-secondary`/`--link-visited` ≥4.5:1 on page+card (light + dark), and `--text-tertiary` _below_ AA (documenting why caption exists); plus a **per-theme tag-palette completeness** check — every `--tag-{hue}-{bg,fg,dot}` resolves in BOTH themes (regression guard for the dark-mode missing-dot bug).

Spacing-token contract: `src/lib/components/ui/spacing-lint.test.ts` — a **ratchet** over the 27 primitives' `<style>` blocks. Each primitive has a frozen raw-px-spacing baseline; new raw-px `gap`/`padding`/`margin`/`height`/`width` above baseline fails the build (1px hairlines + `var(--token)` refs allowed). Baselines may only be lowered as primitives migrate to `--space-*`/layout tokens. TODO (recorded in DESIGN.md §7): widen the scan to all `src/**/*.svelte` once primitive baselines reach zero.

Permanent regression guards (run after any UI change — all must return **zero**):

```bash
# Zero emoji-as-icons (i18n bundles excluded — CJK/native strings legitimately contain them):
grep -rPn '[\x{1F300}-\x{1F9FF}\x{2600}-\x{27BF}]' src/ --include="*.svelte" --include="*.ts" | grep -v 'i18n/'
# Zero legacy glass classes in markup:
grep -rn 'glass-pane\|glass-toolbar\|glass-popover\|glass-modal\|glass-dock' src/ --include="*.svelte"
# compat.css must not exist:
test ! -f src/lib/theme/compat.css
# No legacy-token hex fallbacks: `var(--something, #hex)` / `var(--x, rgba(...))` means the
# named token no longer exists and the surface is silently rendering off-design (the M9 trap —
# bridge-dependent surfaces fell back to these when compat.css was deleted). Must be zero:
grep -rnE 'var\(--[a-z0-9-]+,\s*(#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}|rgba?\()' src/ --include="*.svelte"
```

Token-fallback rule: never write `var(--token, <literal>)`. Either the token exists (use it bare) or it does not (the literal is a bug — pick the right setjoin token). Defaults belong in `theme/`, not at call sites.

For new or changed UI primitives: add Vitest tests covering variants, a11y attributes, and keyboard behavior in the matching `.test.ts` file alongside the component.

Visual parity: check at 360/768/1024/1440px widths, light + dark theme, EN + 中文, before considering a surface done.

---

## 7. Child DOX Index

- `DESIGN.md` — the **design contract** for this area: ratifies the M0–M9 decisions (flat-over-glass, indigo-only accent `#4A50D0`, the 4px grid, IBM Plex Sans plus system-first CJK and physically sharded self-hosted Noto fallback, the dot+colour+icon status convention, the AA `--text-caption` rule, the documented inline-style exceptions, and the token-only contract extended to spacing). The "why" behind `src/lib/theme/`.
- `src/lib/components/ui/AGENTS.md` — the 28-component setjoin UI primitive library (M1): Button, IconButton, Input, Select, Checkbox, Switch, Radio, Textarea, Tag, Badge, Avatar, Card, Tabs, SidebarItem, Menu, Tooltip, Resizer, SplitPane, Dialog, ConfirmDestructive, Toast/toast service, Spinner, Progress, EmptyState, Pagination, DateTimePicker, Table, Slider, plus helper modules (avatarHash, tag colors, table sort, pagination math, splitMath, datetime utils, toasts service). Token-only styling, scoped styles, Vitest tests.
- `src/lib/components/mail/` — M3 mail-kit presentational components: `MailRow` (3-line row + hover action cluster), `MailSidebar` (7 fixed views + tag tree), `MessageList` (threaded/flat list shell), `ReadingPane` (toolbar + body + inline reply), `MessageCard` (one collapsible conversation card; owns body render + attachment preview dialog), `SenderCard` (sender people-pane popover). Built on M1 primitives; emit intent via callbacks; receive all data via props.
